### [1] [Effect-Driven Skill Abstractions for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33229)
*Burcu Kilic,David Drexel,Emre Ugur,Justus Piater*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于下一状态预测的动作块量化方法，学习离散技能抽象以支持长时程目标条件强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过聚类或重构量化动作的技能与当前状态可提供的选项或目标需求不直接相关，因此主张目标导向技能应由其对状态的影响来驱动。

Method: 引入一种新的动作块量化方法，优化以当前状态和动作为条件的下一状态预测，使技能按未来可达状态区分；将离线RL方法的连续actor头替换为在学得技能中选择的离散头，并通过训练好的低层控制器将技能转换回连续动作执行。

Result: 在基准导航和操作任务上，相较连续actor基线和基于重构的离散化方法取得大幅且一致的改进。

Conclusion: 以状态影响为目标的技能量化能形成覆盖多样可达结果的技能词汇表，并可即插即用地增强常见离线RL方法。

Abstract: We seek to learn discrete skill abstractions to facilitate long-horizon, goal-conditioned reinforcement-learning (RL) tasks. Recent approaches have shown success by quantizing actions via clustering or reconstruction. However, these objectives lead to skills that are not directly related to what the current state affords or what the goal requires. Instead, we argue that goal-directed skills should be driven by \emph{how they affect the state} of the agent. To realize this, we introduce a novel quantization method for action chunks that optimizes next-state predictions conditioned on the current state and action. This objective encourages skills to be distinguished by the future states they lead to, resulting in a vocabulary that spans diverse outcomes reachable from a given state. Our framework can easily be paired with common offline RL methods by replacing their continuous actor head with a discrete one that selects among the learned skills. We can execute these skills in the environment by converting them back to continuous actions using a trained low-level controller. Experiments on benchmark navigation and manipulation tasks largely show substantial and consistent improvements over both the continuous-actor baselines and reconstruction-based discretizations. Our project page can be found at: this https URL .

</details>


### [2] [Hetero-Associative Visuomotor Memory: Soft Recall as a Base for Learned Residuals in Few-Demonstration Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34014)
*Runcong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现平滑的softmax加权记忆召回比最近邻块更适合作为纠错基础，并提出异联想视觉运动记忆HAVM（14M参数），在少样本演示下实现快速适应和高效控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索-纠错策略通常只优化最近邻演示块，但作者想探究演示记忆应为纠错器提供什么，以提升少样本下的适应性和执行成功率。

Method: 提出HAVM（异联想视觉运动记忆），使用全记忆上的平滑softmax加权召回（含可学习检索温度β），配合动作感知键和留一演示训练，离线训练约5分钟，推理1.1ms。

Result: 在ALOHA方块转移上，软召回纠错比最近邻提升22.4点（新编码器）和7.4点（闭环调优top-1）；在robomimic Square上领先7.9和6.7点。HAVM在六个新ALOHA编码器上24秒内达76.4%，接近264M Diffusion Policy（78.8%），优于ACT（71.7%）和微调VLA（65.2-75.2%），并在8秒限制内领先所有40演示基线（74.6%）。LIBERO上40任务记忆达92.2%，对换词鲁棒；真实SO-101机械臂维持30Hz控制。

Conclusion: HAVM以牺牲广泛分布外泛化为代价，换取对现有演示的快速适应，在少样本、稀疏演示场景下高效且性能优越。

Abstract: Retrieve-and-correct policies typically refine the nearest retrieved demonstration chunk, the readout that works best when executed directly. We study what a demonstration memory should supply to the corrector instead and find that a smooth, softmax-weighted recall over the whole memory is a much better base. On ALOHA cube transfer (40 human demonstrations), soft recall is no better to execute alone than the nearest chunk; however, with keys, candidates, and training fixed, correcting it raises success by 22.4 points on fresh resets with new encoders and by 7.4 against a closed-loop-tuned top-1 corrector. On robomimic Square, it leads by 7.9 points with the two better operators and by 6.7 on a label-free draw of equal size. This gain holds on sparse, varied demonstrations under an action-aware key and leave-one-demonstration-out training, and fades or slightly reverses on dense, scripted, or generated data. Building on this finding, our hetero-associative visuomotor memory (HAVM, 14M parameters) trains offline in about five minutes, including a learned retrieval temperature $\beta$, and runs in 1.1 ms. Across six fresh ALOHA encoders, it reaches 76.4% within 24 s, comparable to a 264M Diffusion Policy trained for 6.7 h (78.8%) and ahead of ACT (71.7%) and fine-tuned VLAs (65.2-75.2%), while leading every 40-demonstration baseline within the benchmark's 8 s limit (74.6%; +6.5 over Diffusion Policy, +15.1 over ACT). On LIBERO, one 40-task memory reaches 92.2% and loses far less than SmolVLA to rewording; on a real SO-101 arm, it sustains 30 Hz control without stalling. Unlike pretrained foundation policies aimed at zero-shot generalization to unseen tasks and workspaces, HAVM trades broad out-of-distribution generalization for rapid adaptation to the available demonstrations.

</details>


### [3] [Aegis-VLA: Reliability-Gated Action Refinement via a Trajectory World Model for Dynamic Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33993)
*Lennox Wei,Jiaqi Yao,Shixin Sun,Yuanzheng Zhang,Jianhua Han,Meng Tian,Shaohui Liu,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Aegis-VLA是一个世界模型引导的视觉-语言-动作框架，通过预测动作修正并独立判断是否应用修正，在动态物体操作基准上取得了56.72%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态物体操作需要在推理和执行过程中与持续运动的目标保持时空对齐；虽然未来预测可以指导动作修正，但准确的预测并不能确定修改现有动作是否有益。

Method: 提出Aegis-VLA框架：轨迹世界模型结合运动学外推与学习到的残差动力学来预测物体运动并估计位置不确定性；差异引导动作细化利用仅历史与基于基础动作条件的末端执行器预测之间的差异提出有界手臂修正；可靠性门控基于示范相对改进学习何时采纳修正，并在推理时利用预测不确定性和预期物体-末端执行器接近度调节其应用；被拒绝的修正保留对应的基础动作；仅引入约2.45M可训练参数。

Result: 在模拟动态物体操作（DOM）基准上，Aegis-VLA在1800次评估试验中达到56.72%的成功率，比复现的DynamicVLA基线45.44%高出11.28个百分点。

Conclusion: Aegis-VLA通过世界模型引导的预测修正与可靠性门控，在仅增加少量可训练参数的情况下显著提升了动态物体操作的成功率。

Abstract: Dynamic object manipulation requires maintaining spatial and temporal alignment with targets that continue to move during inference and execution. Although future prediction can inform action correction, an accurate forecast does not establish whether modifying an existing action would be beneficial. We present Aegis-VLA, a world-model-guided vision-language-action (VLA) framework that generates predictive action corrections and separately determines whether and how strongly to apply them. A Trajectory World Model combines kinematic extrapolation with learned residual dynamics to predict object motion and estimate positional uncertainty. Discrepancy-Guided Action Refinement uses differences between history-only and base-action-conditioned end-effector forecasts to propose bounded arm corrections. A Reliability Gate learns when to admit corrections based on demonstration-relative improvements over base actions, and regulates their application during inference using predictive uncertainty and anticipated object--end-effector proximity. Rejected corrections preserve the corresponding base actions. Aegis-VLA introduces only approximately 2.45M trainable parameters. On the simulated Dynamic Object Manipulation (DOM) benchmark, Aegis-VLA achieves 56.72\% success over 1,800 evaluation trials, exceeding our reproduced DynamicVLA baseline of 45.44\% by 11.28 percentage points.

</details>


### [4] [QK-Bench: Benchmarking Quantitative Kinematic Understanding for Dynamic Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33991)
*Lennox Wei,Jiaqi Yao,Shixin Sun,Yuanzheng Zhang,Jianhua Han,Meng Tian,Shaohui Liu,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出QK-Bench基准，系统评估多模态大模型在定量运动学理解上的能力，发现现有模型（包括空间智能模型）存在严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在空间智能中能识别物体接近等语义信息，但无法准确判断接近速度、是否相撞等定量运动学属性，这在碰撞避免、轨迹跟踪等任务中至关重要。

Method: 构建QK-Bench，包含379个视频的2,341道多选题，覆盖物体中心运动学、相机自运动运动学、多物体运动学关系三类17项任务；利用隐藏几何与时间信号构建轨迹，用固定运动学规则生成可验证答案，并统一输入单目RGB。

Result: 最强模型总体准确率仅41.2%，专有、开源及空间MLLM均表现有限，空间MLLM无一致优势。

Conclusion: 当前空间智能进展不能可靠迁移到定量运动学理解，需针对性提升模型对运动量级、速率、参考系及未来空间演化的定量推理能力。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence concerns both scene structure and its temporal evolution. Yet a semantically correct description can conceal a quantitative error: recognizing that objects are approaching does not determine their closing speed or whether they will meet. Such errors are critical in collision avoidance, trajectory tracking, and reactive grasping, where actions depend on motion magnitude, timing, and relative geometry. We study quantitative kinematic understanding in multimodal large language models (MLLMs): resolving motion magnitude, rate, reference-frame dependence, and future spatial evolution from visual evidence. We introduce the Quantitative Kinematics Benchmark (QK-Bench), comprising 2,341 multiple-choice QA pairs from 379 videos and 17 tasks spanning Object-Centric Kinematics, Camera Ego-Motion Kinematics, and Multi-Object Kinematic Relations. Hidden geometric and temporal signals support trajectory construction and filtering, while fixed kinematic rules map physical quantities to verifiable answers, including categorical labels. Trajectory and question validation ensure target reliability and sufficient visual support in the sampled frames. All models receive a unified monocular RGB input. Evaluations of proprietary, open-source, and spatial MLLMs reveal substantial limitations: the strongest model achieves only 41.2\% overall accuracy, and spatial MLLMs show no consistent advantage. These findings indicate that current advances in spatial intelligence do not reliably transfer to quantitative kinematic understanding.

</details>


### [5] [Targeted Adaptation of World-Action Models via Interaction and Motion Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33963)
*Yuze Zhu,Jacky Li,Yusen Luo,Ken Jiankun Zheng,Chancharik Mitra,Trevor Darrell,Roei Herzig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HeadLight是一种参数高效微调方法，通过联合适配功能专门化的Next-Interaction Heads和Motion Heads，在仅更新1.1%参数的情况下提升了机器人操作策略的泛化成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World-Action Models需要同时识别交互位置并建模运动，但全量微调和LoRA等标准适配方法并未显式选择对齐这些操作特化能力的参数。

Method: 发现两类功能专门化的注意力头——定位抓取释放目标的Next-Interaction Heads和跨帧追踪运动的Motion Heads，并提出HeadLight方法联合适配这两类头。

Result: 在微调未见过的RoboTwin任务上，HeadLight成功率达62.28%，高于全量微调的57.89%和LoRA的58.66%，仅更新1.1%参数；消融Motion Heads使成功率从57.89%降至0.94%，消融Next-Interaction Heads降至40.61%，均显著大于随机消融同等数量头的影响。

Conclusion: 按操作功能专门化选择参数进行适配能显著提升WAMs的微调效果与泛化性，且该方法在多种WAM架构和真实机器人操作场景中均有效。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) are a promising new paradigm of robot manipulation policies that jointly generate future video and actions. Successful manipulation requires both identifying where to interact and modeling how the robot and objects move. Yet standard adaptation methods, such as full fine-tuning and LoRA, do not explicitly select parameters aligned with these manipulation-specific capabilities. Motivated by this challenge, we discover two functionally specialized families of attention heads: Next-Interaction Heads, which localize upcoming grasp-and-release targets, and Motion Heads, which track movement across frames. Furthermore, we introduce HeadLight, a parameter-efficient refinement method that jointly adapts Next-Interaction Heads and Motion Heads. On RoboTwin tasks unseen during fine-tuning, HeadLight achieves 62.28% success, compared with 57.89% for full fine-tuning and 58.66% for LoRA, while updating only 1.1% of parameters. Inference-time ablations further validate the functional importance of these heads: ablating Motion Heads reduces manipulation success from 57.89% to 0.94%, while ablating Next-Interaction Heads lowers it to 40.61%, both substantially more than ablating equally many random heads. Finally, we show that our approach is effective across multiple WAM architectures and real-robot manipulation settings.

</details>


### [6] [Beyond the Happy Path: Generalizing Robot World Models from Simulated Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33925)
*Zhiyue Zhao,Mingyu Liu,Bolin Zhang,Liyang Li,Guangyu Huang,Canyu Zhao,Jinchuan Shi,Haokai Zhang,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WorldReforge方法，通过构建包含扰动抓取失败轨迹的Reforge数据集并施加交互对齐与表面对应两种训练信号，显著降低世界模型在操作失败场景下的物体运动预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人世界模型训练数据以成功演示为主，在抓取错位、滑落等失败场景下预测严重退化，现有预训练动作条件模型的物体运动误差甚至不如复制首帧的静态基线。

Method: 构建62k条模拟轨迹的Reforge数据集，通过在位置、朝向和闭合时序上扰动参考抓取，记录推、滑、抓取失败及真值物体位姿、接触和表面点；据此引入无需额外推理成本的两个训练信号：交互对齐（将重建聚焦于物体与接触小区域）和表面对应（训练骨干特征跨帧跟踪同一物理表面点）。

Result: 在Cosmos3 Nano上，物体轨迹误差整体降低28.2%，未见物体上降低35.0%，两动作结果差异预测误差降低43.3%；仅在仿真训练，但在DROID和真实失败视频上重建亦有改善；对照数据研究表明失败接触与成功持握教授互补物理规律。

Conclusion: 利用扰动抓取失败数据并配合区域聚焦与表面点对应监督，可有效教会世界模型操作出错时的物理响应，且泛化到真实场景。

Abstract: Robot world models are increasingly used to evaluate and plan policies, yet robot training data are dominated by successful demonstrations. Off the happy path, when a grasp is misaligned or mistimed and the object is pushed, tilted, or slips, their predictions break down. Across five pretrained action-conditioned models, object-motion error is at most 10.4% lower than that of a static baseline that copies the first frame, and two models are worse than this baseline. We present WorldReforge, which teaches world models what happens when manipulation goes wrong. We construct the Reforge dataset of 62k simulated trajectories by perturbing reference grasps in position, orientation, and closure timing, recording each resulting push, slip, or failed pickup with ground-truth object pose, contact, and surface points. WorldReforge turns this ground truth into two training signals without additional inference cost. Interaction Alignment focuses reconstruction on the small object and contact regions that a global loss underweights, and Surface Correspondence trains backbone features to follow the same physical surface points across frames. On Cosmos3 Nano, WorldReforge reduces object trajectory error by 28.2% overall and by 35.0% on unseen objects, and it lowers the error in predicting how two actions' outcomes diverge by 43.3%. Although trained only in simulation, it also improves reconstruction on DROID and on real failure videos. Controlled data studies show that failed contact and successful holding teach complementary physics: failed contact teaches how objects respond, and holding teaches how a grasped state persists. Our code, data, and models are available at this http URL .

</details>


### [7] [iMFPO: Improving Mean Flow Policy Optimization with Drift-Aware Velocity Alignment for Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33922)
*Lisiyu Pan,Ting Huang,Zeyu Zhang,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出iMFPO单步生成式策略优化框架，将瞬时速度学习与区间平均动作生成解耦，并通过漂移感知速度对齐实现高效一步控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的生成策略在具身控制中表达力强，但在奖励驱动的在线强化学习中，动作分布不断演化，导致瞬时速度学习与区间平均动作生成之间出现不一致，难以实现高效的一步生成。

Method: iMFPO采用共享的区间条件actor同时支持瞬时速度监督和区间平均动作生成；联合优化JVP校正的速度回归与奖励驱动策略目标，训练时引入冻结PPO教师提供动作级引导；推理时直接通过区间平均速度预测生成动作，无需迭代流积分。

Result: 在Go2、G1、H1和Spot上零潜变量评估的平均回报分别为35.69、34.44、416.23和29.86；在Go2上动作生成延迟从64步RFPO的15.57 ms降至0.41 ms。

Conclusion: iMFPO在保持生成式策略表达力的同时实现高效一步动作生成，显著降低推理延迟并在多个具身平台上取得良好回报。

Abstract: Flow-based generative policies offer expressive action modeling for embodied control, yet achieving efficient one-step generation under reward-driven policy optimization remains challenging. In particular, evolving action distributions during online reinforcement learning may introduce inconsistencies between instantaneous-velocity learning and interval-averaged action generation. To address this challenge, we propose iMFPO, a one-step generative policy optimization framework with decoupled velocity learning and drift-aware velocity alignment. A shared interval-conditioned actor supports instantaneous-velocity supervision and interval-averaged action generation. JVP-corrected velocity regression is jointly optimized with reward-driven policy objectives, with action-level guidance from a frozen PPO teacher during training. At inference, iMFPO directly generates actions through interval-averaged velocity prediction, achieving one-step control without iterative flow integration. Across Go2, G1, H1, and Spot, zero-latent evaluation yields mean returns of 35.69, 34.44, 416.23, and 29.86. On Go2, action-generation latency drops from 15.57 ms to 0.41 ms relative to 64-step RFPO. Code: this https URL . Website: this https URL .

</details>


### [8] [SEEK: Self-Evolution through Execution Knowledge for Agentic Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33915)
*Zeyang Guan,Qihao Zhao,Rui Gong,Zhang Haitian,Yalun Dai,Yangyu Huang,Lingao Xiao,Haozhe Du,Xuehui Yu,Ye Lu,Jianjun Gao,Ruoyu Wang,Haowen Bai,Hao Li,Scarlett Li,Kim-Hui Yap*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SEEK是一个智能体框架，通过在固定基础模型下保留并进化可执行程序化策略与动作效果库，使机器人跨多次尝试持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态基础模型让机器人无需任务演示即可适应，但其学习仅保留在模型工作上下文中，任务结束后执行知识丢失，导致重复尝试时耗时且重复犯错；仅回放过去动作不够，因为效果依赖当前条件，而基于当前场景决定动作的程序在遇到未覆盖条件时需借助先前动作的测量效果来修订。

Method: 提出SEEK框架，采用自进化程序化策略（SEPP）将操作编码为可执行程序并依据当前观测确定每个动作；执行过程中在持久动作-效果库（PAEB）记录动作的测量效果（含失败尝试）；当保留程序适用时无需请求模型即可执行，遇到未覆盖条件时用这些证据指导修订，底层模型保持固定。

Result: 在RoboDojo全部42个任务上，经十次尝试保留知识使SEEK平均任务得分从27.0提升至54.4，成功率从19.7%提升至48.0%；在34个任务上使用GPT-6.2 Astra，SEEK五次尝试后达到80.1，而同一模型直接行动为49.6，且模型请求减少77.6%。

Conclusion: 同时保留可执行程序与动作效果证据，可使机器人在不更新基础模型的情况下跨尝试积累执行知识并持续改进，显著提升任务表现并降低模型调用开销。

Abstract: General-purpose multimodal foundation models enable robots to adapt during deployment from observations and execution feedback, without task-specific demonstrations or training. However, the robot retains what it learns only in the model's working context. Once the task ends, it loses this execution knowledge, such as how to carry a bowl between two grippers. On the next attempt at the task, the robot may take just as long and repeat the same mistakes. To avoid this repetition, robots must retain execution knowledge across attempts. Yet replaying past actions is insufficient, because their effects depend on the current conditions. Procedures that determine actions from the current scene address this, but when they meet conditions that earlier attempts did not cover, revising them requires the measured effects of earlier actions. We therefore propose SEEK, an agentic framework that retains both, enabling robots to improve across attempts while keeping the underlying model fixed. To adapt retained knowledge to the current scene, SEEK adopts a Self-Evolving Programmatic Policy (SEPP) that encodes manipulation as executable procedures and determines each action from current observations. As the robot executes these procedures, SEEK records the measured effects of its actions, including those from failed attempts, in a Persistent Action-Effect Bank (PAEB). Where a retained procedure applies, it acts without a model request; where it meets a condition it does not cover, this evidence guides its revision. On RoboDojo, knowledge retained over ten attempts raises SEEK's average task score from 27.0 to 54.4 and its success rate from 19.7% to 48.0% across all 42 tasks. On 34 of these tasks with GPT-6.2 Astra, SEEK reaches 80.1 after five attempts, against 49.6 for the same model acting directly, and uses 77.6% fewer model requests. this https URL

</details>


### [9] [X-World: Transferring Video Priors to Cross-Embodiment Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33914)
*Chun Gu,Xiaofei Wei,Yiming Chen,Juntong Zhang,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-World 通过适配预训练视频扩散模型实现跨具身语言指令操作，在仿真和真实机器人上取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作标注的机器人轨迹成本高且覆盖有限，而人类和机器人视频提供互补的时空先验，可用于跨具身控制。

Method: 提出 X-World，将预训练视频扩散模型适配到可变视角；用 Q-Former 压缩隐藏状态为 token，输入共享的 flow-matching 专家预测动作块；数据集特定投影映射原生状态和动作；在大规模机器人和人类视频上适配骨干，冻结后进行仅动作的 Q-Former 与动作专家预训练，再在目标平台后训练；推理时以 20 Hz 响应，并通过异步实时分块重叠更新与执行。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上表现强劲，在 LIBERO-Plus 的零样本偏移下同样表现良好；在硬件上，侧装双臂 UR5e 的六个双臂任务平均成功率为 81.7%，为所展示的三種真实机器人配置之一。

Conclusion: X-World 有效利用视频先验实现跨具身闭环操作，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证了其性能。

Abstract: Language-instructed manipulation requires robust closedloop policies, yet action-labeled robot trajectories are costly and limited in coverage. Human and robot videos provide complementary spatiotemporal priors for crossembodiment control. We propose X-World, which adapts pretrained video diffusion to variable views. A Q-Former compresses hidden states into tokens for a shared flowmatching expert that predicts action chunks. Datasetspecific projections map native states and actions. We adapt the backbone on large-scale robot and human videos, freeze it for action-only pretraining of the Q-Former and action expert, then post-train on the target platform. At inference, X-World responds at 20 Hz and overlaps updates with execution via asynchronous real-time chunking. X-World performs strongly on LIBERO and under zero-shot shifts on LIBERO-Plus. On hardware, it averages 81.7% success across six bimanual tasks on the side-mounted dual-arm UR5e, one of three real-robot configurations presented.

</details>


### [10] [CoTAMP: Cooperative Task and Motion Planning for Quadruped Robots with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33911)
*Woojin Kim,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合大语言模型与代码模块的协同任务与运动规划框架，可发明未预定义的动作并生成满足动力学的轨迹，在仿真中全部试验成功并优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单四足机器人无法通过某些环境（如借助同伴背部攀爬的岩架、需用箱子搭桥的间隙），需要协同任务与运动规划；已有工作局限于单机器人运动或从预定义技能集中选择动作。

Method: 框架分两部分：任务规划（将整体轨迹分解为步骤并固定边界位姿）和运动生成（产生关键帧并通过运动学综合转为连续轨迹），二者均由基于大语言模型（LLM）的模块与代码模块组成；代码模块检查规划与运动并将失败反馈给LLM模块修正；最后用轨迹优化精化以满足动力学。

Result: 在两个RAIBO2机器人模型的三个仿真课程中，30次试验全部生成运动学有效参考且均通过轨迹优化收敛；与多个基线相比，在成功率、动态可行性、执行时间、生成时间与成本上均有优势。

Conclusion: 该LLM与代码模块结合的协同TAMP框架能自动发明并实现协同动作，在仿真中验证了有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Some environments cannot be traversed by a single quadruped robot, for example a ledge that one robot can climb only from its partner's back or a gap that must be bridged with boxes. Solving them requires cooperative task and motion planning (TAMP). Previous works are limited to the motions of a single robot or only select actions from a predefined set of skills. We propose a framework with two parts: task planning that invents motions not defined in advance, and motion generation that realizes the invented motions. Both parts consist of large language model (LLM)-based modules and code-based modules. Task planning divides the trajectory of the whole task into steps and fixes the robot poses at the step boundaries. Motion generation produces keyframes that describe the motion of each step and turns them into a continuous trajectory through kinematic synthesis. The code-based modules check every plan and motion and return failures to the LLM-based modules for correction. The whole trajectory is then refined by trajectory optimization to satisfy the dynamics. On three simulated courses with two RAIBO2 robot models, our framework produced kinematically valid references in all 30 trials, all of which converged in trajectory optimization. Compared with several baselines, our framework showed advantages in success rate, dynamic feasibility, execution time, and generation time and cost. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [11] [Integration of Vision-Language-Action Control for Autonomous Forklift Unloading with Limited Real-World Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33880)
*Koshi Oishi,Seigo Ito*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究首次将视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型集成到自主叉车卸货任务中，在仅有少量真实数据的情况下验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叉车在仓储物流中至关重要，但现有自动化系统难以处理需要顺序决策的复杂任务；VLA模型在机械臂控制中表现优异，却尚未应用于叉车，且缺少公开的叉车操作数据集。

Method: 基于主要用机械臂数据预训练的VLA模型，通过低成本仿真构建可扩展数据集，再用9.38小时仿真数据和0.8小时真实演示（约占叉车数据时长的8%）将预训练模型适配到叉车控制，并在四种模型变体上进行72次真实世界试验验证。

Result: 在72次真实世界试验中，集成系统能够在真实数据有限的情况下完成卸货任务。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将预训练的VLA模型与仿真数据结合并辅以少量真实演示，即可实现自主叉车卸货，为叉车自动化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Forklifts play a critical role in warehousing and distribution, but existing automation systems remain limited in handling complex tasks that require sequential decision-making. Recently, vision--language--action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising approach for multi-step decision-making by directly generating control actions from visual observations and language prompts. Although VLA-based control has been extensively researched for manipulators, to the best of our knowledge, its application to forklifts remains unexplored. Therefore, this study demonstrates the integration of VLA control for an autonomous forklift unloading task with limited real-world data. Moreover, training VLA models typically requires large-scale datasets. As there is currently no public dataset for forklift operations, we use a VLA model pretrained primarily on robotic manipulator data and construct a training dataset via simulation, which can be scaled up at low cost. We then adapt the pretrained VLA model to forklift control using 9.38 h of simulation data and 0.8 h of real-world demonstrations, with the latter accounting for approximately 8% of the collected forklift data by duration. The integrated system was validated through 72 real-world trials across four model variants. The results show that the proposed system can accomplish an unloading task even with limited real-world data.

</details>


### [12] [Measuring the Value of One Shared Image at Robot Handoffs in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33865)
*Yuze Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在机器人接力任务中，领航机器人交接时附加其最后动作前的视角图像，可显著提高接收机器人完成路线的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领航机器人无法继续时，应如何向必须完成路线的队友传递信息以最大化任务成功率。

Method: 在106个环境、3920次自主出发中重放冻结的接收策略，比较不同交接消息（仅动作、动作加最后动作前视角图像、单帧图像等）对成功率的提升，并以领航机器人模拟继续作为全历史上限基准。

Result: 附加最后动作前视角使每次RxR交接成功率提升7.51个百分点、SAGE交接提升8.54个百分点；每次出发分别提升2.23和1.44个百分点。单帧图像在SAGE中恢复全历史优势的24%、RxR中恢复30%。相比估计进度线索，图像在SAGE中挽救100条路线并逆转54次成功。接近目标的接收者更少结束于目标外（63.1%降至51.7%，事后分析）。较早视角比交接时视角每次交接优6-7个百分点。约7 kB每次交接即可检测到增益，随机传输在相同大小区间内几乎等同学习发送者的团队成功率。

Conclusion: 在必需交接时，提供接近最后机动动作的视角图像有助于接收机器人完成路线。

Abstract: When the lead robot cannot continue, what should it send the teammate that must finish the route? We replay a frozen receiver at each contact under alternative messages. Across 3,920 autonomous departures in 106 environments, adding the view before the lead's last action block to its executed actions raised success by 7.51 percentage points (pp) per RxR handoff and 8.54 pp per SAGE handoff. Per departure the gains were 2.23 and 1.44 pp, respectively. The lead continuing in simulation defines the empirical full-history ceiling (Solo). At contact, the lead had accumulated a median of 41 frames. Sending one frame when available recovered 24% of the full-history advantage over actions alone in SAGE and 30% in RxR. Over an estimated progress cue in SAGE, the image rescued 100 routes and reversed 54 successes. Near-goal receivers finished outside less often (63.1% to 51.7%, post hoc). In secondary comparisons the earlier view outperformed the contact view by 6-7 pp per handoff. Exact-route specificity and image gains at controlled starts with mostly unknown progress or outside the goal region remained unresolved. Gains were detectable from about 7 kB per handoff. Random transmission matched within size bins nearly equaled the learned sender's team success. At a required handoff, a view of the approach to the last maneuver helps the receiver finish.

</details>


### [13] [Learning a Whole-Body Pivot-Walking Gait for Heavy-Box Transport with a Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33828)
*Rikuto Hidaka,Tsung-Yuan Chen,Kento Kawaharazuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个强化学习策略，统一协调人形机器人的迈步、平衡控制与物体操作，实现枢轴行走搬运重物，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多依赖分离执行操作与迈步的规划序列，且物体在两次旋转间需静止，缺乏单一学习控制器。

Method: 训练单一强化学习策略，奖励鼓励交替枢轴运动，并用随物体位移推进的相位参考；部署时将策略微调为无物体感知版本，按周期时钟运行。

Result: 仿真中人形机器人可枢轴搬运箱子；真实机器人五次试验均将箱子前移超过1米，每次以基座倾斜安全停止结束。

Conclusion: 协调的人形枢轴行走可通过强化学习习得，并无需直接物体状态感知即可迁移至物理人形机器人。

Abstract: Through pivot walking, humans can transport objects that are too heavy to lift. Previous humanoid studies have relied on planned sequences in which object manipulation and stepping are executed separately and the object is brought to rest between successive rotations. However, few studies have developed a single learned controller for humanoid pivot walking. In this paper, we propose a single reinforcement-learning policy that jointly coordinates stepping, balance control, and object manipulation. The policy is trained using rewards that promote alternating pivot motions and a phase reference that progresses according to the displacement of the object. In simulation, we confirmed that the humanoid can pivot a box. For deployment, we fine-tuned the policy into an object-blind version that follows a periodic clock instead of the progress reference. On the real robot, the deployed policy transported the box forward by more than 1 m in all five trials; each trial ended at a base-tilt safety stop. These results demonstrate that coordinated humanoid pivot walking can be learned with reinforcement learning and transferred to a physical humanoid robot without direct object-state sensing.

</details>


### [14] [DexLink: Synthesizing Dexterous Behaviors from Chained Hand-object Poses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34936)
*Meenal Parakh,Deep Patel,Beining Han,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexLink通过稀疏可复用的手-物交互模板合成多样灵巧操作策略，结合重定向与残差强化学习，在仿真中实现细粒度控制并展现零样本部署潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于仿真的灵巧操作策略合成方法依赖难以扩展的人类演示或使用通用奖励函数，导致对精细手-物交互的控制有限。

Method: DexLink以关键任务路点为输入，通过保持空间手-物关系的优化将静态交互模板重定向到目标物体，再将重定向姿态链接为近似参考轨迹，并利用残差强化学习将其精炼为仿真中的闭环策略。

Result: 在单手、双手、关节物体及动态任务中，DexLink实现了对任务时序、抓取类型和高层执行的细粒度控制；模块化交互模板可跨物体尺度、实例和类别可靠迁移，无需物体特定人类演示即可匹配密集参考跟踪方法的性能；纯仿真训练的策略零样本部署展现出良好前景。

Conclusion: DexLink通过可复用交互模板与残差强化学习相结合，为灵巧操作策略合成提供了可扩展且控制精细的仿真框架，并具备向真实场景零样本迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Synthesizing diverse dexterous manipulation policies is challenging: existing simulation-based approaches rely on human demonstrations which are difficult to scale, or use generic reward functions, which provide limited control over the hand-object interactions. We propose DexLink, which addresses these limitations by synthesizing diverse dexterous behaviors from sparse, reusable hand-object interaction templates. Given key task waypoints, DexLink retargets static interaction templates to target objects using an optimization that preserves spatial hand-object relationships. It then links the retargeted poses into an approximate reference trajectory and uses residual reinforcement learning to refine this reference into a closed-loop policy in simulation. Experiments spanning single-hand, bimanual, articulated, and dynamic tasks demonstrate that DexLink provides fine-grained control over task timing, grasp types, and high-level execution. The modular interaction templates transfer reliably across object scales, instances, and categories; without object-specific human demonstrations, the framework also matches the performance of dense reference-tracking methods. Finally, zero-shot deployment of policies trained purely in simulation shows promise for the simulation-based framework.

</details>


### [15] [MERLoc: Robust Robot Localization in Dynamic Environments with Pose-Based Retrieval over 3D Gaussian Splatting References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34932)
*Michael Baumgartner,Dario Mylonopoulos,Sammy Christen,Markus Gross,Moritz Bacher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MERLoc 将单次度量的 3D 高斯泼溅扫描离线渲染为稠密的“功能参考记忆”，并用基于空间位姿而非外观的检索方式为现有定位器提供参考视图，从而在动态场景中实现更鲁棒的机器人定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已建图空间中运行的机器人需要在有人走动、物体被重新摆放的情况下仍能准确定位。现有定位流程多依赖外观检索出一小批参考图像，在场景变化和重复结构下容易失效。

Method: 把一次带度量的 3D Gaussian Splatting 扫描转换为稠密的 Functional Reference Memory：按照机器人形态与传感器、以目标密度在可导航空间上采样参考视角并离线渲染，再预编码供现成的定位器使用，使参考编码器不再参与推理。检索时依据空间邻近度与观察方向（位姿）而非外观来选取参考，从而在有先验时绕开视觉歧义。

Result: 在仿真与真实动态场景中，对一种经典定位器和四种学习型定位器进行测试，基于位姿的检索在多数情况下达到或优于外观检索，即使后者也用同一先验做门控。在真实数据上，十个后端/划分组合中有九个将位置 RMSE 降低 15–60%；渲染出的记忆优于用 Structure-from-Motion 配准的真实图像缓存。系统在 Jetson Orin NX 上以 5 Hz 运行，使导航策略能在有人走动和物体被重新摆放的走廊中成功通行。

Conclusion: 用 3D Gaussian Splatting 离线构建的稠密参考记忆配合位姿检索，比依赖外观的稀疏参考图像缓存更适合动态环境下的机器人定位，且可在嵌入式平台上实时运行并支撑导航。

Abstract: Robots operating in previously mapped spaces must localize accurately despite moving people and rearranged objects. Most localization pipelines match the query against a sparse set of captured reference images retrieved by appearance, which is brittle under scene change and repeating structures. We present MERLoc, which turns a single metric 3D Gaussian Splatting scan into a dense Functional Reference Memory. Reference views are sampled over the navigable space for the robot's morphology and sensors at a target density and rendered offline. Pre-encoding them for the chosen off-the-shelf localizer removes the reference encoder from inference. A pose-based retrieval strategy then selects references by spatial proximity and viewing direction rather than appearance, sidestepping visual ambiguities whenever a prior exists. Across one classical and four learned localizers in simulated and real dynamic scenes, pose-based retrieval matches or outperforms appearance retrieval in most cases, even when the latter is gated by the same prior. On real data, it lowers position RMSE by 15-60 % in nine of ten backend/split combinations, and the rendered memory outperforms real-image caches posed by Structure-from-Motion. The system runs onboard a Jetson Orin NX at 5 Hz, enabling a navigation policy to successfully traverse a hallway with people roaming and rearranging objects.

</details>


### [16] [One Ocean, Many Tasks: A Data-Grounded Simulation Suite and Benchmark for Marine Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34925)
*Shuaijun Liu,Qifu Wen,Xingwei Chen,Xinwen Hu,Feiyang You,Sijia Chen,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OneOcean是一个基于真实数据的海洋机器人仿真套件与基准，统一了水深、洋流、潮汐及可选污染场，提供多任务、多智能体、3-6自由度的标准化评测环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有海洋机器人仿真平台难以兼顾视觉真实感、高通量实验和基于真实数据的海洋环境真实性，缺乏统一的数据驱动基准。

Method: 构建统一的时空海洋环境表示（水深+数据驱动洋流与潮汐+可选污染场），在其上定义从导航、定点保持、路径跟踪、深度剖面跟踪、区域扫描、管道巡检到多智能体协同（编队、驱赶护鱼、污染定位/围控/清理）的任务阶梯，支持2-10个智能体、3-6自由度，输出标准化指标与可复现运行清单。

Result: 在多种数据集变体和场景中系统测试了数据驱动扰动下的规划与控制，揭示了启发式基线、行为克隆（BC）和基于大语言模型（LLM）的规划器之间的权衡。

Conclusion: OneOcean提供了数据驱动、可复现、可跨任务与难度聚合的海洋机器人仿真基准，适用于系统评估规划与控制方法并揭示不同方法间的取舍。

Abstract: Simulation is essential for marine robotics, yet existing platforms often trade off between visually realistic worlds, high-throughput experimentation, and ocean-condition realism grounded in real data. We present OneOcean, a data-grounded simulation suite and benchmark built around a unified spatiotemporal ocean-environment product that harmonizes bathymetry with data-driven currents and tides, with optional pollution fields for cleanup-related evaluation. On top of this environment representation, OneOcean defines a task ladder spanning navigation and station-keeping under currents, waypoint and route following, depth-profile tracking, area scanning, pipeline inspection, and multi-agent coordination, including formation transit, fish protection and patrol (herding), and surface pollution localization, containment, and cleanup. The suite supports multi-agent settings with 2-10 vehicles. Our simulator supports three to six Degrees of Freedom (DoF) and produces standardized metrics and reproducible run manifests, enabling consistent aggregation across tasks, difficulties, and scaling settings. Experiments across dataset variants and scenes systematically stress planning and control under data-grounded disturbances and reveal trade-offs among heuristic baselines, Behavior Cloning (BC), and a Large Language Model (LLM)-based planner.

</details>


### [17] [Memory Is Not Ground Truth: Revising Navigation Authority under Unreliable VLM Evidence for Persistent Semantic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34915)
*Sumukh Shrote,Shenbagaraj Kannapiran,Eric Eaton,Camillo Jose Taylor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在Boston Dynamics Spot上实验表明，VLM感知下的重复未检出证据模糊，无法区分目标缺失与记忆失效；提出依赖感知的可逆记忆更新机制，用相关性消除重复漏检的双重计数，并将无支持记忆从导航目标降级但不断言目标缺失，后续正面证据可恢复其可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆能减少野外机器人冗余搜索，但重复漏检可能意味着记忆过时；然而VLM感知下的漏检证据存在歧义，需明确何时应继续用记忆引导行为，以及如何避免把检测失败误判为目标不存在。

Method: 使用四台固定相机的Spot与持久时空视觉记忆，跨目标、视角、语言查询和重复回访测量负验证行为；提出依赖感知、可逆的记忆更新机制，避免对相关漏检重复计数，将缺乏支持的记忆停止用作直接导航目标而不宣称目标缺失；机载单一VLM共享于记忆验证、语义前沿推理和运动感知。

Result: 假阴性率随物体显著变化，邻近回访常复现相同检测器结果，目标存在与目标移除的匹配实验可产生相同漏检历史；该歧义在观察几何改变、保持身份的查询改写及以已验证目标图像为条件下仍存在。三个匹配真实机器人决策情境中，情节局部基线均选择过时记忆为导航目标，本方法均选择语义前沿；补充试验在运动中重新验证并提升新鲜记忆。

Conclusion: 应修正记忆的可行动性而非将检测失败解释为目标缺失；依赖感知的可逆更新机制能在证据模糊时安全降级记忆用途，并在后续正面证据出现时恢复其导航可用性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable robots to reason about semantic and spatio-temporal context, verify task-relevant objects, and guide sensing and navigation. Persistent memory can reduce redundant search for field robots, but when should a memory continue to guide behavior? Repeated misses may suggest that a memory is stale. Our experiments show why this evidence can remain ambiguous under VLM-based perception. Using a Boston Dynamics Spot with four fixed cameras and a persistent spatio-temporal visual memory, we measure negative-verification behavior across targets, viewpoints, language queries, and repeated revisits. False-negative rates vary substantially across objects, nearby revisits often reproduce the same detector outcome, and some matched target-present and target-removed experiments yield identical miss histories. This ambiguity persists under changed observation geometry, identity-preserving query reformulation, and conditioning on a previously verified target image. These findings raise two questions: is the object still there, and is this memory still useful for navigation? We introduce a dependence-aware, reversible memory-update mechanism that avoids double-counting correlated misses and stops using unsupported memories as direct navigation goals without claiming that the object is absent. Later positive evidence can restore a memory's use for navigation. One resident onboard VLM is shared across memory verification, semantic frontier reasoning, and in-motion perception. In three matched real-robot decision contexts, an episode-local baseline selected a stale memory as its navigation objective in every case, whereas our method selected semantic frontiers in all three. A complementary trial re-verified the target during motion and promoted fresh memory. These results support revising memory actionability rather than interpreting failed detection as object absence.

</details>


### [18] [Cairn: Object-Centric 3D Trajectory Priors for Steering Frozen VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34914)
*Agelos Kratimenos,Kostas Daniilidis,Evangelos Chatzipantazis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cairn 保持视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）冻结，在测试时用一个学习到的 SE(3) 轨迹先验引导其去噪过程，从而在物体被移动或存在障碍物的场景中显著提升操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 作为低级控制器，会把物体的放置位置绑定到训练时的布局上：物体被移动或障碍物挡路时，它会把物体带到错误位置或撞上障碍。针对新布局进行微调既需要为每种布局采集机器人数据，又会损害模型已有的技能。

Method: 提出 Cairn 两层分层规划器：小型先验网络读取场景与所持物体的点云、抓取时的位姿以及指令，采样从抓取到释放的 SE(3) 轨迹，使物体绕开障碍并调整朝向以穿过狭窄空间；先验与冻结的 VLA 构成双向反馈——VLA 的预期运动用于选择和重塑轨迹，选中的轨迹则引导 VLA 的去噪；VLA 预测的动作经抓取关系映射为所持物体位姿，并被拉向下一路点，而 VLA 权重保持不变。

Result: 在 1,941 个包含物体位移与障碍的留出 LIBERO 场景上，Cairn 将冻结的 π0.5 成功率从 24.5% 提升到 95.1%，高于在训练布局上微调的 π0.5，且不损害标准 LIBERO 表现；在第三方 LIBERO-PRO 基准上成功率从 27.3% 提升到 38.0%，在其引导的回合上从 54.4% 提升到 81.1%；在真实 Franka 上，仅用仿真训练的先验引导冻结的 DROID π0.5 绕过障碍并完成手臂可达极限处的放置，而原模型在此失败。

Conclusion: 通过在测试时用轨迹先验引导冻结的 VLA，Cairn 无需额外机器人数据或微调即可显著提升对新布局与障碍场景的泛化能力，并保持原有技能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are capable low-level controllers, but they tie where objects go to the layouts they were trained on: when objects are moved or obstacles block the way, they carry them to the wrong place or into collisions. Fine-tuning on new layouts needs robot data for each one and erodes skills the model already has. We introduce Cairn, which keeps the VLA frozen and steers it at test time along learned 3D trajectories of the object in its gripper. A small prior reads point clouds of the scene and the held object, along with its pose when grasped and the instruction, and samples SE(3) trajectories up to its release that lead it around obstacles and turn it to fit through gaps. The prior and the VLA form the two levels of a hierarchical planner in which feedback runs both ways: the VLA's intended motion chooses and reshapes the trajectories, and the chosen one guides the VLA's denoising. Its predicted actions, mapped to the held object's poses through the grasp, are pulled toward the next waypoint, while the VLA's weights stay untouched. Across 1,941 held-out LIBERO scenes with displaced objects and obstacles, Cairn raises the success rate of a frozen {\pi}0.5 from 24.5% to 95.1%, above that of a {\pi}0.5 fine-tuned on our training layouts, without hurting standard LIBERO. On the third-party LIBERO-PRO benchmark, it raises success from 27.3% to 38.0%, and from 54.4% to 81.1% on the episodes it steers. On a real Franka, with a prior trained purely in simulation, it guides the frozen DROID {\pi}0.5 around an obstacle and to a placement at the limit of the arm's reach, where it otherwise fails. All code, models and data will be made public.

</details>


### [19] [FineDex: Bridging Fine-Grained Human-Object Interaction Generation to Dexterous Humanoid Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34884)
*Yujia Zeng,Yuxin Chen,Yuzhen Song,Runhan Huang,Chensheng Peng,Yiyang Shao,Koushil Sreenath,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FineDex是一个框架，使手指级意图既可表达又可执行，通过学习协调的人体、手和物体运动来遵循细粒度手部选择、单个手指和关节物体接触指令，并将生成交互作为物理仿真数字孪生中智能体的可操作先验，实现无需逐实例重训练的自适应泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作和人物交互（HOI）生成方法很少允许指定交互如何执行：五指手常被简化为夹爪，或手指使用方式直接复制自单个人类演示，缺乏对手指级控制的表达与执行能力。

Method: FineDex学习协调的人体、手和物体运动，遵循关于手部选择、单个手指和关节物体接触的细粒度指令；将生成的交互作为可操作先验，供智能体在基于物理的数字孪生中通过练习发现物理可行的接触策略；在测试时自适应而无需逐实例重训练。

Result: 实验表明FineDex实现了最先进的HOI生成质量和细粒度可控性，灵巧操作成功率显著提升，并成功部署在真实灵巧人形机器人上。

Conclusion: FineDex弥合了生成与执行之间的差距，使手指级意图既可表达又可执行，并泛化到细粒度指令和物体配置，在仿真和真实机器人上均有效。

Abstract: Dexterity is, at its core, finger-level control, yet existing dexterous manipulation and human-object interaction (HOI) generation methods rarely let one specify how an interaction is performed: five-fingered hands are often reduced to grippers, or finger usage is copied from individual human demonstrations. We present FineDex, a framework that makes finger-level intent both expressible and executable. FineDex learns coordinated human body, hand, and object motions that follow fine-grained instructions on hand selection, individual fingers, and contacts with articulated objects. To bridge generation and execution, FineDex leverages generated interactions as actionable priors for an agent that discovers physically feasible contact strategies through practice in a physics-based digital twin. By adapting at test time without per-instance retraining, FineDex generalizes across fine-grained instructions and object configurations. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art HOI generation quality and fine-grained controllability, substantial improvements in dexterous manipulation success, and successful deployment on a real dexterous humanoid robot.

</details>


### [20] [RePace: Reasoning about Demonstration Speedup for Faster Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34864)
*Insup Lee,Hojin Jeon,Sunguk Shin,Seoungbin Bae,Taekwan Kim,Jinwoo Shin,Dongsu Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RePace是一种离线推理框架，通过语言模型结合视觉上下文和动作序列为演示的每个部分选择变速方案，以加速视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略训练并提升执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略继承人类演示的慢速，离线加速演示可让VLA策略在部署时无需额外速度选择模块即更快。现有离线加速方法按片段分配固定加速因子，但同一片段内稳态运动合适的因子在需精确对齐时可能过高。

Method: RePace对同一记录动作，衡量每个候选加速因子缩短序列的程度，并比较生成的观测-动作对；预训练语言模型利用任务指令、视觉上下文和连续窗口原始动作解读候选重定时效果，并为整个回合选择加速配置文件。

Result: 在LIBERO和RoboCasa-Kitchen上，RePace整体任务执行效率优于基线；在Franka Research 3机械臂的三个任务上，成功率匹配或超过加速基线，且成功试验完成更快。

Conclusion: RePace通过离线为演示不同部分自适应选择加速方案，能在不牺牲成功率的前提下提升VLA策略的任务执行效率。

Abstract: Since vision-language-action (VLA) policies can inherit the slow pace of human demonstrations, accelerating these demonstrations before fine-tuning can lead to faster VLA policies without an additional speed-selection module at deployment. Several offline speedup methods have focused on identifying segments that require less precision and assigning predefined speedup factors to the segments. However, even within a single segment, a speedup factor suitable for steady motion may be too high when precise alignment is required. To this end, this paper presents RePace, an offline reasoning framework that determines how much to accelerate each part of a demonstration. For the same recorded movement, RePace measures how much each candidate speedup factor shortens the sequence and compares the resulting observation-action pairs used for training. Given the task instruction, a pretrained language model uses the visual context and original motion of successive windows to interpret these candidate retiming effects and select a speedup profile for the complete episode. Experimental results demonstrate that RePace outperforms the baselines in overall task execution efficiency on both LIBERO and RoboCasa-Kitchen. On all three tasks with a gripper-equipped Franka Research 3 arm, RePace matches or exceeds the acceleration baselines in success rate and completes successful trials faster.

</details>


### [21] [Real2SimLab: A Unified Platform for Real-to-Simulation Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34849)
*Yongchang Zhang,Jie Huang,Yunhe Ni,Ningyu Zhou,Guangyu Zhao,Ruixun Liu,Rui Huang,Yidi Wu,TianZe Zhang,Zixiang Jiang,Xiang Li,Kevin Shen,Yiwu Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一平台Real2SimLab，解决真实到仿真迁移评估中多个基准和方法组合导致的N×M集成问题，并支持训练数据与奖励标注生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实到仿真迁移的各个基准定义独立的输入、交付物和评分流程，评估N个方法在M个基准上需要N×M次集成，效率低下。

Method: Real2SimLab通过运行方法生成的程序并用各基准原始评分器对输出仿真打分来评估方法；同时提供训练数据和奖励标注流水线，通过过滤基准中已有视频避免数据污染，对每个视频生成程序并用与评估相同的代码计算奖励。

Result: 平台已支持47个编码智能体和5种非智能体方法，覆盖6个基准；并发布近300篇真实到仿真论文的研究地图Awesome-Real2Sim，二者均开源。

Conclusion: Real2SimLab作为统一平台简化了真实到仿真迁移的评估与数据生成，并邀请Real2SimHub社区共同建设。

Abstract: Real-to-simulation transfer, which builds simulations from real-world observations, has long been studied in research and industry. Recently, coding agents have become capable of generating simulation programs from video recordings, and several benchmarks have been proposed to evaluate them. However, each individual benchmark defines its own inputs, deliverables, and scoring pipeline. This leads to $N \times M$ integrations when evaluating $N$ methods on $M$ benchmarks. To this end, we present Real2SimLab, a unified platform for real-to-simulation transfer. It evaluates a method by running its written program and scoring the output simulation with the original scorer of each benchmark. Moreover, Real2SimLab provides a pipeline to create training data and reward annotations for real-to-simulation methods. Our pipeline carefully avoids data contamination by filtering out the video recordings that exist in benchmarks. For each video recording, a program is first produced, and then our pipeline computes its reward with the same code that Real2SimLab uses for evaluation. So far, it supports 47 coding agents and 5 non-agent methods on 6 benchmarks. Both the platform and Awesome-Real2Sim, our research map of nearly 300 real-to-simulation papers, are open source, and we invite the Real2SimHub community to build them together.

</details>


### [22] [Toward Agentic Robotics: A Large-Scale Study of Harness Design for Robot-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34778)
*Yuxiang Lai,Jike Zhong,Yu-Jhe Li,Lan Wang,Chengzhi Liu,Youbao Tang,Ming Li,Xi Xiao,Xiaofeng Yang,Liang Zhao,Zheda Mai,Ruixi Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过47万次仿真实验系统分析了VLM推理与VLA/WAM控制结合的策略框架设计，提出了X-Harness参考方案，无需权重更新即可将基础策略性能提升高达42.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM作为机器人代理在精确3D抓取位姿预测上表现差，而VLA/WAM模型在域偏移和长程推理任务上失败；将二者结合的agentic机器人策略框架设计空间巨大，同一模型下不同设计成功率相差数十点，但测试成本极高，导致现有工作往往只从少量方案中选定单一设计。

Method: 在八个仿真基准、六种VLM和15个VLA/WAM检查点上评估超过47万次rollout，开展七项受控研究，分析策略框架如何适配VLM与VLA/WAM的能力，并据此提出参考配方X-Harness。

Result: 实验表明有效的策略框架必须同时适配VLM与VLA/WAM的能力；研究明确了VLM策略代码所需的结构、VLA操作需要工具与策略代码辅助的场景，以及测试时引入VLM的时机；X-Harness组合这些发现后无需权重更新即将基础策略在记忆、推理和域偏移任务上提升最高42.8个百分点。

Conclusion: Agentic机器人的策略框架设计需以VLM和VLA/WAM能力互补为核心原则，X-Harness证明了系统化设计规则可组合并显著提升性能，为后续框架设计提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Recent vision-language models (VLMs) can act as robot-use agents that understand diverse scenes and control robots through policy code and tools. However, they predict 3D gripper poses poorly even with extended reasoning, struggling with precise manipulation. Vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action (WAM) models are the opposite: they execute fast, smooth control on trained tasks, yet fail under domain shifts or when tasks require long-horizon memory and reasoning. Agentic robots therefore combine VLM reasoning and perception with precise VLA/WAM control through a policy harness. Yet harness design spans thousands of configurations, shifting success by tens of points with the same models. Moreover, testing even one design takes thousands of rollouts at high API and GPU cost, so prior works often commit to a single design chosen from few alternatives. To guide harness design, we evaluate over 470,000 rollouts across eight simulation benchmarks, six VLMs, and 15 VLA/WAM checkpoints. Seven controlled studies show effective harnesses must adapt to both VLM and VLA/WAM capabilities. Our findings specify what structure the VLM's policy code needs, where VLA manipulation needs help from tools and policy code, and when to involve the VLM at test time. The reference recipe X-Harness shows that these findings compose without weight updates, lifting base policies up to $+42.8$ points across memory, reasoning, and domain shifts.

</details>


### [23] [Anytime-Valid Sequential Verification for Sensing-Efficient Robot Navigation under Perception Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34764)
*Saad Korchi,Abderrahim Waga*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于高斯似然比的序贯扫描门控规则，按需判定机器人下一步足迹覆盖的单元格是否足够自由，从而在静态栅格仿真中显著减少扫描次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定扫描次数在导航中对每个位置投入相同感知代价，噪声传感器读数使判断下一移动单元格是否自由变得困难；希望仅在需要时增加扫描以降低不确定性。

Method: 在静态栅格仿真中，采用序贯规则利用高斯似然比，判断机器人足迹新覆盖的单元格是否具有足够自由空间证据；与验证数据选出的四次扫描参考方法对比，并在多个公共地图的留出场景上评估扫描次数与任务完成情况。

Result: 在18个公共地图的324个留出场景上，序贯门控与四次扫描参考均无观测碰撞完成全部任务；各自验证选择的路径权重下，门控平均123.15次扫描/回合，参考为224.13次，减少45.05%（95%地图分层自助区间42.21–48.95%）。等路径权重回顾检查中减少43.91%和43.37%，任务全部完成。在12个未使用地图的216个场景上，将更多同一认证误差预算分配到起点—目标矩形附近，使序贯门控扫描数再降11.58%；空间变体完成206次任务（原门控208次）且计算时间增加。碰撞界依赖观测模型与足迹几何假设；在持续逐单元格偏差的压力测试中，原门控完成0/54次任务。

Conclusion: 序贯门控在静态栅格仿真中能在保持无碰撞任务完成的同时大幅减少扫描次数，但性能依赖观测模型与足迹几何假设，且在持续偏差下鲁棒性不足。

Abstract: When a robot navigates an unfamiliar grid, noisy sensor readings can make it hard to tell whether the cells along its next move are free. Additional scans can reduce that uncertainty, but a fixed scan count spends the same sensing effort at every position. In a static-grid simulation, a sequential rule uses a Gaussian likelihood ratio to determine when the cells newly covered by the robot's footprint have enough evidence of free space. On 324 held-out scenarios from 18 public maps, the sequential gate and a four-scan reference chosen on validation data both completed every mission without an observed collision. With their respective validation-selected routing weights, the gate averaged 123.15 scans per episode versus 224.13 for the reference, a reduction of 45.05% (95% map-stratified bootstrap interval: 42.21--48.95%). In a retrospective check on the same scenarios with routing weights held equal, the reductions were 43.91% and 43.37% at the two tested weights, with all missions completed. On 216 scenarios from 12 previously unused maps, allocating more of the same certification error budget near the initial start--goal rectangle cut the sequential gate's scan count by a further 11.58%. The spatial variant completed 206 missions versus 208 for the original gate and increased computation time. The collision bound depends on the assumed observation model and footprint geometry. In a stress test with persistent per-cell bias, the original gate completed 0 of 54 missions.

</details>


### [24] [SOLVE: Sparse, Ordered Keyposes Make LLM Agents Versatile and Efficient Robot Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34750)
*Xiang Li,Hongyu Zhou,Ya-Li Li,Tao Li,Rui Zhang,Junjie Lai,Weihua Zhang,Shengjin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SOLVE，一种无需训练的多模态LLM智能体策略，通过稀疏关键位姿序列与运动规划器插值实现高效机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在机器人操作中推理成本高、需密集逐步控制且存在工具瓶颈。

Method: LLM智能体每次调用输出稀疏有序的6-DoF末端关键位姿序列，观察结果后输出下一段，由运动规划器插值；智能体自主决定关键位姿数量与间距，利用稀疏深度查询（多视图三角、立体、单目深度）进行定位并处理噪声，同时通过执行反馈反思并蒸馏任务规则。

Result: RoboDojo达74.5%、RoboLab达84.5%的最优性能，平均每回合仅1.7美元（比密集控制策略少12倍）；真实机器人16个零样本任务成功率达88%（85回合中75次成功）。

Conclusion: 稀疏关键位姿策略能匹配遥操作演示的稀疏性，在无需训练的情况下实现高效、低成本且泛化性强的机器人操作。

Abstract: LLM agents have made remarkable progress in robot manipulation, yet they suffer from expensive inference, dense step-by-step control, and tool bottlenecks. We introduce SOLVE, a training-free, versatile and efficient agent policy: a multimodal LLM agent writes a sparse, ordered sequence of 6-DoF end-effector keyposes per call, observes the result, and writes the next, while a motion planner interpolates between them. The agent decides the number and spacing of keyposes for both fast long-range motion and fine-grained manipulation. This granularity matches the sparsity of teleoperated manipulation: most demonstrations in DROID can be compressed into a few keyposes instead of dense actions. SOLVE grounds keyposes with sparse depth queries answered by noisy sources, such as multi-view triangulation, stereo, or monocular depth, and copes with that noise itself. It also improves from execution feedback, reflecting on its own rollouts and distilling a few rules per task. SOLVE reaches state-of-the-art performance of 74.5% on RoboDojo and 84.5% on RoboLab, with only $1.7 per episode on average, 12x less than previous dense-control agent policies. On a real robot, the same agent achieves an 88% success rate (75 of 85 episodes) over 16 challenging zero-shot tasks. Our project page is at this https URL .

</details>


### [25] [How Well Do Vision-Language Scene Graphs Serve as Memory for Embodied Question Answering?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34745)
*Divyansh Pandey,Shubh Garg,Vedant Khandelwal,Niyati Rawal,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出用“可回答性”（固定规则阅读器仅凭场景图正确回答问题的比例）作为分离记忆保留证据、阅读错误和问题先验的指标，并在模拟多房间场景图流程中评估记忆修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身问答的答案准确率混合了保留的证据、阅读错误和问题先验，无法单独衡量记忆质量，因此需要一个能剥离这些因素的评估指标。

Method: 在模拟多房间场景图流程中定义可回答性：固定规则阅读器仅从图结构回答问题并计算正确率；实施四项针对性修复，并对比基线、重训练QA及真实视频测试。

Result: 基线仅恢复24.1%的可见物体类型且房间标签闪烁；四项修复使召回率翻倍，可回答性从27.2%升至37.4%，重训练QA从71.4%升至75.9%；但更高的物体F1可能降低可回答性，问题类型先验在每个预测图上均优于该阅读器，真实视频测试中图记忆反而降低准确率。

Conclusion: 这些探索性发现支持在评估答案准确率之外，同时评分保留的证据（如可回答性），以更细粒度地诊断具身问答的记忆质量。

Abstract: Embodied QA needs memory, but answer accuracy mixes retained evidence, reading errors and question priors. For a simulated multi-room scene-graph pipeline we define answerability: the fraction of questions a fixed rule-based reader answers correctly from the graph alone. The baseline recovers 24.1% of visible object types and its room labels flicker. Four targeted repairs double recall, raise answerability from 27.2% to 37.4%, and improve retrained QA from 71.4% to 75.9%, yet better object F1 can lower answerability. A question-type prior beats this reader on every predicted graph. In one real-video test, graph memory lowers accuracy. These exploratory findings favour scoring retained evidence alongside accuracy.

</details>


### [26] [ELMO: Emergent Representations In Imitation Learning Via Dynamic Hierarchical Action Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34741)
*Jasper Gerigk,Yang Xu,Nicholas Rhinehart,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ELMO提出端到端框架，将机器人轨迹编码为基于环境的语义动作码，无需两阶段训练，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜动作模型依赖两阶段训练和上下文盲解码器，产生的表示与上下文无关（如“向下移动”），无法捕捉行为意图（如“拿起积木”），且最小化MSE的训练目标掩盖了动作的语义结构。

Method: ELMO联合训练VLA规划器与观测条件解码器，在动态分块的轨迹上学习语义接地的动作空间，将任务级行为意图与当前环境结合，无需单独预训练。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus上比同类方法高9.6个百分点，CALVIN平均任务长度提升0.75；在Blocks基准上成功率达38.6%，而所有基线均低于5%。

Conclusion: ELMO能够捕捉演示的语义结构，验证了端到端学习语义接地动作空间的有效性，并显著提升了泛化能力。

Abstract: Robot trajectories naturally decompose into variable-length phases with distinct semantic intents, such as reaching, grasping, and lifting. However, training policies to minimize MSE treats all actions as equal, obscuring their semantic structure. While latent action models attempt to create a structured prediction target by compressing action chunks into latent representations, existing approaches rely on two-stage training and context-blind decoders. Consequently, their representations are context-agnostic motion codes (e.g., "move down") which fail to capture behavioural intent (e.g., "pick up the block"). To overcome these bottlenecks, we introduce the Emergent Latent-action MOdel (ELMO), an end-to-end framework that encodes robot trajectories as grounded semantic codes -- representations that ground task-level behavioural intent in the current environment. ELMO jointly trains a VLA planner and an observation-conditioned decoder over dynamically chunked trajectories, learning a semantically grounded action space without separate pretraining. Across standard benchmarks, ELMO outperforms comparable prior methods by 9.6 percentage points on LIBERO-Plus and by 0.75 in average task length on CALVIN. Furthermore, on Blocks -- our shortcut-free simulation and real-world generalization benchmark -- ELMO reaches a 38.6% success rate while all baselines remain below 5%, demonstrating its ability to capture the semantic structure of demonstrations. Website: this https URL

</details>


### [27] [RoboEvo$^2$: Recursive Robot Self-Improvement via Harness-Policy Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34675)
*Chenzhi Liu,Jiehong Lin,Wei Huang,Xuzhao Huang,Haoyuan Li,Yang Zhang,Junchao Huang,Chenyeyang Cai,Bo Wang,Kazuhiro Kosuge,Zhongrui Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboEvo²框架，通过递归的harness与策略协同进化，让机器人无需额外人类演示即可适应新环境并高效获得新技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作策略控制高效但面对陌生场景需额外人类演示；推理智能体可借助先验知识和API发现解决方案，但反复推理与修复代价高。受人类推理与实践学习启发，希望融合二者优势。

Method: 框架包含两个闭环过程：Policy-to-Harness（P2H）进化中，策略失败引导编码智能体搜索Code、Code+ICL、Code+Policy互补路径，将验证过的解决方案整合为可复用技能、路由策略和经验记忆；Harness-to-Policy（H2P）进化中，验证轨迹提供监督训练，反事实偏好蒸馏（CPD）使策略偏好同一初始状态下的成功修正而非失败。改进后的策略为后续搜索提供更强运动技能，剩余失败再驱动harness进化，形成递归互惠循环。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO上，独立成功率从11.0%提升至84.1%，混合成功率从30.4%提升至90.5%；在RoboTwin C2R上取得类似增益并降低智能体开销；实验还展示了自主任务获取、跨具身harness迁移，以及在Unitree G1-D上达到95.0%混合成功率。

Conclusion: RoboEvo²通过无需额外人类演示的递归协同进化，有效提升机器人策略的独立与混合成功率，并具备自主任务获取与跨具身迁移能力，模型与代码将公开。

Abstract: Robots should adapt to unfamiliar environments and acquire new skills through interaction, then execute those skills efficiently with practice. Vision-language-action policies provide efficient control, but often need additional human demonstrations to handle unfamiliar settings; reasoning agents can discover solutions using broad prior knowledge and robot-control APIs, but repeated deliberation and repair are costly. Inspired by human learning through reasoning and practice, we propose RoboEvo$^2$, a framework for recursive harness-policy co-evolution. In Policy-to-Harness (P2H) evolution, policy failures guide a coding agent to search complementary Code, Code+ICL, and Code+Policy routes, consolidating verified solutions into reusable skills, routing strategies, and experience memory. In Harness-to-Policy (H2P) evolution, verified trajectories provide supervised training, while Counterfactual Preference Distillation (CPD) teaches the policy to favor successful corrections over failures from the same starting state. The improved policy provides stronger motor skills for subsequent search, while its remaining failures redirect harness evolution, closing the reciprocal loop. Through recursive co-evolution without additional human demonstrations, RoboEvo$^2$ improves standalone success from 11.0% to 84.1% and hybrid success from 30.4% to 90.5% on LIBERO-PRO, with similar gains and reduced agentic costs on RoboTwin C2R. Further experiments demonstrate autonomous task acquisition, cross-embodiment harness transfer, and 95.0% hybrid success on the Unitree G1-D. Our models and code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [28] [DemoFoundry: Toward Scalable Photorealistic Robot Data with Coding Agents and Generative Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34671)
*Ruixiang Wang,Xinyue Yao,Xuanning Zhou,Yueci Deng,Yongyi Su,Kui Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DemoFoundry框架通过结合编码智能体与生成式渲染，自动合成具有真实感视觉和可靠动作监督的机器人演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习需要兼具真实视觉观察和可靠动作监督的演示数据，但仿真合成存在视觉差距，纯视频生成难以保证物理一致性和动作标签对齐。

Method: 将编码智能体与生成式渲染耦合：智能体从现有机器人片段构建对齐的真实-代理视频对以训练视频渲染器，并编写可执行交互程序；给定场景参考和任务指令，智能体重建任务相关几何并编写控制程序，在物理仿真器中执行生成带对齐状态和动作的代理视频，再经渲染器转为逼真观察；自动任务执行检查筛选成功轨迹用于渲染和策略学习。

Result: 实验显示视觉质量和动作-视频一致性提升；在生成演示上训练的策略优于原始仿真训练，并接近使用等量真实演示训练的性能。

Conclusion: DemoFoundry能够自动合成高质量、物理 grounded 的机器人演示，有效缩小仿真与真实之间的差距，为机器人学习提供可扩展的演示数据来源。

Abstract: Scaling robot learning requires demonstrations that combine realistic visual observations with reliable action supervision. Simulation-based synthesis faces a substantial visual gap relative to real environments, while video-only generation struggles to maintain physically consistent interactions and provide aligned action labels. We present DemoFoundry, an automated framework that couples coding agents with generative rendering to synthesize photorealistic and physically grounded robot demonstrations. Coding agents both construct aligned real--proxy video pairs from existing robot episodes to train a video renderer and write executable interaction programs to generate new demonstrations. Given a scene reference and task instruction, an agent reconstructs task-relevant geometry and writes a control program without requiring target-task human demonstrations. Program execution in a physics simulator produces proxy videos with aligned robot states and actions. The video renderer translates these proxy videos into scene-consistent, photorealistic observations, reducing the need for detailed appearance modeling in each scene. Automated task-execution checks select successful trajectories for rendering and policy learning. Experiments show improved visual quality and action--video consistency. Policies trained on the generated demonstrations outperform raw-simulation training and approach the performance of training on an equal number of real demonstrations.

</details>


### [29] [Autonomous Ergodic Robotic Palpation for Elastic Mapping of Curved Soft Tissues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34670)
*Luca Beber,Cem Bilaloglu,Edoardo Lamon,Matteo Saveriano,Daniele Fontanelli,Luigi Palopoli,Sylvain Calinon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于曲面点云和Laplace-Beltrami算子的遍历式机器人触诊框架，实现复杂曲面软组织上刚度包涵体的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主机器人触诊方法依赖先验网格模型或平面假设，难以泛化到复杂人体解剖曲面。

Method: 利用点云数据直接在曲面软组织上操作；以Laplace-Beltrami算子刻画曲面内蕴几何；用无迹卡尔曼滤波在线估计局部粘弹性参数并融合进曲面感知高斯过程；由扩散模型驱动的遍历运动规划器生成连续探索轨迹，闭环反馈新样本；混合力-阻抗控制器调节接触力。

Result: 在仿真中与现有方法对比评估，并在不同几何形状和粘弹性性质的仿组织硅胶体模上开展物理实验，实现几何一致的映射及接触丰富条件下的刚度包涵体定位。

Conclusion: 该框架无需先验网格或平面假设，可在复杂曲面软表面上实现一致的属性映射与刚度包涵体定位。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic palpation offers repeatable, quantitative estimates of tissue properties for stiff inclusion localization. However, existing methods often rely on prior mesh models or planar surface assumptions that do not generalize to complex human anatomy. We propose an ergodic palpation framework that operates directly on curved soft surfaces using point cloud data. Central to our approach is the Laplace--Beltrami operator, which grounds both online estimation and exploration in the surface intrinsic geometry. We estimate local viscoelastic parameters using an Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF) and fuse them into a surface-aware Gaussian Process (GP). This probabilistic model drives a diffusion-based ergodic motion planner, generating a continuous exploration trajectory that feeds the new informative samples back to the GP in a closed loop. Throughout this process, a hybrid force-impedance controller regulates interaction forces to maintain contact. We evaluate the approach against existing methods in simulation and validate it in physical experiments on tissue-mimicking silicone phantoms with varying geometries and viscoelastic properties, demonstrating geometry-consistent mapping and stiff inclusion localization under contact-rich conditions.

</details>


### [30] [FeaXDrive2: Body-Scene Spatial Grounding for Feasibility-Aware Generative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34658)
*Bo Leng,Baoyun Wang,Wenxin Wen,Ming Liu,Weiqi Zhang,Ran Yu,Chongfeng Wei,Zhuoren Li,Lu Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FeaXDrive2 通过车身-场景空间对齐提升生成式驾驶规划的全身空间可行性，在 NAVSIM 上显著降低有责碰撞并提升 PDMS/EPDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式驾驶规划器在参考点处轨迹看似可行，但整车车身在完整轨迹上可能违反环境约束，缺乏对车身几何的空间可行性建模。

Method: 提出端到端生成规划框架 FeaXDrive2：以真实车身角点构建空间查询，随 Flow 步中演化的轨迹估计动态更新视觉采样位置；检索的局部视觉特征条件化 Action Flow-DiT 速度预测，实现轨迹生成引导空间信息检索；采用 Rollout-Consistent Safety Fine-Tuning（RC-F）结合高风险轨迹监督与参考行为保持，并进行规划监督的 VLM-Action 对齐；辅以车辆几何反事实分析。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 上有责碰撞相对基础规划器降低 36.5%，PDMS 从 87.0 提升至 88.8；NAVSIM v2 下 EPDMS 从 86.5 提升至 88.3；消融与车身间隙分析进一步验证车身-场景空间对齐的安全收益，车辆几何反事实分析揭示相同轨迹下不同车身几何的安全差异。

Conclusion: 车身-场景空间对齐能有效提升车辆具身空间可行性，FeaXDrive2 在降低碰撞风险与提升规划安全指标方面均优于基线。

Abstract: Generative driving planners may produce trajectories that appear feasible at their reference points but violate environmental constraints over the full vehicle body. We propose FeaXDrive2, an end-to-end generative planning framework that enhances vehicle-embodied spatial feasibility (VESF) through body-scene spatial grounding. The proposed method constructs spatial queries from actual vehicle-body corners and dynamically updates visual sampling locations using evolving trajectory estimates across Flow steps. Retrieved local visual features condition subsequent Action Flow-DiT velocity predictions, allowing trajectory generation to guide spatial information retrieval. Rollout-Consistent Safety Fine-Tuning (RC-F) combines high-risk trajectory supervision with reference behavior preservation, followed by planning-supervised VLM-Action Alignment. Vehicle-geometry counterfactual analysis reveals safety differences under identical trajectories but varying vehicle geometry. On NAVSIM, FeaXDrive2 reduces at-fault collisions by 36.5% relative to the base planner and improves PDMS from 87.0 to 88.8. Under NAVSIM v2 evaluation, EPDMS increases from 86.5 to 88.3. Ablation and body-clearance analyses further demonstrate the safety benefits of body-scene spatial grounding.

</details>


### [31] [Nav4Manip: From Navigation to Dexterous Mobile Manipulation through Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34655)
*Yusen Feng,Zixi Kang,Zhijun Yin,Changxiao Liu,Tianhong Gao,Jiazhao Zhang,Jiahang Liu,Libin Liu,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双向耦合的协同演化系统Nav4Manip，解决移动操作中导航精度不足与导航-操作割裂的问题，并构建Nav-N-Manip基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统导航过于粗糙，难以满足下游操作对精确基座位置和接近方向的需求；且导航与操作孤立训练，缺乏反馈闭环，无法协同演化，导致长程精细移动操作任务在真实环境中不可靠。

Method: Nav4Manip包含两个互补架构：Nav4Manip-N学习长程导航并通过操作反馈精化策略，Nav4Manip-M训练世界-动作模型（WAM）负责下游精细操作。Nav4Manip-N分三阶段训练：多任务协同微调学习导航行为与操作感知可供性预测；拒绝采样微调（RFT）剔除低质量路径并在保留轨迹上微调；基于AI反馈的强化学习（RLAIF）利用反事实偏好改进导航决策。同时建立Nav-N-Manip基准，并从基准推演中构建Nav4Manip-Pairs，提供大规模成对导航-操作轨迹与可供性监督。

Result: 在公开视觉语言导航（VLN）基准和Nav-N-Manip上取得最先进性能；真实世界评估表明Nav4Manip能完成多样化的精细移动操作任务。

Conclusion: 双向耦合的协同演化框架有效提升了长程精细移动操作的可靠性，导航与操作可相互反馈、共同进化，并在仿真与真实环境中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Dexterous mobile manipulation is essential for robots to perform useful tasks in complex, unstructured environments. However, existing systems face two challenges. First, navigation remains too coarse for downstream manipulation, which often requires a precise base position and approach orientation rather than merely reaching a meter-scale goal region. Second, navigation and manipulation are commonly trained in isolation, lacking a feedback loop that enables the two capabilities to co-evolve. These limitations prevent current systems from reliably executing long-horizon dexterous mobile-manipulation tasks in real-world environments. To address them, we introduce Nav4Manip, a bidirectionally coupled co-evolution system for long-horizon dexterous mobile manipulation. It comprises two complementary architectures: Nav4Manip-N learns long-horizon navigation and refines its policy through manipulation feedback, while Nav4Manip-M trains a world-action model (WAM) for downstream dexterous manipulation. We train Nav4Manip-N in three stages: multi-task co-tuning learns navigation behavior and manipulation-aware affordance prediction; rejection sampling fine-tuning (RFT) rejects low-quality policy-generated paths and fine-tunes the model on retained trajectories; and reinforcement learning from AI feedback (RLAIF) uses counterfactual preferences to further improve navigation decisions. To support this framework, we establish the Nav-N-Manip benchmark and construct Nav4Manip-Pairs from benchmark rollouts, providing large-scale paired navigation--manipulation trajectories and affordance supervision. Extensive experiments on public vision-language navigation (VLN) benchmarks and Nav-N-Manip demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, while real-world evaluations show that Nav4Manip completes diverse dexterous mobile-manipulation tasks. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [32] [$μ$MPC: Microsecond-Scale Nonlinear and World Model Control from Edge to GPU via Koopman-Linearized Precompilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34597)
*Devesh Nath,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出μMPC框架，利用学习的Koopman线性化动力学和预计算控制-输出映射，将MPC在线计算转化为仅含Hn_u个决策变量的QP，实现快速求解并支持控制感知学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MPC在线计算量大，尤其在高维或被控系统复杂时；现有Koopman方法虽能线性化动力学，但提升状态维度导致优化变量增多、求解慢。

Method: 预计算有限时域控制到输出映射以消除提升状态轨迹，得到仅含Hn_u个决策变量的Koopman-Compiled QP；采用固定预计算原始算子进行原始-对偶迭代，仅需矩阵-向量乘和投影，适应变化的目标与约束而无需重新分解；通过可微KC-QP和批量DAgger式数据聚合进行控制感知动力学学习。

Result: 桌面CPU求解时间低至23μs，168MHz Crazyflie微控制器上24ms；扩展到4096维四足控制<5ms、2048维世界模型视觉规划0.4ms、200架四旋翼碰撞感知规划GPU上>30Hz；相比竞争方法，约束MPC快至108倍，视觉运动规划快至1907倍。

Conclusion: μMPC通过Koopman编译QP显著降低在线计算，保持约束处理能力，并支持高效学习与大规模高维应用。

Abstract: We present $\mu$MPC, a fast model predictive control (MPC) framework that reduces online computation using learned Koopman-linearized dynamics. By precomputing finite-horizon control-to-output maps, $\mu$MPC eliminates lifted-state trajectories, yielding an action-only Koopman-Compiled QP (KC-QP) with $Hn_u$ decision variables, independent of lifted dimension. A fixed, precomputed primal operator enables efficient primal-dual iterations using only matrix-vector products and projections, accommodating changing goals and constraints without refactorization. This efficiency also enables control-aware dynamics learning through differentiable KC-QPs in the training loop and batched DAgger-style data aggregation. $\mu$MPC achieves solve times as low as $23\mu$s on a desktop CPU and 24 ms onboard a 168 MHz Crazyflie microcontroller. It scales to 4096D quadruped control in under 5 ms, visual planning with 2048D world models in 0.4 ms, and collision-aware planning for 200 quadrotors at over 30 Hz on the GPU. Across these benchmarks, $\mu$MPC achieves up to $108\times$ faster constrained MPC and $1907\times$ faster visuomotor planning than competing methods.

</details>


### [33] [Geometric Acceleration Obstacles: Physical Boundary Structure and Affine Safety Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34586)
*Xuheng Gao,Yuhan Pang,Bi-Ke Zhu,Ronghao Chen,Lingxiao Meng,Jiankun Wang,Max Q.-H. Meng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出几何加速度障碍(GAO)，首次对加速度障碍(AO)的几何结构进行统一且物理验证的刻画，并据此构建实时避碰的二次规划导航方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境下的安全导航需要兼顾运动约束与计算效率。速度障碍(VO)方法依赖特定速度跟踪模型；加速度障碍(AO)方法虽直接在加速度空间表示碰撞输入，但现有方法要么采用保守近似，要么仅给出解析结构而未验证其几何对应性。

Method: 推导配置相关的半平面以全局支撑碰撞集，并证明其紧致性，从而将经认证的无碰撞加速度输入与碰撞输入分离；由此得到统一的仿射安全约束，结合执行器限制构成实时导航的二次规划(QP)。

Result: 大量实验表明GAO在评估方法中取得有竞争力的成功率与最低控制代价，计算延迟为毫秒级；圆形交换仿真验证了可扩展至1000个智能体；物理实验在行人相遇、互惠多智能体导航和高速机动场景中验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: GAO提供了首个统一且物理验证的加速度障碍几何刻画，所导出的仿射约束可高效嵌入QP，实现兼顾安全、效率与可扩展性的实时避碰导航。

Abstract: Safe robot navigation in dynamic environments requires collision avoidance that respects motion constraints while remaining computationally efficient. Conventional velocity-obstacle (VO) methods characterize collision-inducing motions in velocity space. Although some formulations account for acceleration-limited velocity transitions, their collision constraints remain tied to specific velocity-tracking models. Acceleration obstacles (AO) address this limitation by representing collision-inducing inputs directly in acceleration space. However, existing AO methods either rely on conservative approximations or derive analytical structure expressions without verifying their geometric correspondence. To address this gap, this paper presents Geometric Acceleration Obstacles (GAO), which provides, to the best of our knowledge, the first unified and physically validated characterization of AO geometry and its supporting structure. Specifically, we derive configuration-dependent half-planes that globally support the collision set and establish their tightness, thereby separating certified collision-free acceleration inputs from collision-inducing parts. These geometric results yield unified affine safety constraints. Combined with actuator limits, these constraints define a quadratic program (QP) for real-time navigation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GAO achieves competitive success rates and the lowest control effort among the evaluated methods, with millisecond-level computational latency. Further circle-interchange simulations demonstrate scalability to 1,000 agents. Physical experiments validate the approach in pedestrian encounters, reciprocal multi-agent navigation, and high-speed maneuvering.

</details>


### [34] [Riding the Wave: Sim-to-Real Humanoid Control on Elastic Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34569)
*Yinglei Zhu,Qin Li,Honghao Pan,Bozhao Wang,Binghui Xu,Jie Lu,Wentao Song,Xiyu Wang,Xinyu Zhu,Wei Liu,Xinjun Liu,Kai Lu,Huichan Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RIPPLE仿真到真实学习框架，结合ElasticMembrane可变形膜模型，实现人形机器人在大变形弹性地形上仅凭本体感觉的平衡与连续跳跃，并零样本迁移到真实蹦床。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形弹性地形的位置与运动在时空上耦合：形变与回弹在接触间持续，一处落脚载荷会影响其他支撑点，因此训练必须捕捉每次接触如何改变后续支撑。

Method: 构建ElasticMembrane（预张紧质量-弹簧网格，建模空间耦合形变、储能与回弹）及可变形接触并集成到MJLab进行GPU并行策略学习；采用分阶段训练将刚性地面技能迁移到弹性地形的平衡与跳跃，仅用本体感觉，无需视觉或力传感。

Result: 零样本部署使MagicBot Z1实现平衡、连续跳跃及从刚性平台进入蹦床；仿真中在0.3 m/s扰动下，ElasticMembrane训练在三种地形上站立成功率至少98.7%（三种子均值），而刚性地面或接触柔顺性训练迁移到前两种地形效果差；固定策略可容忍0.35至2.8倍训练刚度、最宽7 m床面及七种额外支撑场景。

Conclusion: RIPPLE无需针对蹦床刚度进行标定即可实现迁移，验证了仅凭本体感觉在时空耦合弹性地形上控制人形机器人的可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots on deformable elastic terrains face a surface whose position and motion are coupled in space and time: deflection and rebound persist between contacts, and load at one foothold alters the support at others, so training must capture how each contact changes the support that follows. We present RIPPLE (Robust Interaction via Proprioceptive Policies on Large-deformation Elastic terrain), a simulation-to-real learning framework for humanoid control on such terrains. RIPPLE integrates ElasticMembrane, a pretensioned mass-spring mesh that models spatially coupled deflection, energy storage, and rebound, together with deformable contact into MJLab for GPU-parallel policy learning. Staged training then transfers rigid-ground skills to balance and jumping on these terrains using only proprioception, without vision or force sensing. Deployed zero-shot, the policies enable a MagicBot Z1 humanoid to balance, jump consecutively, and enter a trampoline from a rigid platform. In simulation, ElasticMembrane training holds standing success of at least 98.7% (mean over three seeds) on ElasticMembrane, IndependentSprings, and BouncyPlate under 0.3 m/s perturbations, whereas rigid-ground or contact-compliance training transfers poorly to the first two. A fixed policy tolerates stiffness from 0.35 to 2.8 times the training value, beds up to 7 m wide, and seven further simulated support scenarios, so transfer needs no calibration to the trampoline's stiffness. We plan to release the code soon. The interactive browser demo, videos, and scene configurations are available on the project page: this https URL

</details>


### [35] [Compact Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environmentsvia On-Policy Training and Route-Bearing Output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34561)
*Reshad Ul Karim,Syeda Maliha Tabassum,Md. Khalilur Rhaman,Md Rashad Al Hasan Rony*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出2B参数单目RGB视觉语言导航策略，用方位角预测和在线纠正提升连续环境导航成功率并降低训练与推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高精度连续环境视觉语言导航策略依赖4-8B参数语言模型、长观测历史和大型辅助语料，难以在单GPU上低成本训练与运行；标准模仿学习仅覆盖专家状态且图像平面路点仅在路线位于相机视野内时有定义。

Method: 基于Qwen3-VL-2B加LoRA适配，单次前向处理八帧记忆与当前视图；策略在智能体机体坐标系中预测路线方位角，由确定性控制器将方位角转换为运动，从而可对策略自身访问状态进行重标注与在线纠正。

Result: 在R2R-CE的val unseen划分上三次种子平均成功率达rFinSR%，在RxR对应版本上达rRxFinSR%；推理耗时rDecMs毫秒、显存rInferGB GiB，单张12GB消费级GPU训练耗时rCostTrainTyp GPU小时；相同权重下方位角解码在所有评估起始朝向上优于图像平面路点扫描。

Conclusion: 方位角预测与在线纠正使小型2B策略在保持高成功率的同时显著降低训练和推理成本，适合单GPU部署。

Abstract: Instruction-following robots need navigation policies that reach high accuracy and remain inexpensive to train and to run on a single graphics processing unit (GPU). The most accurate vision-language navigation policies for continuous environments rely on language models with 4 to 8 billion parameters, long observation histories, and large auxiliary corpora. Two properties of the standard training setup limit smaller policies. Imitation labels cover the states that an expert visits, whereas the running policy reaches states that its own errors produce. In addition, an image-plane waypoint exists only while the route lies inside the camera view. We present \textbf{\paper}, a monocular red-green-blue (RGB) policy with 2 billion (2B) parameters that addresses both limitations. \paper adapts Qwen3-VL-2B with low-rank adapters and processes an eight-frame memory and the current view in one forward pass. The policy predicts the route direction as a bearing in the body frame of the agent, which remains defined for every heading, and a deterministic controller converts this bearing into motion. This design allows training to relabel the states that the policy itself visits. On-policy correction improves success, while bearing decoding outperforms image-plane waypoint scanning across all evaluated start headings with identical weights. On the \valunseen split, \paper achieves \rFinSR\% success on Room-to-Room in Continuous Environments (\rtor), averaged over three seeds, and \rRxFinSR\% on its Room-Across-Room counterpart (\rxr). Inference requires \rDecMs\,ms and \rInferGB\,GiB of memory, while training takes \rCostTrainTyp\ GPU-hours on one 12\,GB consumer GPU.

</details>


### [36] [M$^{2}$AdaptVLA: Multimodal Memory-Augmented Vision-Language-Action Model with Predictive Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34560)
*Liufan Tan,Tong Wang,Yuehao Yin,Jian Chen,Yihang Yin,Ying Qu,Wenyu Li,Ruyi Gan,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: M²AdaptVLA 是一个融合视觉记忆、力觉记忆与预测式测试时适应的视觉-语言-动作模型，用于提升接触敏感机器人操作的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略仅保留视觉或力觉历史，易丢失另一模态的关键线索；且固定策略在部署时接触条件变化下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 视觉记忆用测试时训练(TTT)将观测历史编码进快速权重，有界TTT库中每条目结合对应帧特征与权重读出；力觉记忆用上下文门控控制动作专家在联合去噪未来动作与力觉时对力觉历史的利用；预测式测试时适应以辅助力觉预测损失提供在线自监督，用实测力觉为目标，仅通过最终去噪步反向传播更新低秩策略适配器。

Result: 真实实验显示TTT库在进度跟踪、场景召回与示范跟随上优于视觉记忆基线；接触敏感任务中上下文门控成功率高于直接注入或门控交叉注意力；预测式测试时适应提升接触动力学变化下的鲁棒性；八个需视觉与力觉记忆的任务上平均成功率82.71%，较最强基线绝对提升31.46%。

Conclusion: 融合双模态记忆与预测式测试时适应可显著提升接触敏感操作的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Effective robotic manipulation calls for multimodal memory and rapid adaptation to shifts in contact dynamics. However, many existing policies, which retain either visual or wrench history, may miss key cues from the other modality. Moreover, even with wrench memory, fixed policies can struggle when contact conditions shift at deployment. To this end, we introduce M$^{2}$AdaptVLA, a vision-language-action model that integrates visual and wrench memories with predictive test-time adaptation.
