For visual memory, the model uses test-time training (TTT) to encode observation history in fast weights. Each entry in the bounded TTT bank combines the corresponding frame's features with a readout from these weights, preserving cues that predate the oldest retained frame. For wrench memory, a context gate controls how much the action expert draws on wrench history as it jointly denoises future actions and wrenches. For predictive test-time adaptation, the auxiliary wrench prediction loss also provides online self-supervision. Using measured wrenches as targets, we update low-rank policy adapters by backpropagating only through the final denoising step. Real-world experiments show that the TTT bank outperforms visual memory baselines on progress tracking, scene recall, and demonstration following. On contact-sensitive tasks, the context gate yields higher success rates than direct injection or gated cross-attention. Predictive test-time adaptation improves robustness to shifts in contact dynamics. On eight tasks requiring visual and wrench memory, M$^{2}$AdaptVLA achieves a mean success rate of 82.71%, a 31.46% absolute gain over the strongest baseline. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [37] [Reference-Model-Guided Distributed MPC for UAV Swarm Formation Flying Under Switching Directed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34540)
*Yiming Wang,Yenan Wu,Junda Wu,Yanke Sun,Huizhe Li,Ke Yu,Youmin Gong,Guangfu Ma,Jie Mei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出参考模型引导的分布式MPC框架，解决无人机集群在复杂环境下的编队控制问题，并通过仿真与实飞验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机集群在复杂环境中面临通信拓扑切换、通信受限、外部扰动和碰撞避免等挑战，需要一种可扩展且鲁棒的分布式编队控制方法。

Method: 每架无人机将邻居相对位置输入局部参考模型，输出作为机载MPC的编队引导；用卡尔曼滤波预测邻居运动，仅基于瞬时相对状态构建避碰约束；解耦MPC优化与分布式协调；采用扰动观测器前馈补偿，并在CBF避障约束中显式考虑有界残差扰动。

Result: 仿真和真实飞行实验验证了框架的有效性，飞行验证在无外部全局定位的全分布式多无人机平台上完成。

Conclusion: 该架构保留了MPC的预测规划与约束处理能力，增强了对切换有向图的适应性，降低了通信带宽需求，具备大规模集群可扩展性，并提升了鲁棒性与安全性。

Abstract: Distributed formation control of unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms in complex environments faces several challenges, including switching inter-agent connectivity, limited communication, external disturbances, and collision avoidance. To address these challenges, we propose a reference-model-guided distributed model predictive control (MPC) framework for UAV swarm formation. Each UAV uses relative-position information from its neighbors as input to a local reference model, whose output serves as the formation guidance for the onboard MPC controller. Meanwhile, a Kalman filter is utilized to predict neighboring UAV motions, enabling the construction of inter-UAV collision-avoidance constraints solely from instantaneous relative-state information. By decoupling MPC optimization from distributed coordination, this control architecture preserves the look-ahead planning and constraint-handling capabilities of MPC while enhancing the swarm's adaptability to switching directed graphs. Moreover, eliminating the exchange of neighbors' predicted trajectories reduces communication bandwidth demands, offering strong scalability for large-scale UAV swarms. To improve robustness and safety, disturbance-observer-based feedforward compensation is employed, while the bounded residual disturbance is explicitly considered in the control barrier function (CBF)-based obstacle-avoidance constraints. Finally, the proposed framework is validated through both simulations and real-world flight experiments. Specifically, the flight verification is conducted on a fully distributed multi-UAV platform without external global localization.

</details>


### [38] [Dynamic Task Update Benchmark (DTU-Bench): Evaluating Runtime Task Adaptation in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34538)
*Yanran Jiang,Cécile Paris,Dana Kulić,Leimin Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出DTU-Bench基准，评估VLA策略在执行中应对指令动态更新的能力，发现成功率从95.8%骤降至1.6%，并测试了改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略在固定指令基准上表现良好，但执行过程中指令变化时的适应能力缺乏研究。

Method: 构建DTU-Bench，基于LIBERO模拟环境，覆盖7种更新类型、3个执行阶段共40个任务修订；对比执行前与执行中给出新指令的表现，并测试释放物体并回退机械臂的脚本控制器。

Result: 13种VLA配置平均成功率：原任务95.8%，执行中改变操作对象仅1.6%；策略常先完成原任务且响应依赖机器人物理状态；脚本控制器将π0.5成功率从2%提升至38%、DM0.5从8%提升至48%，但会引起不期望的物体运动。

Conclusion: 固定指令基准与任务成功率指标会掩盖VLA策略在动态指令更新下的严重局限，需更全面的评估方法。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies perform well on fixed-instruction robotic manipulation benchmarks, but their ability to adapt to instruction changes during execution remains understudied. We introduce the Dynamic Task Update Benchmark (DTU-Bench), evaluating 40 task revisions across seven update types and three execution stages using LIBERO, a simulated robotic manipulation benchmark. We compare performance when a new instruction is given before execution starts versus during execution, and examine whether the new task instructions are followed. Across 13 representative VLA configurations, average task success is 95.8% on the original object-manipulation tasks, compared with only 1.6% when the instruction changes which object to manipulate during execution. Controlled diagnostics show that policies often complete the original task before the updated one, and that their responses depend on the robot's physical state. We further test whether a simple scripted controller that releases the grasped object and moves the arm back helps robots respond to instruction changes. This improves success from 2% to 38% for {\pi}0.5 and from 8% to 48% for DM0.5, but can cause unwanted object motion. These findings reveal limitations that fixed-instruction benchmarks and task-success metrics alone can overlook in evaluating robotic manipulation. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [39] [AGLR: Attribute-Guided Latent Refinement for Fine-Grained Humanoid Motion Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34519)
*Shuai Yuan,Yuwei Gui,Mingshuang Luo,Zhuo Li,Siyuan Meng,Wenhao Cui,RuiBing Hou,Bingpeng Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AGLR是一个统一的细粒度人形运动生成与编辑框架，通过属性引导的潜在空间精修，在保持源运动细节的同时满足局部几何约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形运动生成需适应变化的任务需求，局部调整须在不破坏期望运动细节的情况下满足新几何约束；但语言控制缺乏几何精度，关节级约束需显式空间目标，局部几何约束又欠定，难以兼顾目标满足与源保持。

Method: 提出AGLR（属性引导潜在精修）框架：紧凑几何接口支持肢体范围、躯干朝向和根轨迹的可组合、时变控制；给定生成或编码的基础潜在，属性残差适配器预测单次修正，并通过属性与运动坐标的双层监督实现目标匹配和选择性保持。

Result: 在HumanML3D-G1上，AGLR在可控生成和保持约束编辑成功率上优于对比方法；对缓存500帧运动编辑仅需9.1 ms，无需额外采样或测试时优化；在BONES-SEED-G1上零样本迁移无需微调。

Conclusion: AGLR为人形运动提供了高效、精确且可迁移的细粒度几何控制与编辑方案。

Abstract: Humanoid motion generation must accommodate evolving task requirements, where localized adjustments should satisfy new geometric constraints without disrupting desired motion details. However, language-based control lacks geometric precision, while joint-level constraints require explicit spatial targets. Local geometric constraints are also underdetermined, complicating target satisfaction and source preservation. We present AGLR (Attribute-Guided Latent Refinement), a unified framework for fine-grained humanoid motion generation and editing through source-anchored refinement. A compact geometric interface supports composable, time-varying control of limb extents, torso orientation, and root trajectories. Given a generated or encoded base latent, an Attribute Residual Adapter predicts a single-pass correction, with dual-level supervision enforcing target matching and selective preservation across attributes and motion coordinates. On HumanML3D-G1, AGLR achieves the highest controllable-generation and preservation-constrained editing success rates among evaluated methods. Editing take only 9.1 ms for a cached 500-frame motion, without additional sampling or test-time optimization. Zero-shot evaluation on BONES-SEED-G1 further demonstrates transfer without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [40] [Long-Horizon Planning with Latent World Models via Graph Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34509)
*Syed Nazmus Sakib,Abdul Monaf Chowdhury,Peizhen Li,Nafiul Haque,Chandak Chakma,Md Mehedi Hasan,Zehong Cao,Shifat E. Arman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出潜在世界模型中基于目标图像潜在距离的规划在远距离目标和接触操作时失效，提出免训练的Graph Anchors规划器，用已录轨迹上的近邻节点替代远目标，大幅提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型通过预测动作结果的潜在状态与目标图像的潜在距离来评估计划，但该距离在两类情形下失效：距目标几步时丢失方向信号导致远目标失败；权重偏向机械臂而非被操作物体导致接触时操作失败。

Method: 在记录数据上直接测量两种失败（较低潜在距离是否对应真实进展），跨五个环境、六种视觉表示和九个Meta-World任务验证排序测试可区分两类失败，并用图距离预测闭环规划崩溃点。提出Graph Anchors：在模型自身训练帧上构建稀疏图，每步规划瞄准沿最短路径前方几跳的节点，使路径尽量落在已记录转移上，无需训练且适用于冻结的潜在世界模型。

Result: 在远达400步的目标上，Graph Anchors将LeWM成功率从13%提升至85%（PointMaze-Medium）、从8%提升至87%（PointMaze-Large）、从5%提升至71%（Push-T）；而在这些场景中，更强的目标搜索会失败，先前免训练规划器在缺失步长视界、子采样捷径或接触碎片化时也失效。

Conclusion: 潜在距离作为规划目标存在方向信号丢失和物体权重偏差两类系统性失败，用已录轨迹上的近邻锚点替代远目标可同时缓解两者，Graph Anchors作为免训练规划器显著提升长程与接触操作任务的成功率。

Abstract: Latent world models plan in a compact latent space by predicting the outcomes of actions. Their planners judge a plan by the latent distance between its predicted end state and the goal image. We show that this distance fails in two distinct ways. First, it loses directional signal a few steps from the goal, causing distant goals to fail. Second, it weighs the robot arm over the manipulated object, causing manipulation to fail at contact. We measure both failures directly on recorded data by asking whether a lower latent distance corresponds to true progress toward the goal. Across five environments, six visual representations (including raw pixels and V-JEPA 2), and nine Meta-World tasks, this ranking test cleanly separates the two failures, and the graph distance to a goal predicts where closed-loop planning breaks down. Both failures share a single remedy: replacing the distant goal with a nearby target that lies on a recorded trajectory. We realize this principle with Graph Anchors, a training-free planner for frozen latent world models. It builds a sparse graph once over the model's own training frames and aims each planning step at a node a few moves ahead along the shortest path to the goal, keeping paths on recorded transitions wherever contact matters. On goals up to 400 steps away, Graph Anchors raise LeWM's success from 13% to 85% on PointMaze-Medium, from 8% to 87% on PointMaze-Large, and from 5% to 71% on Push-T, where stronger search toward the goal fails and where prior training-free planners break down under missing step horizons, subsampling shortcuts, or fragmented contact.

</details>


### [41] [An Exact Branch-Price-and-Cut Algorithm for the Vehicle Routing Problem with Floating Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34490)
*Michał Paradowski,Anoop Bhat,Zhongqiang Ren,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对浮标车辆路径问题(VRPFT)提出精确的分支定价切割算法，核心是基于闭式轨迹成本上下界的优势准则，加速定价标签算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VRPFT是容量约束车辆路径问题的推广，目标可主动以有限速度离开初始位置与车辆在时空点会合；已有精确动态规划方法计算时间较长，需要更高效的精确求解算法。

Method: 设计分支定价切割(Branch-Price-and-Cut)精确算法；关键贡献是定价标签算法中的优势准则，利用给定目标访问序列下车辆轨迹成本的闭式上下界来剪枝，并证明其保持最优性；另给出更激进的松弛准则形成启发式。

Result: 在相同硬件上，精确算法计算时间短于已有精确动态规划；松弛准则剪枝更强，所得启发式比精确算法更快，且在基准算例上匹配所有已认证最优解。

Conclusion: 基于闭式轨迹成本上下界的优势准则能有效加速VRPFT的精确求解，并可通过松弛版本获得高效且实验表现优良的启发式。

Abstract: The Vehicle Routing Problem with Floating Targets (VRPFT) is a generalization of the capacitated vehicle routing problem where targets may actively move to locations ''convenient'' for the vehicles. In particular, each target may leave its home location at a bounded speed. A solution to the VRPFT is a sequence of targets for each vehicle to visit and a meeting point in space-time for each target. In this paper, we solve the VRPFT exactly with a branch-price-and-cut algorithm. Our key contribution is a dominance criterion for the pricing labeling algorithm that utilizes closed-form lower and upper bounds on a vehicle's trajectory cost given a fixed sequence of targets. We prove that the criterion preserves optimality, and the algorithm has smaller computation time than the exact dynamic program of prior work on identical hardware. We also provide a relaxed criterion that prunes more aggressively; the resulting heuristic is faster than our exact algorithm and matched every certified optimum on the benchmark.

</details>


### [42] [SkySearcher: High-Altitude Onboard VLM Reasoning for Aerial Semantic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34485)
*Giacomo Vianello,David Morilla-Cabello,Juan Dendarieta Serrano,Ruggero Carli,Eduardo Montijano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkySearcher 是一种面向大型室外场景的空中语义搜索导航方法，通过机载视觉语言模型推理与轻量概率表示相结合来指导信息驱动的探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机开放词汇搜索系统多沿用低空策略，依赖密集三维度量语义重建或云端大模型，需要大量存储或持续联网，难以适应高空大范围场景。

Method: 集成机载 VLM 推理与轻量概率表示：VLM 在高空提取目标或其位置线索的语义信息，利用相机视锥而非有限深度传感器将信息投影至地面，以利用高空大视场；随后将语义线索存在与否的信念融入概率地图，维护任务驱动记忆并引导探索至语义潜力区域。

Result: 在公开基准的空中物体目标导航仿真中，SkySearcher 在成功率、飞行时间和导航精度上显著优于最先进基线；全机载部署实验验证了方法的适用性。

Conclusion: SkySearcher 为大型室外场景提供了一种高效、可全机载运行的空中语义搜索导航方案，克服了现有低空策略对密集重建或云端依赖的局限。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have improved semantic and visual reasoning, enabling UAVs to search for open-vocabulary queries in unstructured environments at high altitude. However, existing systems mainly follow low-altitude strategies, focusing on close-range iterative exploration, requiring storage-intensive dense 3D metric-semantic reconstructions or relying on large cloud-serviced VLMs assuming constant internet access. We propose SkySearcher, an aerial semantic search navigation method designed for large outdoor scenes. SkySearcher integrates onboard VLM reasoning with a lightweight probabilistic representation that guides an information-driven exploration. The VLM extracts semantic information at high altitude about the target or cues informing about its location. This information is projected to the ground using the camera frustum instead of limited-depth sensors, actively exploiting the expansive field of view offered by elevated sensing. The information regarding the presence or absence of semantic cues is then integrated into a probabilistic map by modeling the spatial belief of the query, maintaining a task-driven memory for the mission and guiding the exploration towards semantically promising regions. Extensive simulations for Aerial Object Goal Navigation on public benchmarks demonstrate that SkySearcher significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in success rate, flight time, and navigation precision, while a deployment of SkySercher running fully onboard a drone demonstrates the applicability of our approach.

</details>


### [43] [Least Restrictive Halfplane Control Barrier Functions for an Autonomous Hydrofoil Boat with Moving Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34463)
*Mattias Trende,Petter Ögren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种针对Dubins车动力学系统的半平面控制屏障函数（CBF）方法，通过优化半平面方向和多半平面近似，降低保守性并减少目标到达延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性系统在输入约束和复杂环境中难以找到有效的CBF；将障碍物表示为支撑半平面可解析推导CBF，但半平面CBF可能高度保守，过估障碍物并限制运动。

Method: 为Dubins车动力学系统设计半平面CBF；优化单个障碍物支撑半平面的方向以找到最接近期望的安全控制输入；使用多个半平面近似复杂几何形状，并推导处理半平面交集的规则。

Result: 对比仿真表明，该方法相比现有CBF技术降低了保守性，实现更紧的障碍物间距和更快的目标到达，延迟减少超过33%；在自主水翼船上的实验验证了实际可行性。

Conclusion: 通过优化半平面方向和多半平面近似，可有效降低半平面CBF的保守性，提升运动效率，并成功应用于实际系统。

Abstract: Finding valid Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) is difficult for non-linear systems subject to actuation constraints and moving in complex environments. One way to enable analytically derived CBFs is to represent obstacles as supporting halfplanes, allowing the agent to move safely on one side. In this paper, we propose such a CBF for an agent with Dubins car dynamics. A potential drawback of halfplane CBFs is that they can be highly conservative, overestimating the obstacles and thereby restricting the agent's motion. To address this problem, we make the CBF halfplanes less restrictive as follows. First, the orientation of the supporting halfplane for a single obstacle is optimised to find a safe control input as close as possible to the desired one. Second, we use multiple halfplanes to better approximate complex geometries, and derive rules for handling halfplane intersections. Comparative simulations show that our method reduces conservatism compared to existing CBF techniques, enabling tighter obstacle clearance and faster goal arrival, with delays reduced by more than 33%. Finally, experiments on an autonomous hydrofoil boat demonstrate successful real-world implementation.

</details>


### [44] [DynaRC: Dynamics-Robust 4-D Radar Relocalization with Rate-Adaptive Sequence Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34453)
*Chuan Cao,Xingyi Li,Tianchen Deng,Jingchuan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出DynaRC框架，利用多普勒证据分离静态回波并掩蔽运动影响单元，结合单帧检索与序列重定位，在动态场景下实现4D毫米波雷达地点匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D毫米波雷达能测径向速度且不受恶劣天气影响，但方向性回波稀疏、运动物体和遮挡导致地点匹配歧义。

Method: 构建极坐标描述子，用多普勒证据分离静态回波并掩蔽受运动影响的单元，以中值环键检索候选；序列变体Seq_DynaRC采用端点锚定的速率对齐和候选索引投票，配合有序验证，在无需里程计或点云累积的情况下关联最新扫描。

Result: 在四组室内外地图—查询对中，Seq_DynaRC在2米内条件定位成功率最高；校园道路重访场景成功率91.8%，在保留各方法全部已发表输出时比描述子匹配的SeqSLAM高55.0个百分点，输出可用性另行报告。

Conclusion: DynaRC与Seq_DynaRC能有效应对动态环境和不等推进条件下的4D雷达地点匹配，代码及室内外动态4D雷达数据已公开。

Abstract: Four-dimensional millimeter-wave radar measures radial velocity within a single scan and remains resilient under adverse weather and visibility. Yet sparse directional returns, moving objects and occlusion make place matching ambiguous. We propose DynaRC, a dynamics-robust framework encompassing single-frame retrieval and sequence relocalization. Doppler evidence separates static returns and masks motion-affected cells in a polar descriptor; median ring keys retrieve candidates. Its sequence variant, Seq_DynaRC, couples endpoint-anchored rate alignment and candidate-index voting with ordered verification to associate the latest scan under unequal map/query advancement, without odometry or cloud accumulation. Across four indoor and outdoor map--query pairs, Seq_DynaRC achieves the highest conditional localization success within 2m among tested methods. On a campus-road revisit under changing traffic, success reaches 91.8%, exceeding descriptor-matched SeqSLAM by 55.0 percentage points when each method retains all its published outputs; output availability is reported separately. Code and indoor/outdoor dynamic 4D radar data are available.

</details>


### [45] [HoloBrain-0.5 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34449)
*Xuewu Lin,Jiahao Wang,Jiawei Li,Mengdi Li,Zhengmao Sun,Yiwei Jin,Hongyu Xie,Zhengyang Feng,Kun Wu,Zihao Chen,Lianzhou Zhang,Lichao Huang,Zhizhong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HoloBrain-0.5 是一族用于跨机器人本体的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过共享动作空间和标准化运动学表示实现多本体操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型难以泛化到不同机器人本体，需要统一的动作空间和运动学表示来支持跨本体策略学习。

Method: 标准化 URDF 中的关节轴、坐标系和工具中心点，将运动学图编码为共享 3D 表示；提出连续扩散策略和基于残差 FSQ 与延迟自回归的离散策略，并在超 1 万小时真实机器人、仿真和人类操作数据（15 类本体）上预训练。

Result: 离散策略在六个闭环仿真中收敛更快、平均成功率更高（56.77% vs 49.71%）；真实七任务零样本平均进度 87.76%；后训练后连续策略在七任务上达 94.52%（对比 π0.5 的 86.25%），双臂 11 任务达 79.07%（对比 69.41%）；离散初始化连续策略零样本迁移五任务至 Piper-X 平均 63.28%；目标机器人演示后 Franka Panda 达 89.2%，移动双臂平台达 92.94%；仅少量 G1 演示后训练，三个人形任务平均进度 70.24%（π0.5 为 11.00%）。

Conclusion: HoloBrain-0.5 通过共享动作空间和标准化运动学表示有效实现跨本体操作策略，离散策略预训练效率高，连续策略后训练性能强，并展现出优异的零样本迁移和少样本适应能力。

Abstract: We present HoloBrain-0.5, a family of vision-language-action (VLA) models for manipulation across robot embodiments. The policies plan in a shared action space using robot state, kinematic structure, and calibrated camera observations. We standardize joint axes, coordinate frames, and tool-center points in robot descriptions (URDFs) and encode their kinematic graphs in a shared 3D representation. HoloBrain-0.5-Continuous generates action chunks by diffusion, while HoloBrain-0.5-Discrete uses residual finite scalar quantization (FSQ) and delayed autoregression. We pretrain both policies on over 10,000 hours of real-robot, simulation, and human-manipulation data spanning 15 embodiment categories. Across six closed-loop simulation settings, the discrete policy converges faster and reaches a higher mean success rate than the continuous policy (56.77% vs. 49.71%). On seven real-robot tasks, it achieves a mean progress score of 87.76% without task-specific post-training. After post-training, the continuous policy outperforms $\pi_{0.5}$ on the seven-task benchmark (94.52% vs. 86.25%) and an 11-task dual-arm benchmark (79.07% vs. 69.41%). The discrete-initialized continuous policy transfers five tasks zero-shot from Piper to Piper-X, scoring 63.28% on average. With target-robot demonstrations, the continuous policy scores 89.2% on Franka Panda and 92.94% on a mobile dual-arm platform. Although pretraining includes no Unitree G1 data, post-training with limited G1 demonstrations yields a mean progress score of 70.24% across three humanoid tasks, compared with 11.00% for $\pi_{0.5}$.

</details>


### [46] [Calibration of Neuromusculoskeletal Models Using Differentiable Physics: Recommendations for Improving Model Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34428)
*Philip Sanderink,Yingfan Zhou,Shuzhen Luo,Cheng Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究在同一可微上肢模型上系统比较了肌肉协同、可学习骨骼参数、包裹几何优化和约束力矩等模块对校准精度的影响，发现肌肉协同外推和骨骼动力学参数优化分别将力矩损失平均降低23.6%和10.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微物理为神经肌肉骨骼模型校准带来新范式，但各校准模块此前仅在不同研究中被单独评估，缺乏在同一模型和同一基准下的直接比较，难以确定最优校准流程。

Method: 构建含3自由度肩、2自由度肘及11块浅层和5块深层肌肉的可微上肢模型，设置16种校准配置（单独或组合使用肌肉协同、可学习骨骼参数、包裹几何优化、约束力矩），在4名受试者的9种测试动作上以正逆动力学计算的两关节力矩误差作为评价指标进行比较。

Result: 采用肌肉协同外推估计深层肌肉活动可使力矩损失平均降低23.6%，骨骼动力学参数优化可使力矩损失平均降低10.9%；其余模块的效果及交互作用也在16种配置中得到比较。

Conclusion: 肌肉协同外推与骨骼动力学参数优化均能显著提升可微NMS模型校准精度，建议将二者纳入校准流程。

Abstract: Differentiable physics has shown potential to usher in a new paradigm in human neuromusculoskeletal (NMS) model calibration. Early results from pioneering works have demonstrated that auto-differentiable NMS models can greatly reduce NMS model calibration time while achieving comparable accuracy compared to traditional calibration methods. The efficient gradient-based optimizers have enabled calibration of increasingly sophisticated human models with a large number of parameters, which seek to narrow the gap between simulation and reality. Several modules have been evaluated separately in different studies, but have never been directly compared within the same study. In this work, these modules are compared on the same differentiable upper limb model with a 3-DoF shoulder, a 2-DoF elbow, and 11 superficial and 5 deep muscles in the calibration to find an optimal pipeline for improving model accuracy. The considered modules include muscle synergy, learnable bone parameters, optimizable geometry of wrap objects, and constraint torque of the differentiable arm model. To evaluate the effect and interplay between the modules on calibration accuracy, one or more modules were used in each of 16 calibration configurations, which were compared in terms of the error between two joint torques calculated by the forward and inverse dynamics with the calibrated model on 9 distinctive testing movements across 4 subjects. Results showed that torque loss was reduced by 23.6% on average using muscle synergy extrapolation to estimate deep muscle activities, and by 10.9% on average through bone dynamic parameter optimization, which are both recommended to be incorporated in the calibration.

</details>


### [47] [CHASE: Curiosity-Guided Skill Acquisition for Embodied Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34416)
*Zixuan Wang,Wei Zhang,Chenlei Zhu,Naiyu Chen,Xihua Wang,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CHASE框架，利用好奇心驱动选择能解决任务不确定性并扩展可执行能力的调查，在任务执行中同时进行假设检验与技能获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境中，机器人既面临不确定性又缺乏完整技能，需要一种机制能在执行任务时主动探索并学习缺失技能。

Method: CHASE包含四个模块，连接动态场景理解、假设驱动规划、按需技能获取和验证执行；规划器权衡信息价值、技能复用与获取/执行成本；缺失技能触发有界探索、从验证片段学习及留出准入，并分别校验物理效果与证据。

Result: 仿真与真实机器人实验表明，在信息与能力双重缺口下任务成功率达50-80%，证据解决率达70-90%。

Conclusion: 将好奇心操作化为选择能同时解决不确定性和获取技能的研究，可有效闭合“消解不确定性”与“获取能力”之间的循环，提升机器人在不完整技能下的任务执行表现。

Abstract: Robots executing tasks in partially observed environments must resolve uncertainty while operating with incomplete skills. Inspired by human curiosity, we investigate how robots can formulate task-relevant hypotheses, seek evidence, and acquire missing skills during task execution. We propose CHASE, a Curiosity-guided Harness for Acquiring Skills from Evidence, which operationalizes curiosity as selecting investigations that resolve task uncertainty and expand executable capabilities. Four modules connect dynamic scene understanding, hypothesis-driven planning, on-demand skill acquisition, and verified execution. The planner balances information value, skill reuse, and acquisition/execution cost. Missing skills trigger bounded exploration, learning from verified segments, and held-out admission during acquisition; separate checks verify physical effects and acquired evidence. Newly admitted skills expand the executable investigation set, closing the loop between resolving uncertainty and acquiring capabilities. Simulation and real-robot experiments evaluate investigation, skill acquisition, and task execution. CHASE achieves 50-80% task success under combined information and capability gaps, with 70-90% evidence resolution.

</details>


### [48] [Embodied Lower-limb Exoskeleton Assistance: Survey, Challenges, and Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34408)
*Xiao-Yin Liu,Guotao Li,Robert Riener,Zeng-Guang Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述从具身视角定义下肢外骨骼，按结构自适应、任务适应、用户特定适应和控制自调节四个维度梳理文献，指出现有研究局限并展望未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年AI使下肢外骨骼能适应复杂任务和多样用户，但现有综述多围绕机械设计、控制策略或单一AI算法组织，缺乏对具身下肢外骨骼及其使能技术的系统覆盖。

Method: 定义具身下肢外骨骼，围绕结构自适应、任务适应、用户特定适应和控制自调节四个维度对先进与代表性研究进行分类讨论，分析轻量化柔顺设计、数据采集与融合、复杂策略优化、鲁棒柔顺控制等挑战。

Result: 识别了当前研究的主要局限，并沿四个维度提出未来方向；从具身视角构建了机械结构、传感与控制之间的闭环关系框架，用于分析外骨骼性能与人机交互研究。

Conclusion: 所提方向指向轻量化、舒适且实用的物理辅助，该闭环框架为下肢外骨骼性能分析和人机交互研究提供指导。

Abstract: Over the past decade, artificial intelligence (AI) has enabled lower-limb exoskeletons to adapt to complex tasks and assist diverse users. Existing reviews, however, are organized around mechanical design, control strategies, or individual AI algorithms, and provide little systematic coverage of embodied lower-limb exoskeletons and their enabling technologies. This review defines embodied lower-limb exoskeletons and organizes the literature around four dimensions: structural self-adaptation, task adaptation, user-specific adaptation, and control self-regulation. In each dimension, state-of-the-art and representative studies are classified and discussed in terms of the associated challenges, including lightweight and compliant design, data acquisition and fusion, optimization of complex policies, and robust, compliant control. The review then identifies the principal limitations of current research and outlines future directions along these dimensions. From an embodied perspective, this review sets out the closed-loop relationship among mechanical structure, sensing, and control as a framework for analyzing lower-limb exoskeleton performance and for research on human-robot interaction. The directions it identifies point toward lightweight, comfortable, and practical physical assistance.

</details>


### [49] [Recoverability policy editing for closed-loop safety in autonomous driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34403)
*Yang Guan,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种状态级策略编辑技术，使神经网络自动驾驶模型在可恢复扰动下保证闭环安全，并复用其原有驾驶能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络自动驾驶模型缺乏安全保障，需要在不重新训练模型的情况下保证闭环安全。

Method: 引入策略可恢复性概念，识别原策略下长期不安全但可恢复的状态，仅在该区域内选择性编辑策略输出；车辆进入该区域时修正动作，恢复后回到原策略。

Result: 在nuPlan城市驾驶场景的闭环仿真中，原策略下的安全事件减少48%，同时基本保持驾驶性能。

Conclusion: 状态级策略编辑能够在复用现有驾驶能力的同时，为可恢复扰动提供闭环安全保证。

Abstract: Autonomous driving powered by neural network models lacks safety guarantees. We propose a state-level policy editing technique that guarantees closed-loop safety under recoverable disturbances while reusing the model's existing driving capabilities. Closed-loop safety means that the vehicle remains free of collisions at all future times. We introduce the concept of policy recoverability to characterize whether a vehicle can safely return to the original policy's feasible set in finite time. Our method identifies states that are unsafe in the long term under the original policy but recoverable, and selectively edits policy outputs within this region. The vehicle corrects its actions upon entering this region and resumes the original policy upon recovery. Closed-loop simulations on nuPlan urban driving scenarios show a 48% reduction in safety incidents under the original policy while largely preserving its driving performance.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Natural-Language Instructions: Temporal Logic as a Task Language for VLA Models with Visual-Logical Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34352)
*Zezhi Liu,Jingwen Sun,Hongjin Chen,Shaojie Ren,Peiyan Tu,Xin Jin,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TL-VLA用时间逻辑(TL)替代自然语言作为任务指令，通过类型化图编码器和视觉-逻辑融合实现动作条件化，并在多个机器人基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务包含对象关系、逻辑依赖和时间约束，自然语言指令难以显式表达这些时空结构，而TL虽被规划和控制领域常用，却未被视觉-语言-动作模型直接利用。

Method: 提出TL-VLA框架：以TL作为任务规范，采用类型化图编码器与视觉-逻辑融合将连续动作条件化于任务规范与当前观测；并设计TL感知的机器人预训练，含冻结语言教师对齐语义、结构读出编码TL语法、自回归动作预测连接图表示与行为；下游策略继承预训练骨干和TL编码器，学习新的融合与动作模块。

Result: CALVIN ABC→D上平均链长3.800、五任务成功率62.3%（FAST初始化3.578/54.5%，Qwen+NL 3.485）；LIBERO-10成功率由91.2%提升至95.8%；LIBERO-Mem无观测历史下达65.0%（Qwen+NL 45.0%）；Panda规划规范上Success@256为59.6%（Qwen+TL 48.4%，Qwen+NL 37.0%）。

Conclusion: 结果支持显式逻辑任务条件化及可迁移的TL感知表示对机器人动作学习的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic tasks combine object relations, logical dependencies, and temporal requirements, which robot planning and industrial control commonly specify in temporal logic (TL). We introduce TL-VLA, a vision--language--action framework that replaces natural-language task instructions with temporal logic, making this spatiotemporal structure explicit. A typed graph encoder and visual--logical fusion condition continuous actions on the task specification and the current observations. To prepare a general vision--language backbone for this interface, we develop TL-aware robot pretraining with separate semantic and structural readouts: a frozen language teacher aligns task semantics, the structural readout encodes TL syntax to support complex, long-horizon tasks, and autoregressive action prediction connects the graph representation to robot behavior. Downstream policies inherit the pretrained backbone and TL encoder and learn new fusion and action modules. On CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, our TL-aware initializer reaches \textbf{3.800} average chain length and \textbf{62.3\%} five-task success, compared with 3.578 and 54.5\% for a FAST initializer pretrained on the same data and 3.485 average chain length for a Qwen+NL baseline. On LIBERO-10, TL-aware initialization improves success from \textbf{91.2\% to 95.8\%}. On LIBERO-Mem, our policy reaches \textbf{65.0\%} success without observation history, compared with 45.0\% for a Qwen+NL reference. On held-out Panda planning specifications, it reaches \textbf{59.6\%} at Success@256 over three generation seeds, versus 48.4\% for Qwen+TL and 37.0\% for Qwen+NL. These results support explicit logical task conditioning and transferable TL-aware representations for robot action learning.

</details>


### [51] [LUDIUS: A Benchmark for Embodied Social Intelligence in Everyday Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34328)
*Chaewon Kim,Sangheon Lee,Seyeon Choi,Kangwook Lee,Yea Seul Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LUDIUS基准，评估视觉语言模型在机器人场景中通过言语和行动展现的社会智能，涵盖15项社会智能技能、6个版本（3个全技能家庭版和3个专题版），并发现模型在推理他人既往感知、平衡利益冲突、在安全约束下满足请求方面表现较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人社会智能要求从参与者视角解读情境并据此决定何时及如何行动，但现有视觉语言模型在此类判断上的表现缺乏系统评估，因此需要一个基准来测试模型通过机器人言语响应和行动体现的社会判断能力。

Method: 构建LUDIUS基准，围绕来自社会心理学、社会学和认知科学的15项社会智能技能设计；包含6个版本，每个版本为覆盖模拟一周的episode集合，其中3个版本在不同家庭中覆盖全部15项技能，另3个分别聚焦多人交互、安全和情境特定规范；使用统一评估框架评测前沿模型及在生成的社会交互数据上微调的小模型，框架提供机器人相机视角、居民反应、检索相关交互历史，并将模型工具调用转换为机器人言语响应和行动。

Result: 模型在需要推理居民此前看到或听到的内容、协调相互竞争的利益、或在安全约束和家庭规则内完成请求时，表现往往更差。

Conclusion: LUDIUS的设计流程可将社会智能技能转化为情境特定判断和可观察的机器人行为，从而为构建社会智能基准提供可推广的方法。

Abstract: Social intelligence in a robot requires interpreting a situation from the perspectives of the people involved and using that understanding to decide when and how to act. We introduce LUDIUS, a benchmark for evaluating how vision-language models exercise this judgment through a robot's verbal responses and actions, organized around 15 social intelligence skills drawn from social psychology, sociology, and cognitive science. LUDIUS comprises six editions, each a collection of episodes spanning one simulated week. Three editions each cover all 15 skills in a different household. The other three focus on interactions involving multiple people, safety, and context-specific norms, respectively. We evaluate frontier models and a smaller model finetuned on social-interaction data we generated, using a common evaluation harness. The harness supplies each model with the robot's camera views and residents' reactions, retrieves relevant interaction history, and converts the model's tool calls into robot verbal responses and actions. Models tend to perform worse when they must reason about what a resident saw or heard earlier, reconcile competing interests, or fulfill a request within safety constraints and household rules. Beyond the home setting, LUDIUS's design pipeline offers a way to construct social intelligence benchmarks by translating social intelligence skills into context-specific judgments and observable robot behavior.

</details>


### [52] [Co-World Model for Human-Video-to-Robot Transfer via Co-Constructed Shared Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34302)
*Xuetao Li,Xuan Jiang,Chen Liu,Zhongrui Rao,Yu Ling,Hui Wang,Sheng Liu,Miao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Co-World Model（CoWM），通过对齐跨具身的潜在动作实现从单次人类演示到机器人的零样本技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频丰富廉价而机器人轨迹稀缺昂贵，现有方法或重定向人类几何结构、或仅匹配静态视觉目标，难以应对跨具身动力学不匹配与动作执行落地问题。

Method: 构建由配对但不同步的人-机器人数据流联合优化的预测框架：将观测解耦为共享物体关系状态与具身特定上下文；用effect-swap与动作敏感性目标对齐潜在动作；采用相位感知滚动时域控制器估计演示进度，并将推断的人类动作先验投影到机器人动作支撑密度上，转化为可执行指令。

Result: 在仿真及两个真实机器人平台上的实验验证了CoWM的有效性、跨平台泛化能力及各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: CoWM通过效果驱动的潜在动作对齐与落地执行控制，实现了单次人类演示的零样本跨具身技能迁移。

Abstract: Human videos are abundant and cheap to obtain, whereas robot trajectories remain scarce and costly, making zero-shot skill transfer from a single human demonstration an appealing route to scalable robot learning. Existing methods, however, either retarget human-specific geometry or match only static visual goals, and thus stumble over the cross-embodiment dynamics mismatch and the ungrounded action execution. We present the Co-World Model (CoWM), a predictive framework jointly optimized from paired yet unsynchronized human and robot streams. To bridge the cross-embodiment dynamics gap, CoWM disentangles each observation into a shared object-relation state and embodiment-specific context. It aligns latent actions across embodiments using effect-swap and action-sensitivity objectives,such that actions are matched by their predicted effects on the manipulated objects rather than by embodimentdependent kinematics. For grounded execution, a phase-aware receding-horizon controller estimates demonstration progress, projects the inferred human action prior onto the robot action-support density, and translates the resulting latent dynamics into executable robot commands. Extensive experiments in simulation and on two physical robot platforms demonstrate the effectiveness of CoWM, its cross-platform generalizability, and the contribution of each component. See the demo video in the supporting information for details.

</details>


### [53] [BOT-Scale: A Traceable Evaluation Methodology for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34297)
*Yunfei Xue,Kemi Chen,Weiling Chen,Wei Gao,Tiesong Zhao,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BOT-Scale方法，将操作得分定义为行为因子记录上的结果条件惩罚之和，使分数与解释共享同一计算，实现可追溯评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作评估器将偏差压缩为单一分数或附加独立解释，分数无法说明依据和修复方向。

Method: 借鉴医学行为评估量表，将十二个候选因子组织为四层评估层，每层因子绑定可观察证据和判定准则；通过参考校准为每个因子设定惩罚，并用闭式规则聚合为带因子贡献的分数；基于数据筛选保留的七个因子开发自动评分系统BOTScorer。

Result: 提供了BOT-Events数据集，包含八位评分者的质量分与因子事件标注，以及可追溯、可解释的得分。

Conclusion: 将操作分数定义为可追溯的因子级惩罚聚合，可使评分与解释一体化，每个因子级扣分对应可检查的偏差。

Abstract: A successful manipulation rollout can still involve a mid-task object drop or a placement deviation. Existing evaluators reduce such deviations to a single score or attach explanations computed separately; the score itself explains neither its basis nor what to fix. We show that a manipulation score becomes traceable when defined as the sum of outcome-conditioned penalties over retained behavioral-factor records. Explanation and score share the same computation, so each factor-level deduction names an inspectable deviation. BOT-Scale applies this principle by adapting behavioral assessment scales from medicine. It organizes twelve candidate factors into four assessment layers; each factor is tied to observable evidence and an explicit decision criterion. Reference calibration fixes a penalty per factor, and a closed-form rule aggregates the factor records into a score with per-factor contributions. We developed BOTScorer, an automatic scoring system based on the seven factors retained by data-driven screening, to validate this methodology. We provide BOT-Events, a dataset with eight-rater quality scores and factor-event annotations, alongside the traceable, interpretable score. The code will be released publicly.

</details>


### [54] [Rehearse in 3D, Render in 2D: Physically Consistent and Editable Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34288)
*Guangyu Liu,Hanzhang Wang,Huijia Zhang,Hanbei Zhan,Linjie Hu,Bin Xiao,Xuan Zhou,Dong Zhang,Hanwei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RehearsalWorld，一种基于3D交互排练的可编辑机器人世界模型，通过解耦物理响应与视觉合成实现物理一致且可编辑的交互视频预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身世界模型仅做显式3D运动预测，无法保证物体结构与接触关系不丢失；3D表征可作为施加结构与物理约束的显式状态空间。

Method: 采用3D交互排练（3DIR）：交互一致性排练（ICR）以预训练3D点流模型为先验，经响应感知物体分组、持久抓握记忆与接触结构精修优化轨迹；排练对齐视频生成（RAVG）以排练的几何投影作时间条件微调视频生成器。

Result: 在真实DROID抓取序列上相较PointWorld，对可变形、铰接与刚性物体的夹爪相对偏移降低约79%至84%，并降低参考运动误差；视频预测PSNR为23.92 dB，FVD为149。

Conclusion: 将物理响应与视觉合成解耦，使模型在保持场景稳定、物体结构与抓握附着的同时，支持材质属性与相机视角的独立编辑。

Abstract: Embodied world models aim to predict future interactions that are both visually realistic and physically consistent. Yet explicit 3D motion prediction alone does not guarantee that object structure and contact relationships are preserved. Beyond motion prediction, 3D representations provide an explicit state space for imposing structural and physical constraints. Building on this insight, we introduce RehearsalWorld, an editable robot world model driven by 3D Interaction Rehearsal (3DIR). Given an initial RGB-D observation and robot actions, Interaction-Consistent Rehearsal (ICR) uses a pretrained 3D point-flow model as a motion prior. It refines the predicted trajectories through response-aware object grouping, persistent grasp memory, and contact structure refiner, preserving scene stability, object structure, and grasp attachments. Rehearsal-Aligned Video Generation (RAVG) then uses geometric projections of the rehearsal as temporal conditioning. We fine-tune a video generator on projection-video pairs to synthesize realistic interaction videos. Thanks to the decoupling of physical response from visual synthesis, RehearsalWorld also supports independent editing of material properties and camera viewpoints. Compared with PointWorld on real DROID grasping sequences, RehearsalWorld reduces gripper-relative offset by approximately 79% to 84% across deformable, articulated, and rigid objects and lowers reference motion error. For video prediction, it achieves a PSNR of 23.92 dB and an FVD of 149.

</details>


### [55] [DataForgeVLA: Fully Synthetic Manipulation Demonstrations for Vision-Language-Action Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34287)
*Sergei Kurchev,Iaroslav Kolomiets,Filipp Maksimov,Elizaveta Semenyakina,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Artem Lykov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文发布了一个由真实种子数据生成的340条全合成操作演示数据集，用于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）训练，并初步验证了仅用合成数据微调策略的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作采集真实操作演示成本高，作者希望探索无需额外遥操作、仅由真实种子数据生成合成演示来训练VLA策略的可行性。

Method: 构建包含32340帧、8类物体、同步RGB与动作的LeRobot v2.1数据集；用LoRA微调的Cosmos-3生成操作视频，再由RoboHaMeR从RGB恢复机器人轨迹；经质量筛选保留300/340条用于策略微调，并让预训练GR00T N1.6策略仅在该300条上微调。

Result: 仅用300条合成数据微调的策略在Oreo包装任务真实抓取放置中达到16/20（80%）成功率；同一协议下，用105条真实遥操作数据微调的策略为18/20（90%）。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明合成演示可用于VLA策略微调，但结果仅基于单一物体和每策略20次试验，应视为可行性初步证据而非普遍性能结论。

Abstract: We present a dataset of 340 fully synthetic manipulation demonstrations generated from real seed data for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) training. The corpus contains 32,340 frames with synchronized RGB and actions across eight object categories, represented as LeRobot v2.1 episodes with H.264 video, per-frame robot states and pose-valued action labels, language task annotations, summary statistics and quality decisions. Each synthetic demonstration is produced without additional teleoperation: a LoRA-fine-tuned Cosmos-3 model generates a manipulation video, and RoboHaMeR recovers the corresponding robot trajectory from RGB. Quality screening accepted 300 of 340 episodes for policy fine-tuning. As preliminary evidence of feasibility, a pretrained GR00T N1.6 policy fine-tuned exclusively on the 300 accepted episodes reaches 16/20 (80%) real-world pick-and-place success on the Oreo-package task. A policy fine-tuned on 105 real teleoperated episodes achieved 18/20 (90%) under the same protocol. These results are based on one object and 20 trials per policy and are therefore reported as preliminary evidence rather than a general performance claim.

</details>


### [56] [Escapingthe instruction blackhole of vision-language-action policies via optimal-transport wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34280)
*Xuetao Li,Chen Liu,Jing Wu,Sheng Liu,Miao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出VLA策略存在“指令黑洞”现象：一旦开始执行任务，新指令会被忽略，机器人继续旧任务或冻结；提出免训练插件Wormhole，通过最优传输选择新任务的接近状态并限速迁移机器人，再由原策略接管，在LIBERO上将切换成功率从最高1.2%提升至93.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类会在机器人执行过程中改变意图，但现有VLA策略被训练为遵循固定指令，无法在执行中响应新指令，形成指令黑洞；静态模仿学习无法区分“会倾听”与“失聪”的策略，因为执行状态一旦暴露任务，策略行为即固定。

Method: 用传输比和事件视界量化指令黑洞；证明其为静态模仿学习的盲点；提出Wormhole：免训练即插即用，通过最优传输选择新任务的接近阶段状态，以限速遍历将机器人迁移至该状态，随后由未修改的原策略继续执行。

Result: 在LIBERO上，Wormhole将π和π0.5的已承诺切换成功率从至多1.2%提升至93.1%，达到或超过脚本化“撤退并重启”方法，同时动作不连续性仅为其五分之一。

Conclusion: Wormhole通过重定位机器人而非重新下达指令，有效解决了VLA策略在执行中无法响应新指令的问题，且无需额外训练，显著提升任务切换成功率并降低动作突变。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies are trained to follow a fixed instruction, yet people change their minds while the robot acts. We identify the instruction black hole: once a VLA policy commits to a task, new instructions are absorbed and the robot completes the old task or freezes. We quantify it with a transmission ratio and an event horizon and prove it is a blind spot of static imitation, which cannot distinguish a policy that listens from a deaf one once the execution state reveals the task. We propose Wormhole, a training-free plug-in that relocates the robot instead of re-instructing it: optimal transport selects an approach-phase state of the new task, a rate-limited traversal carries the robot there, and the unmodified policy takes over. On LIBERO,Wormhole raises committed-switch success of pi to and pi0.5 from at most 1.2% to 93.1%, matching or exceeding scripted retreat-and-restart with one fifth of its action discontinuity.

</details>


### [57] [CamAct: Learning Robotic Manipulation from In-the-Wild Videos via Camera Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34257)
*Haoming Gu,Yunpeng Qing,Zhi Hou,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CamAct方法，利用无操作演示的野外视频中的相机运动作为额外运动监督，与机器人演示结合微调VLA策略，提升操作任务成功率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据采集成本高，现有利用人类视频的工作主要依赖人物-物体交互演示，本文探究不含操作演示的野外视频是否也能为机器人操作学习提供有效监督。

Method: 从野外视频中恢复相机运动，将其作为运动监督信号，与机器人演示一起融入VLA策略的微调过程，构建CamAct。

Result: 在LIBERO上成功率从96.40%提升至98.10%，在RoboCasa 365上从22.96%提升至29.32%；在LIBERO-Plus的分布外泛化及真实世界操作任务中表现良好。

Conclusion: 即使视频不含物体操作演示，其相机运动信息也能作为互补监督信号，扩展人类视频在机器人操作学习中的用途并提升策略性能与泛化性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models benefit from large, diverse datasets for robotic manipulation, yet collecting robot demonstrations remains costly. Recent work has explored egocentric videos as a source of supervision, primarily drawing on demonstrations of human-object interaction. We show that in-the-wild videos can improve robotic manipulation even when they contain no demonstrations of object manipulation. Specifically, camera motion recovered from these videos provides an additional source of motion supervision that complements robot demonstrations. Building on this finding, we introduce CamAct, which incorporates this camera-motion supervision into VLA policies alongside robot demonstrations, extending the use of human videos for manipulation learning beyond object interactions. Experiments show that incorporating this video supervision during fine-tuning improves success rates from 96.40% to 98.10% on LIBERO and from 22.96% to 29.32% on RoboCasa 365. The resulting policy also demonstrates strong results in out-of-distribution generalization on LIBERO-Plus and real-world manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [58] [Autonomous Vineyard Exploration using LiDAR Sensing: A Field Evaluation on Legged-Wheeled Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34246)
*Spencer Sweeney,Hongbo Li,Michael Ziegltrum,Dimitra Koumoutsou,Andromachi Maria Delfaki,Taskin Padir,Dimitrios Kanoulas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在Unitree Go2轮式四足机器人上部署基于LiDAR的自主探索系统，利用迭代RANSAC直线检测识别葡萄园行并配合有限状态导航策略，在Denbies葡萄园完成370米无干预上下行自主循行，同时分析了稀疏植被下直线检测不稳定与空间滤波权衡等失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 葡萄园自主导航面临地形不平、植被稀疏以及行间几何高度重复等挑战，需要评估LiDAR感知与导航策略在实际商用葡萄园环境中的可行性与局限。

Method: 采用基于迭代RANSAC的直线检测流水线识别葡萄园行，并结合有限状态导航策略控制行的选择与遍历；系统部署于Unitree Go2轮式四足机器人，在英国Denbies葡萄园开展实地评估并分析失效模式。

Result: 系统能够持续自主循行，完成370米上下行往返遍历且无需人工干预；但在稀疏植被条件下出现直线检测不稳定的问题，并需权衡空间滤波带来的影响。

Conclusion: RANSAC可提供稳健的几何基元，但在商用葡萄园环境中实现可靠的换行过渡仍需引入额外的时间滤波与自适应感知能力。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in vineyards presents significant challenges due to uneven terrain, sparse vegetation, and the repetitive geometric nature of vineyard rows. This paper evaluates a LiDAR-based autonomous exploration system deployed on a Unitree Go2 Wheeled quadruped robot. Our approach utilizes an iterative RANSAC-based line detection pipeline to identify vineyard rows and a finite-state navigation strategy to govern row selection and traversal. Field evaluations conducted at Denbies Vineyard (UK) demonstrate the system's ability to perform sustained autonomous row following, completing a 370m up-and-down traversal without intervention. We analyze the failure modes encountered, specifically focusing on line detection instability in sparse vegetation and the trade-offs of spatial filtering. The results suggest that, while RANSAC provides a robust geometric primitive, additional temporal filtering and adaptive perception are required for reliable lane-to-lane transitions in commercial vineyard environments. A video of the experimental results is available at: this http URL

</details>


### [59] [Ground Your Steps: Optimizing Sampling Schedules in Flow Matching Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34148)
*Licheng Yang,Yonghao He,Lingfeng Qian,Xin Shi,Wei Sui,Hu Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的采样调度优化方法GYS，通过匹配生成与演示动作块分布来提升流匹配机器人策略在固定NFE下的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配策略在固定神经函数评估预算（NFE）下，推理积分结果依赖采样调度，优化调度可使生成动作分布更接近演示分布并提升任务成功率。

Method: GYS对每条机械臂关节的动作序列做截断分数阶差分，在每个观测下比较两类动作块成对差异（生成块对演示块、两个独立生成块之间），当生成与演示动作块分布一致时两类差异分布完全重合，通过最小化二者能量距离为每个任务和NFE预算获得改进的采样调度。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0基准上几乎每个预算下成功率均提升，π0.5与GR00T N1.7均提升2.8个百分点；增益可迁移至三个真实世界操作任务，最高提升3.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 仅通过优化采样调度即可使生成动作块分布更接近演示分布并提升成功率，GYS是一种无需训练、可跨模型与仿真/真实场景迁移的有效方法。

Abstract: Flow matching robot policies generate action chunks by integrating a learned velocity field at inference. However, under a fixed budget of neural function evaluations (NFEs), the integration result depends on the sampling schedule. This motivates optimizing the schedule to bring the generated action distribution closer to the demonstrations and improve task success. We propose Ground Your Steps (GYS), a training-free method that optimizes the sampling schedule by matching the generated and demonstrated chunk distributions at each observation. GYS applies truncated fractional differencing along each arm joint's action sequence and compares two types of pairwise differences between action chunks at each observation: differences of a generated chunk against a demonstration, and differences of two independent generated chunks against each other. After fractional differencing, these two pairwise-difference distributions coincide exactly when the distributions of generated and demonstrated action chunks match. GYS minimizes the energy distance between these distributions to obtain an improved schedule for each task and NFE budget. Compared with the uniform grid, GYS brings the generated chunk distribution closer to the demonstrated distribution and raises the success rate through the sampling schedule alone. On RoboTwin 2.0 benchmarks, GYS improves success rates at nearly every budget, with gains of 2.8 percentage points for both $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.7. The gains also transfer to three real-world manipulation tasks, reaching up to 3.3 percentage points. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [60] [WorldPhy: Benchmarking Fine-Grained Physical Plausibility of World Models in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34130)
*Ziyuan Chu,Mingzhi Zheng,Kaiyue Zhang,Jinan Zhang,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WorldPhy基准，用于细粒度评估机器人世界模型生成视频的物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型基准忽视机器人特定物理细节，难以准确评估生成视频是否满足精细物理约束。

Method: 构建含300个任务（初始帧图像+指令）的基准，收集超12K人工标注训练WorldPhyReward，从六个细粒度物理维度和一个指令遵循维度评分并给出理由。

Result: WorldPhyReward与人类评分一致性优于通用VLM和现有奖励模型；系统评估13个前沿开源与闭源世界模型，显示其在多种机器人场景中难以持续保持细粒度物理合理性。

Conclusion: 当前世界模型在机器人场景的细粒度物理合理性上仍有明显不足，WorldPhy为准确评估提供有效工具。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used in robotics to improve the performance of embodied models. Compared with general-purpose video generation, robotics places more stringent requirements on world models. Generated videos must satisfy fine-grained physical constraints on robot motion and object interactions. However, existing world model benchmarks can overlook robotics specific physical details and struggle to assess them accurately. To address this problem, we introduce WorldPhy, a world model benchmark for accurate, fine-grained evaluation of physical plausibility in robotics. Our benchmark contains 300 tasks, each consisting of an initial-frame image and a task instruction, covering diverse robotics scenes, task categories, viewpoints, and robot configurations. We further collect over 12K high-quality human annotations, and use them to train WorldPhyReward to accurately provide scores and reasons across six fine-grained physical dimensions and an instruction-following dimension. Comparisons with general VLMs and existing reward models show that WorldPhyReward better aligns with human ratings. We system- atically evaluate 13 frontier open and closed-source world models. The results reveal that current world models struggle to consistently maintain fine-grained physical plausibility across different robotics scenarios. Our code is available at this https URL WorldPhy.

</details>


### [61] [WAtcher: Watching Ahead for Safe Robotic Manipulation with Efficient World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34124)
*Ziyu Shan,Xiao Ma,Kangrui Yu,Changjie Wang,Gaoyuan Wu,Zaijia Yang,Chuanrui Zhang,Qirui Li,Yanbo Zhang,Duomin Wang,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WAtcher安全感知世界动作框架，在执行候选动作块前利用紧凑世界动作模型快速想象其结果，并通过可插拔Safe-Guard模块评估风险并触发有限回退，从而降低危险执行而不损失任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）虽能结合未来想象与动作生成，但会无条件执行每个动作块，忽视潜在风险，缺乏安全保障。

Method: 构建紧凑WAM，其视频专家仅用两步去噪即可想象候选动作块的视觉结果；设计WAM外部的轻量Safe-Guard模块，根据想象结果对候选块打分并在高风险时触发有界回退，可迁移至其他WAMs；通过自动化流水线在RoboTwin、LIBERO及真实机器人上扰动专家演示，依据执行时记录的接触、失去支撑等物理事件标注约2.5万个窗口用于训练。

Result: Safe-Guard在盲集上达到92.1%平衡准确率，将训练任务闭环危险试验从27.0%降至10.8%且不降低成功率；在300题留出基准上得94.2%，对比GPT-6-Astra的72.5%和Claude Opus 5.5的63.3%；WAtcher在RoboTwin 2.0和ManiSkill3上平均成功率分别为90.4%和82.4%，与领先WAMs持平或更优；在五个真实任务上开启Safe-Guard达74.0%，对比π0.5的59.5%。

Conclusion: WAtcher通过执行前想象与可插拔风险评估有效提升了世界动作模型的安全性，同时保持或提升任务性能，并具备跨模型迁移能力。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) couple future imagination with action generation, but they execute every action chunk while ignoring the potential risk. To fill this gap, we present WAtcher, a safety-aware world-action framework in which the policy imagines the outcome of the candidate chunk before execution. For fast future preview, WAtcher builds on a compact WAM whose video expert imagines the visual outcome in only two denoising steps. In addition, we propose Safe-Guard, a lightweight module kept outside the WAM that scores each candidate chunk from its imagined outcome and triggers a bounded retreat when the risk is high. Safe-Guard is detachable and can be applied to other WAMs. To train Safe-Guard, we build an autonomous pipeline to collect hazard data from RoboTwin, LIBERO, and a real robot. Specifically, the pipeline perturbs expert demonstrations and labels each candidate chunk from the physical events recorded when it is executed, such as contacts and losses of support. Using the ~25,000 labeled windows from this pipeline, Safe-Guard reaches 92.1% balanced accuracy on a blind set, and cuts hazardous closed-loop trials on training tasks from 27.0% to 10.8% without lowering task success. On a held-out 300-question benchmark, it scores 94.2%, against 72.5% and 63.3% for zero-shot GPT-6-Astra and Claude Opus 5.5. WAtcher also reaches 90.4% and 82.4% average success on RoboTwin 2.0 and ManiSkill3, on par with or above leading WAMs, and 74.0% on five real-world tasks with Safe-Guard on, against 59.5% for $\pi_{0.5}$. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [62] [CoTacWAM: Joint Modeling of Vision and Touch for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34117)
*Lei Jin,Yiding Ma,Zhuohang Li,Jiachen Liu,Zibo Zhu,Kairui Liu,Chen Gao,Wei Wu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoTacWAM世界动作模型，通过共享腕部视觉与触觉的接触语义，提升操作性能并增强对缺失输入的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕部图像和触觉从不同信号观察同一局部交互，需连接其共同接触含义并保留各自独特证据，以支持跨输入组合的策略学习。

Method: 构建共享接触语义的世界动作模型，联合表示腕部视觉与触觉，结合头视角场景上下文进行预测性动作学习，并在独立检测器确认失败后条件化修正。

Result: 在四项真实世界任务中，操作性能优于评估基线，对缺失局部观测具有鲁棒性，自主重试带来额外增益。

Conclusion: 共享接触语义的联合表示能有效融合多模态证据，提升操作性能、缺失输入鲁棒性及失败恢复能力。

Abstract: We present CoTacWAM, a world-action model that learns shared contact semantics between wrist vision and touch, achieving the best observed performance in our nominal manipulation and missing-input evaluations. The two modalities observe the same local interaction through different signals: wrist images reveal nearby geometry, while touch records physical contact. Our approach connects their common contact meaning while preserving the evidence unique to each modality, giving the policy a joint description of interaction across available input combinations. This representation supports predictive action learning alongside head-view scene context and conditions corrections after an independent detector confirms a failed attempt. Experiments across four real-world tasks demonstrate improved manipulation performance over the evaluated baselines, robustness to missing local observations, and further gains from autonomous retries.

</details>


### [63] [xFORM: Accelerating Fixed-Lag LiDAR Odometry with Exact Factor Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34053)
*Ruogu Li,Easton R. Potokar,Michael Kaess*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: xFORM通过因子压缩和并行化加速LiDAR里程计，在保持精度的同时显著降低计算延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR里程计处理大规模点云时计算代价高，尤其联合优化窗口内位姿时延迟大，影响下游控制与路径规划。

Method: 提出xFORM，利用因子压缩将每对扫描的数十万特征匹配压缩为34个标量残差，保持原目标与高斯-牛顿系统精确等价；并实现CPU并行特征提取的xFORM-CPU和GPU加速特征处理、对应搜索与因子压缩的xFORM-GPU。

Result: 在三个数据集共15条完整序列上，xFORM-CPU和xFORM-GPU分别比FORM整体加速1.1-1.4倍和2.0-4.0倍，且与原始实现无显著数值差异。

Conclusion: xFORM有效加速了LiDAR里程计三个关键阶段，在保证数值精度的前提下大幅降低延迟，利于实时下游任务。

Abstract: Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) sensors are increasingly popular for state estimation because of their precision, long range, and high measurement rate. Unfortunately, LiDAR Odometry (LO) systems incur substantial computational cost when processing large point clouds, particularly those that jointly refine a window of scan poses. While our prior LO work FORM is real-time, the costs of feature extraction, correspondence search, and the evaluation and linearization of residuals during pose optimization can introduce significant latency. This latency makes downstream tasks such as control and path planning difficult. To overcome this, we present xFORM, which accelerates the three stages of FORM. xFORM uses factor compression to reduce an optimization problem involving hundreds of thousands of feature matches to 34 scalar residuals per scan pair, while preserving the original objective and Gauss-Newton system in exact arithmetic. We integrate this optimizer into two variants: xFORM-CPU, which additionally parallelizes feature extraction on the CPU, and xFORM-GPU, which accelerates feature processing, correspondence search, and factor compression on the GPU. Across fifteen complete sequences from three datasets, xFORM-CPU and xFORM-GPU achieve overall speedups of 1.1-1.4$\times$ and 2.0-4.0$\times$ over FORM, respectively, with no numerically significant differences to the original implementation.

</details>


### [64] [End-to-End Image-Goal Navigation for Agile Quadrotors via Recurrent State Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34046)
*Simone Nascivera,Ismail Geles,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个直接从图像目标输出推力和角速率指令的四旋翼图像目标导航策略，无需状态估计，在未见三维环境中实现82.3%仿真成功率与84%真机零样本成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像目标导航需在无地图、无目标位姿下到达目标照片视点，三维场景还需估计位移并生成连续控制。同一视觉差异在不同速度下需不同动作，而单目图像既无度量尺度也无机体运动信息，导致现有策略局限于平面动作空间且依赖状态估计器进行飞行控制；直接从图像到底层控制指令的策略仅用于敏捷飞行，尚未用于未见环境中的目标图像导航。

Method: 提出直接输出推力和角速率指令的循环视觉策略，不依赖SLAM/VO/SfM等状态估计或独立速度控制器。采用四阶段训练流程：几何监督、运动监督、特权教师蒸馏、端到端强化学习微调。

Result: 在未见环境的1000次留出飞行中成功率为82.3%，成功飞行平均终端位置误差0.10m；零样本部署于真实四旋翼，在未见室内环境25次试验中成功率达84%。

Conclusion: 验证了无需状态估计、直接从图像目标映射到底层飞控指令的端到端策略可在未见三维环境中实现敏捷且精确的四旋翼图像目标导航。

Abstract: Image-goal navigation asks a robot to reach the viewpoint of a target photograph without a map or goal pose. In three dimensions, this requires not only recognizing the goal but also estimating 3D displacement and generating continuous control actions to approach and settle at the target view. This is difficult because the same visual difference between current and goal image can require very different actions depending on the robot's velocity, yet monocular images alone reveal neither metric scale nor body motion. As a result, existing image-goal policies are limited to planar action spaces and rely on a state estimator for flight control. Policies that map directly from images to low-level control commands have been demonstrated for agile flight, but not toward goal images in unseen environments. We present the first image-goal navigation policy that directly commands thrust and angular rates for agile quadrotor flight in unseen 3D environments, without relying on state estimation (e.g., SLAM, VO, or SfM) or a separate velocity controller. We train a recurrent vision-based policy through a four-stage pipeline that combines geometric and motion supervision with privileged-teacher distillation and end-to-end reinforcement learning fine-tuning. On 1000 held-out flights in unseen environments, the policy achieves an 82.3% success rate with a mean terminal position error of 0.10m across successful flights. Deployed zero-shot on a real quadrotor, the policy reaches an 84% success rate over 25 trials in an unseen indoor environment.

</details>


### [65] [WISER-RL: A Wake-Sleep Learning Mechanism for Robot Reinforcement Learning from One Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34043)
*Kwunhang Wong,Yang Bai,Qingyang Bao,Liudi Yang,Chenzhi Liu,Jiehong Lin,Wei Huang,Lin Li,Ziyuan Liu,Yujie Yang,Gitta Kutyniok,Zhongrui Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WISER-RL通过结合模仿学习、冲突感知经验重加权和选择性经验回放的睡眠-清醒循环，将单次人类演示的辅助练习转化为自主控制，在LIBERO和真实机器人任务上达到100%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人仅从一次人类演示学习时自主成功率低，导致强化学习缺乏奖励反馈，需要一种能将辅助练习逐步转化为自主控制的框架。

Method: 提出WISER-RL复合强化学习框架：清醒阶段用GPTDAgger监测扩散策略采样动作间的不一致性，超过阈值时由冻结的GPT控制器利用检索经验继续 episode；睡眠阶段用冲突感知经验重加权（CAER）比较视觉匹配的成功轨迹，对剩余步数更多且冲突的动作窗口降权；策略模仿重加权成功样本，并通过离线优势引导RL从成功和失败中学习，再进行下一轮采集，最后经在线PPO精调。

Result: 在六个LIBERO任务上自主策略均达到100%评估成功率，在两个真实机器人任务上均达到100%试验成功率。

Conclusion: WISER-RL能够有效利用单次演示和辅助练习，通过睡眠-清醒循环与冲突感知重加权实现高成功率的自主机器人控制。

Abstract: A robot learning from one human demonstration may rarely succeed on its own, leaving reinforcement learning (RL) with little reward feedback. We introduce WISER-RL ({W}ake--sleep policy improvement loop, {I}mitation, and {S}elective {E}xperience {R}eplay), a composite reinforcement learning framework that turns assisted practice into autonomous control. During wake, GPTDAgger monitors disagreement among actions sampled by a diffusion policy. When disagreement crosses a threshold, a frozen GPT controller uses retrieved experience to continue the episode. During sleep, Conflict-Aware Experience Reweighting (CAER) compares visually matched successful trajectories and downweights conflicting action windows with more steps remaining to success. The policy imitates the reweighted successes and learns from both successes and failures through offline advantage-guided RL before collecting the next round. After final online PPO refinement, the autonomous policy succeeded in 100% evaluations on each of six LIBERO tasks and 100% trials on each of two real-robot tasks.

</details>


### [66] [Ask to Land: Learning a Mask-Conditioned Open-Vocabulary Landing Policy via Differentiable Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34020)
*Fangyu Sun,Guanyan Wu,Yang Deng,Jiahao Cui,Fanxing Li,Yuxiang Huang,Wenxian Yu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Ask to Land框架，结合冻结的开放词汇感知前端与可微物理学习的控制策略，实现无人机根据自然语言描述在静态或移动平台上着陆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机着陆系统依赖预定义视觉标记或特定平台训练，且假设目标运动结构化；开放词汇研究虽支持文本提示目标选择，但与闭环着陆控制的集成尚未充分探索。

Method: 使用冻结的YOLO-World与MobileSAM作为开放词汇感知前端，输出实时语义分割掩码作为策略输入；控制策略通过可微物理学习（BPTT）优化，奖励函数设计为视觉对齐、高度自适应下降速度和稳定控制。

Result: 真实世界部署验证该方法能在用户指定的多样物体（包括静态和移动平台）上成功着陆，无需目标特定微调。

Conclusion: 将开放词汇感知与可微物理学习的闭环控制相结合，可实现基于自然语言提示的无人机着陆，并连续跟踪动态目标运动。

Abstract: Existing drone landing systems, whether model-based or learning-based, typically rely on predefined visual markers (e.g., ArUco tags) or are trained for specific platform categories, and often assume structured target motion. Recent open-vocabulary studies enable text-prompted target selection, but integrating this capability with learned closed-loop landing control remains underexplored. To this end, we present \textit{Ask to Land}, a mask-conditioned framework that enables a quadrotor to land on various platforms described by a user in natural language, including randomly moving platforms. The core of our approach is the integration of a frozen state-of-the-art open-vocabulary perception frontend (YOLO-World with MobileSAM) and a control policy learned by differentiable physics. By providing real-time, task-agnostic semantic segmentation masks as policy inputs, our framework enables the drone to land on prompt-selected targets without target-specific fine-tuning while continuously tracking dynamic target motion during descent. The frozen frontend processes the prompt, while the policy receives the mask without text embeddings or category labels. To efficiently optimize the control policy, we leverage differentiable physics learning via Backpropagation-Through-Time (BPTT), and employ a carefully designed reward that enforces visual alignment with the landing target and altitude-adaptive descent speed, together with stable control. Real-world deployments validate that our approach achieves landings on diverse user-specified objects, including static and moving platforms.

</details>


### [67] [$\widetilde{\varepsilon}$: Does Initial Noise Induce Directional Bias in Vision-Language-Action Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33816)
*Junmyeong Lee,Dongmin Shin,Wooseok Jeon,JunGyu Lee,Seunghyun Shin,Hae-Gon Jeon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了扩散式VLA中初始噪声在动作空间引入与噪声时间均值一致的方向性偏差，并提出训练时用对偶噪声对约束一致性、推理时去除噪声时间均值的简单框架，显著提升动作鲁棒性与任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式VLA动作生成对初始噪声高度敏感，导致动作误差且执行中难以恢复；现有方法仅搜索有利噪声初始化，而动作专家本身仍对噪声敏感。

Method: 训练阶段强制对偶噪声对(ε, -ε)产生一致的动作预测；推理阶段去除噪声的时间均值，以消除其引起的方向性偏差。

Result: 在仿真基准上大幅降低动作偏差并提升任务表现；真实机器人实验中，π_{0.5}的平均任务成功率从40%提升至68%，执行更稳定一致。

Conclusion: 初始噪声的偏差可通过训练与推理两阶段的干预有效抑制，所提框架简单有效，显著增强VLA对初始噪声的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) have widely adopted diffusion-based action generation for continuous robot motions, enabling fine-grained control across diverse tasks. However, recent studies have shown that their performance is highly sensitive to initial noise, with resulting action errors that make recovery difficult during execution. Existing approaches search for favorable noise initializations to improve performance, yet the action expert itself remains sensitive to noise. In this work, we reveal that initial noise induces a consistent directional bias in action space that closely aligns with the noise's temporal mean. We thus introduce a simple yet effective framework that improves robustness to initial noise through interventions during both training and inference. During training, we enforce consistent action predictions from antithetic noise pairs $(\epsilon, -\epsilon)$ and, at inference, remove the noise's temporal mean. Our method substantially reduces action deviation and improves task performance across simulation benchmarks. In real-robot experiments, our method increases the average task success rate of $\pi_{0.5}$ from 40% to 68%, with more stable and consistent execution.

</details>


### [68] [LSRP*-LA: Scalable and Anytime Multi-Agent Path Finding for Large Agents with Asynchronous Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33793)
*Dachuan Liu,Shizhe Zhao,Zhongqiang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了LSRP*-LA规划器，用于解决大型智能体异步动作的多智能体路径规划问题（LA-MAPF-AA），其中每个智能体可占据多个单元格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPF算法通常假设每个智能体占据单个顶点且所有动作同步，限制了实际应用；而现有摆脱这两个假设的规划器要么只能处理少量智能体，要么无法改进解的质量。

Method: 基于LSRP*扩展开发了LSRP*-LA规划器，支持大型智能体（占据多个单元格）和异步动作，具有可扩展性和完备性，并在初始解获得后持续优化解质量直至运行时间预算耗尽。

Result: 在多种地图上最多800个智能体的测试中，LSRP*-LA能处理的智能体数量是基线方法的两倍，且解的成本约为近期同步大型智能体规划器的一半。

Conclusion: LSRP*-LA有效解决了大型智能体异步动作的MAPF问题，在可扩展性和解质量上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) seeks collision-free paths for multiple agents from their start locations to their goal locations while minimizing path costs. Most MAPF algorithms assume that each agent occupies a single vertex and that the actions of all agents are synchronized. These assumptions may limit the use of MAPF planners in practice. Existing planners that get rid of both assumptions either handle only a few agents or cannot improve their solutions. Recently, a planner called LSRP* was proposed, which is scalable and anytime with asynchronous actions, but still limited by that each agent occupies a single vertex. This paper develops a new planner called LSRP*-LA for MAPF for large agents with asynchronous actions (LA-MAPF-AA), where an agent can occupy several cells. LSRP*-LA is scalable and complete, and it keeps improving solution quality after the initial solution is obtained until the runtime budget depletes. We analyze the properties of LSRP*-LA and test it against several baselines with up to 800 agents on various maps. LSRP*-LA can handle twice as many agents as baseline methods and its solution cost is about half that of recent synchronous planners for large agents.

</details>


### [69] [Triage Before Fine-Tuning: Feasibility-Gated Selection and Monitored Adaptation of Embodied Foundation Models for a Fixed Physical Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33772)
*Zilin Huang,Zhengyang Wan,Zihao Sheng,Boyue Wang,Ruolin Yang,Yuhao Luo,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TRIAGE框架，将具身基础模型在固定平台上的性能差距分解为感知、延迟和分布三类，实现可行性门控的选择与监控适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者常按基准排名选择公开检查点再微调，但平台固定且缺乏本地数据，需明确检查点在固定平台上损失什么、本地数据能弥补什么。

Method: TRIAGE分五阶段：传感器合同和端到端延迟的不可覆盖门控、十分钟本地日志探针、通过门控候选的目标相关评分排序、闭环监控微调与发布前闭环检查。

Result: 驾驶基准上，低于领先闭环模型延迟的截止时间导致80-90%路线碰撞；等像素下丢失视野比丢失分辨率对规划影响大2.2-5.7倍；本地帧弥补75%稳定性差距但小预算下增加扰动敏感度；四类部署中两类按分数选择违反门控；实车中工厂相机未通过感知门控、加装相机修复；延迟门控剔除两个最高分候选且均未完成闭环行程；选中模型5次闭环行程通过4次；闭环检查扣留了离线改进的微调模型。

Conclusion: TRIAGE帮助无法从头训练基础模型的组织判断公开检查点能否运行于自有机器人或车辆，并指出若不能应修复什么。

Abstract: Embodied foundation models are increasingly deployed by selection: a practitioner picks a public checkpoint by benchmark rank and fine-tunes it on local data. The platform is fixed before the model is chosen, with little or no local data. We ask what such a checkpoint loses on a fixed platform and which part of the loss local data can buy back. We split the gap by what can repair it. A sensing gap, information the sensors do not capture, cannot be closed with their data; a latency gap, output arriving after the control loop needs it, belongs to the loop, not the weights; only a distribution gap is fine-tuning's to repair. TRIAGE turns this into feasibility-gated selection and monitored adaptation in five stages: gates on the sensor contract and on end-to-end latency that no score can override, probes on ten minutes of local logs, a ranking by target-relevant score among the candidates that pass, and fine-tuning that is monitored and checked in closed loop before release. On driving benchmarks, a deadline below a leading closed-loop model's latency makes it collide on 80 to 90% of routes, and losing field of view moves plans 2.2 to 5.7 times more than losing resolution at equal pixels. Local frames close 75% of a stability gap but raise perturbation sensitivity at small budgets, and picking by score violates a gate in two of four deployment profiles. On a full-scale vehicle, the factory camera failed the sensing gate and an added camera repaired it. The latency gate removed the two highest-scoring candidates, neither of which completed a closed-loop trip. The selected model passed acceptance in 4 of 5 closed-loop trips, and the closed-loop check withheld a fine-tuned model that had improved offline. TRIAGE lets organizations that cannot train foundation models from scratch find out whether a public checkpoint can run on the robot or vehicle they own, and what to fix if not.

</details>


### [70] [FailPoint-X: A Policy-Matched, Failure-Anchored Benchmark for Offline VLA Post-Training from Human Takeovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33769)
*Zirui Li,Yujie Yan,Jingxuan Qian,Yangang Ren,Haizhou Yin,Haoran Xu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FailPoint-X 是一个用于离线 VLA 后训练的基准，通过人类接管数据与固定初始化、匹配更新次数和统一起点评估，表明在恢复失败上做行为克隆能显著提升恢复率（+29.4pp），但价值学习无进一步增益，且克隆会降低正常任务成功率（-30.0pp），混合演示可缓解（降至-16.5pp）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类接管能为VLA策略提供纠错监督，但需将其对离线后训练的贡献与持续优化区分开，以明确后训练方法本身的效果，避免混淆。

Method: 构建FailPoint-X基准：从固定π0.5检查点初始化，在10个LIBERO-X任务族、最多5个难度上收集629个自然失败，每个失败标注不确定区间并从中采样种子帧开始接管；记录2823次接管尝试，接受2400次恢复，其中2320用于后训练。在匹配actor更新次数下比较三种方法：恢复轨迹的行为克隆、基于恢复与失败rollout的价值离线学习、在现有演示上的持续训练。评估从384个保留失败区间的固定中点重启。

Result: 在240个L1-L3保留失败上训练1200步内，克隆较仅演示控制提升恢复率29.4个百分点；价值学习器未带来进一步增益（1.5pp）。在L1-L5名义起点同 horizon 探索评估中，克隆相对基础策略成功率下降30.0pp；混入演示将损失降至16.5pp，但在更严格的预指定horizon下该缓解缩小。

Conclusion: 人类接管后训练中，行为克隆能有效提升失败恢复能力，但会损害正常任务成功率，且价值学习未显优势；混合演示可部分缓解性能损失。FailPoint-X通过可恢复失败状态和共享评估器支持从相同起点公平比较方法。

Abstract: Human takeovers offer corrective supervision for vision-language-action (VLA) policies, but their contribution to offline post-training must be separated from that of continued optimization. We introduce FailPoint-X, a benchmark for offline VLA post-training with human takeovers. To represent uncertainty in failure onset, an annotator marks an interval for each failure, and training takeovers start at seeded frames sampled within it. From one fixed $\pi_{0.5}$ checkpoint, which also initializes post-training, we assemble 629 natural failures across 10 LIBERO-X task families at up to five difficulty levels. All 2,823 takeover attempts are logged; 2,400 are accepted recoveries, of which 2,320 are used for post-training. Evaluation restarts every method from the fixed midpoints of 384 held-out failure intervals. Under matched actor-update counts, we compare behavior cloning of recoveries, value-based offline learning on recoveries and failed rollouts, and continued training on existing demonstrations. Within 1,200 steps on the 240 L1-L3 held-out failures, cloning improves recovery by 29.4 percentage points (pp) over the demonstration-only control; the value-based learner does not establish a further gain (1.5 pp). In exploratory evaluation from L1-L5 nominal starts at the same horizon, cloning loses 30.0 pp of success relative to the base policy; mixing in demonstrations reduces the loss to 16.5 pp, a reduction that shrinks under a stricter, pre-specified horizon. Restorable failure states and a shared evaluator let all methods be compared from identical starts.

</details>


### [71] [RegHarness: Regulating What Code-as-Policy Agents Keep from Steps to Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33739)
*Shengming Liu,Yunyi Gao,Xinyi Wang,Jianyong Sun,Shuai Wang,Yi Deng,Li Chenye,Mingcai Zhou,Sim Kuan Goh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RegHarness 是一个双层治理框架，显式控制 Code-as-Policy 智能体在单次试验内的步骤提交与跨运行间的代码修改持久化，在 LIBERO-PRO 上达到 87.8% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Code-as-Policy 智能体虽能生成、执行和修订机器人程序，但缺乏治理“哪些动作在试验内保留、哪些代码修改跨运行延续”的显式机制，导致步骤级执行与跨运行适应两个关键方面控制不足。

Method: 提出 RegHarness 双层 harness：试验内运行时治理对重置场景中的每个提议步骤进行回放评估，仅当 RATs 验证器与任务级 LLM 审计器均批准时才由固定规则控制器提交；运行间由编码智能体利用记录的失败开发并测试对技能、原语和规则的修订，且默认禁用直至作者显式启用；不依赖 VLA 策略或演示。

Result: 使用单一低成本模型，在 LIBERO-PRO 配对试验上成功率为 87.8%（158/180），RATs 为 39.4%；MolmoSpaces 上为 72.0%。2×2 消融显示：仅技能精炼使 RATs 成功试验从 71 增至 135，仅运行时治理达 105，而 RATs 原生逐步重置回放模式仅 46。

Conclusion: 对于使用低成本模型的 Code-as-Policy 智能体，任务成功不仅取决于生成程序的执行方式，还取决于所调用可复用技能的质量及其跨运行演化方式。

Abstract: Code-as-Policy agents generate, execute, and revise robot programs, but often lack explicit mechanisms to govern what persists across executions: which actions are retained within a trial and which code modifications carry over to subsequent runs. This leaves two critical aspects of agent behavior insufficiently controlled: step-level execution and cross-run adaptation. We present RegHarness, a two-level harness that governs both forms of persistence for the RATs agent team, without relying on a vision-language-action (VLA) policy or demonstrations. Within each trial, run-time governance evaluates each proposed step from a reset scene, replaying the committed steps, while a fixed-rule controller commits the step only when both RATs' verifier and a task-long LLM auditor approve it. Between runs, a coding agent uses logged failures to develop and test revisions to skills, primitives, and rules, which remain disabled by default until explicitly enabled by the authors. Using a single low-cost model across all run-time roles, RegHarness achieves an 87.8% success rate (158/180) on paired LIBERO-PRO trials, compared with 39.4% for RATs, and a 72.0% success rate on MolmoSpaces trials. A $2\times2$ ablation study of RegHarness's two levels shows that its skill refinement alone increases RATs' successful trials from 71 to 135, whereas run-time governance alone achieves 105 and RATs' native stepwise reset-and-replay mode achieves only 46. These findings suggest that, for Code-as-Policy agents operating with low-cost models, task success depends not only on how generated programs are executed, but also on the quality of the reusable skills they invoke and how those skills evolve across runs.

</details>


### [72] [PROVE: Predict-to-Specify and Observe-to-Verify for Human-Aligned Evaluation of Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33733)
*Bowen Lyu,Yunan Liu,Liaoyuan Fan,Qinchuan Cheng,Jiafei Duan,Cheston Tan,Shijie Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PROVE和PROVE-Lite提出一种先指定成功条件再验证结果的机器人操作评估方法，显著提升开放世界评估与人类判断的一致性，并用于策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放世界机器人操作评估中，模拟器谓词与人类判断一致性仅69.09%，前沿VLM评估器仅86.12%，评估不可靠。

Method: PROVE根据指令、初始场景、目标物体参考图像和生成的目标图像细化成功条件（目标图像仅用于细化条件而非证据），再观察结果进行验证；并将其蒸馏为无需参考图像的4B评估器PROVE-Lite，单次前向预测结果和终端状态。

Result: 在65种原子动作的6,500条人工标注轨迹上，PROVE与人类判断一致率达96.83%；PROVE-Lite达91.20%且比PROVE快约38倍。应用于开源策略时，报告的成功率远低于原生检查并改变策略排名；映射为严重性感知奖励后，其诊断改进PPO后训练，优于模拟器和二值奖励。

Conclusion: 先指定成功条件再验证的评估范式能更准确判断开放世界机器人操作，并可高效蒸馏为轻量评估器，直接提升策略学习效果。

Abstract: Judging open-world robot manipulation is hard because layouts, articulated fixtures, and valid goal configurations vary widely. On RoboCasa365, simulator predicates agree with human judgments on only 69.09\% of rollouts, and a frontier VLM judge on 86.12\%. We present \textbf{PROVE} (Predict-to-Specify, Observe-to-Verify), which first specifies what success should look like and then checks the outcome. PROVE refines success conditions from the instruction, the initial scene, a target-object reference image, and a generated goal image. The goal image only sharpens the conditions and never serves as evidence. On 6,500 human-annotated rollouts over 65 atomic actions, PROVE reaches \textbf{96.83\%} agreement. Applied to open-source policies, it reports far lower success than native checks and changes their ranking. To reduce evaluation overhead in policy learning, we distill PROVE into \textbf{PROVE-Lite}, a 4B evaluator that needs no reference images. In one forward pass, PROVE-Lite predicts the outcome and the terminal state of each task condition. It reaches \textbf{91.20\%} agreement while running about 38$\times$ faster than PROVE. Mapped to a severity-aware reward, its diagnoses improve PPO post-training over both simulator and binary rewards.

</details>


### [73] [RoboJackVR: Turning Robot Tasks into VR Games for Scalable Demonstration Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33731)
*Ben Ma,Jixuan Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboJackVR是一个独立的VR游戏，玩家通过操控模拟机器人执行战斗技能来贡献演示数据；MuJoCo仿真和录制在头显本地运行，无需外部PC或服务器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展机器人演示数据集需要能容纳更多参与者和多样任务的采集系统，但遥操作门槛和任务适配成本限制了扩展。

Method: 将MuJoCo仿真与演示记录在头显本地运行；用教程、技能选择、战斗后果和角色成长组织重复尝试；引入任务契约声明资产、控制、重置、成功标准和记录语义，配合游戏配置和共享绑定实现任务复用游戏流程，并用批处理工具打包资源、生成配置。

Result: 在66个LIBERO-90任务上，仅用VR演示训练的ACT策略在留出初始状态上平均保留完整标准仿真演示基线约91%的成功率（四个训练种子平均）。

Conclusion: 在此设定下，游戏化采集的VR演示可用于策略学习。

Abstract: Scaling robot demonstration datasets requires collection systems that accommodate more participants and diverse tasks, yet teleoperation barriers and task-adaptation effort limit this expansion. We present RoboJackVR, a standalone virtual reality (VR) game in which players manipulate simulated robots to execute combat skills and contribute demonstrations. MuJoCo simulation and demonstration recording run locally on the headset, removing the need for an external PC or simulation server during collection. Tutorials, skill selection, combat consequences, and character progression organize repeated task attempts within a continuing game. Task contracts declare assets, controls, resets, success criteria, and recording semantics; separate game configurations specify guidance, attempt groups, skill effects, and course placement. Shared bindings connect these declarations to task execution, outcome handling, and recording, allowing tasks to reuse the game flow while retaining their control and success semantics.
