Batch tools package supported resources and generate configurations, with manual adaptation and review for new interfaces and unsupported predicates. We instantiate the approach with SO101-Nexus-derived and LIBERO tasks. On the 66 LIBERO-90 tasks covered by the collected data, ACT policies trained solely on VR demonstrations retain approximately 91% of the full-standard simulation-demonstration baseline's success rate on held-out initial states, averaged over four training seeds. These results support the utility of play-collected VR demonstrations for policy learning in this setting.

</details>


### [74] [Practice Makes Fluent: Learning to Use Earlier Predictions in Looped Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33722)
*Minseok seo,Hee-Seon Kim,Changick Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLUENT通过无额外标签的蒸馏与出口门控训练，使循环模型在练习中学会用更少迭代完成相同任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型随经验提升性能，但未必学会减少计算量；循环模型的中间预测支持提前退出，然而共享权重更新可能损害最终循环预测，出口门控也需跟踪早期预测质量变化。

Method: FLUENT将初始模型最终循环预测蒸馏到早期预测，把当前最终循环预测锚定到同一冻结教师，并训练出口门控在偏离当前最终预测与计算成本之间权衡；无需额外任务标签或奖励。

Result: 在新Crafter世界中提升浅层深度性能，在LIBERO中以更少动作头迭代提高成功率，并在较低选定深度维持语言模型准确率；真实机器人成功率从62.3%升至74.6%，成功回合平均时间从69秒降至31秒。

Conclusion: FLUENT使循环模型通过实践在更浅深度和更少迭代下保持或提升性能，验证了学习减少计算量的可行性。

Abstract: People become faster at a task through practice, but models that improve with experience do not necessarily learn to use less computation. We train looped models, whose intermediate predictions allow earlier exits, to perform tasks with fewer iterations through practice. This is difficult because shared-weight updates can degrade the final-loop prediction while improving early ones, and the exit gate must track early predictions as their quality changes. We propose FLUENT, which requires no additional task labels or rewards: it distills the initial model's final-loop prediction into early predictions, anchors the current final-loop prediction to the same frozen teacher, and trains the exit gate to balance disagreement with the current final-loop prediction against computation cost. FLUENT improves performance at shallower depth in new Crafter worlds, raises LIBERO success with fewer action-head iterations, and maintains language-model accuracy at lower selected depth. On a real robot, success rises from 62.3\% to 74.6\% while mean time over successful episodes falls from 69\,s to 31\,s. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [75] [A general-purpose safe manipulator planning framework for diverse environments without retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33721)
*Mingkang Long,Rong Su,Luhang Hong,Qingqing Wang,Ruochen Tai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种通用机械臂任务规划框架，通过有限状态抽象模型和块保守规格生成，使机器人无需重新训练控制器即可在不同环境下安全抓取物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作环境变化时需重新训练，训练成本过高阻碍日常使用，需要无需大量数据和算力即可安全抓取不同物体的创新方法。

Method: 构建与原始系统动力学双相似的有限状态抽象模型描述机器人前方空间可达性；提出块保守规格生成方法将任意环境下的操作任务转化为抽象模型状态可达性规格；任务在抽象模型上验证可行安全后即保证真实机器人安全可达。

Result: 在Unitree G1 U1人形机器人上验证，日常任务如抓取物体、按电梯按钮、捡浆果均可在该框架内处理；抽象模型仅需构建一次，不同任务由特定块保守规格捕获，通用任务规划器结合视觉反馈控制实现。

Conclusion: 该框架实现跨环境免重训练的安全操作任务规划，避免昂贵模型重建，为机器人日常使用提供可行方案。

Abstract: Nowadays, robots need to be retrained whenever their operating environment changes, and this prohibitive training cost keeps them far from daily use. The community is calling for innovations that bestow on a robot the ability to safely grasp different objects under different conditions without excessive training data and computing power. In response to this call, we present a general-purpose manipulator mission-planning framework, allowing a robot to safely perform manipulation tasks, such as grasping objects that fit in its gripper, across different operating environments without retraining its controllers. The framework rests on two innovations: a finite-state abstracted model, bisimilar to the original system dynamics, that describes accessibility of the space in front of the robot under its given arm and gripper, and a block-conservative specification generation method that turns each manipulation task under any specific operational environment into specifications about state accessibility of the abstracted model. A task proven feasible and safe on the abstracted model is guaranteed to be safe and achievable by the robot. Real-world case studies conducted on a Unitree G1 U1 humanoid show that manipulation tasks commonly encountered in daily life, such as grasping an object, pressing an elevator button or picking up a berry, can be handled within this framework, where an abstracted model of the G1 U1 robot is built only once to avoid costly model reconstructions, and different tasks are simply captured by specific block-conservative specifications, achievable by a generic mission planner with vision-based feedback control.

</details>


### [76] [$\mathrm{M}^{2}$-WAM: Efficient Hierarchical Memory Across Modalities and Timescales for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33710)
*Xiaopeng Zhang,Weiqi Yuan,Xin Zhu,Jiaxi Huang,Yueqi Hu,Yin Zhang,Jian Yao,Yanjie Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出M²-WAM世界-动作模型，通过分层视觉与交互记忆机制在有限在线内存下实现长时程机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作需要历史上下文，但保留大量视觉历史带来存储与检索成本增长，激进压缩又会丢失细粒度信息；同时已执行动作历史蕴含稀疏视觉观测可能遗漏的中间行为，因此需要模态特定的记忆机制来平衡近期细节与长期保留。

Method: M²-WAM采用分层视觉与交互记忆：近期视觉观测存入稠密短期记忆，被逐出的条目通过门控delta更新整合进固定大小的关联长期记忆；交互记忆在同一短期-长期层级中将已执行动作前缀编码为紧凑时序表示；两类互补记忆通过逐层检索融入世界-动作建模，实现内存占用有界、与回合长度无关的记忆增强动作生成。

Result: 在依赖记忆的仿真基准MIKASA-Robo和RMBench上，平均成功率分别较Fast-WAM绝对提升53.2%和61.2%；在真实世界三个任务中，平均成功率较LingBot-VA绝对提升29.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明M²-WAM为高效记忆增强的世界-动作建模提供了一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation requires historical context, yet retaining extensive visual histories incurs growing storage and retrieval costs, while aggressive compression risks losing fine-grained information needed for subsequent decisions. Moreover, executed action histories capture intermediate behaviors that sparse visual observations may miss. Preserving this complementary context efficiently calls for modality-specific memory mechanisms that balance recent detail with long-term retention. To this end, we introduce $\mathrm{M}^{2}$-WAM, a world-action model equipped with hierarchical visual and interaction memories. $\mathrm{M}^{2}$-WAM preserves recent visual observations in dense short-term memory while consolidating entries evicted from this window into fixed-size associative long-term memory through gated delta updates. Within the same short- and long-term hierarchy, interaction memory encodes executed action prefixes into compact temporal representations, complementing visual observations with behavioral context. These complementary memories are integrated into world-action modeling through layer-wise retrieval, enabling memory-augmented action generation with a bounded online memory footprint independent of episode length. On the memory-dependent simulation benchmarks MIKASA-Robo and RMBench, $\mathrm{M}^{2}$-WAM achieves +53.2% and +61.2% absolute improvements in average success rates over Fast-WAM, respectively. These gains extend to real-world manipulation, where $\mathrm{M}^{2}$-WAM achieves a +29.3% absolute improvement over LingBot-VA in average success across three tasks. These results suggest that $\mathrm{M}^{2}$-WAM offers a promising approach to efficient memory-augmented world-action modeling.

</details>


### [77] [Lab2Sim: Agentic Generation of Interactive Laboratory Equipment from Operation Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33705)
*Yuxin Lin,Pei Ren,Yirong Yang,Xuxin Lv,Xingchuang Liao,Diyuan Hou,Jiale He,Zhengping Che,Yongfei Zhang,Wenjun Wu,Zhaoxin Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Lab2Sim是一个代理框架，能从操作视频中生成可编辑、可直接用于仿真的实验室设备资产，视觉保真度和交互能力优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资产生成方法难以直接从操作视频生成所有设备的仿真资产，且无法忠实复现真实设备的外观、关节运动和内部空腔。

Method: Lab2Sim包含四个专门代理：证据代理结合视频观察与实验室知识指定设备部件、材料和功能需求；资产代理据此构建可编辑的Blender资产；独立验证器对照证据和需求检查几何与外观并提供反馈；仿真集成代理将验证后的资产转换为具有物理属性和关节机制的仿真实例。

Result: 在人类和机器人实验室场景实验中，Lab2Sim的视觉保真度和交互能力优于编码代理和真实到仿真基线（包括使用相同底层模型的Codex基线）；进一步展示了阀门控制排水，并利用生成的资产库在RoboTwin中复现实验室场景和操作。

Conclusion: Lab2Sim能够从操作视频生成高质量、可复用的仿真就绪实验室设备资产，支持机器人实验室任务的可重用仿真环境构建。

Abstract: Generating laboratory assets that faithfully reproduce the appearance, structure, and joint motions of real equipment remains challenging. Existing asset generation methods struggle to directly generate simulation assets for all equipment observed in operation videos and to faithfully reproduce the appearance, joint motions, and internal cavities of the real equipment. To address the challenge, we present Lab2Sim, an agentic framework that generates editable, simulation-ready laboratory equipment assets from egocentric or multi-view operation videos. Lab2Sim comprises four specialized agents. The Evidence Agent combines video observations with laboratory knowledge to specify equipment parts, materials, and functional requirements, which guide the Asset Agent in constructing editable Blender assets. The independent Verifier checks the generated geometry and appearance against the evidence and requirements, providing targeted feedback for revision. The Simulation Integration Agent converts verified Blender assets into simulation instances with physical properties and articulated mechanisms, using execution feedback to refine their behavior. Experiments on human and robot laboratory scenes show that Lab2Sim achieves higher visual fidelity and stronger interaction capability than coding-agent and real-to-sim baselines, including a Codex baseline using the same underlying model. We further demonstrate valve-controlled drainage and use the generated asset library to reproduce laboratory scenes and operations in RoboTwin, supporting reusable simulation environments for robotic laboratory tasks.

</details>


### [78] [FreshVLA: Frequency-Regularized Time Scale Hierarchical Latent Actions for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33635)
*Jiaming Liang,Yifeng Zhan,Rongyi He,Mu Li,Jiaqi Fan,Zhiyuan Zhang,Qiwei Liang,Zhengjie Zhang,Boyang Cai,Ningyuan Zhang,Xingjian Jing,Qiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FreshVLA通过频率引导、动力学敏感的范式从无动作视频中学习时序层次化的连续潜在动作，显著提升VLA策略性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向重建的潜在动作可能编码与任务无关的静态外观，或在有限离散码中混淆缓慢变化的任务进度与细粒度执行动态，因而不利于策略学习。

Method: 采用高通先验引导视觉过渡编码聚焦动态区域；通过跨时间尺度采样与频域正则将潜在动作组织为粗粒度任务进度和细粒度执行动态；策略训练时冻结预训练潜在动作编码器，并以潜在动作与机器人动作的联合流匹配进行迁移。

Result: LIBERO平均成功率98.2%、LIBERO-Long 98.0%（超GR00T-N1.6 3.6%）；CALVIN平均序列长度4.52；零样本LIBERO-PLUS成功率80.4%；对视觉扰动更鲁棒；35步动作回归相对误差降低比层次离散方法高51.3个百分点；真实实验较π0.5平均成功率提升7.5%。

Conclusion: 频率引导的连续层次化潜在动作能更有效表示长程行为并提升机器人控制的成功率、鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning reusable latent actions from action-free videos offers an effective route to improving Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies. However, reconstruction-oriented latent actions do not necessarily benefit policy learning: as they may encode task-irrelevant static appearance with scene identity, or entangle slowly varying task progress with fine-grained execution dynamics in limited discrete code. We propose FreshVLA, a VLA framework built on a frequency-guided, dynamics-sensitive learning paradigm for acquiring temporally hierarchical continuous latent actions. FreshVLA uses a high-pass prior to guide visual transition encoding toward dynamical regions, thereby reducing reliance on static appearance cues. It further organizes latent actions into coarse-grained task progress and fine-grained execution dynamics through cross-timescale sampling and frequency-domain regularization, enhancing their capacity to represent long-horizon behaviors. During policy training, we freeze the pretrained latent action encoder and transfer its representations to robot control through joint flow-matching of latent and robot actions. FreshVLA achieves 98.2% average success on LIBERO and 98.0% on LIBERO-Long, exceeding GR00T-N1.6 on the latter by 3.6%. It also reaches an average sequence length of 4.52 on CALVIN and strong overall success rate of 80.4% in the zero-shot LIBERO-PLUS evaluation. Furthermore, FreshVLA exhibits greater robustness to visual perturbations and a more distinct division of roles between two branches in modeling low- and high-frequency motion, respectively. In 35-step action regression, it achieves a 51.3-percentage-point higher relative error reduction than its hierarchical discrete counterpart. Real-world experiments demonstrate that FreshVLA improves the average success rate over $\pi_{0.5}$ by 7.5%. The FreshVLA project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [79] [Greedy Decoding Hides Good Plans: Sample-and-Verify Planning for Vision-Language Driving Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33621)
*Zhipeng Bao,Wenjie Zhao,Yiping Li,Haohua Que,Qianwen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Sample-and-Verify（SaV）框架，在不训练VLM规划器的前提下，通过采样多候选轨迹并用轻量验证器选择，显著降低轨迹位移误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM端到端驾驶规划仅基于单一贪心解码轨迹评估，可能遗漏输出分布中更优的候选轨迹；实验显示贪心解码轨迹与最佳采样候选间存在持续性能差距，说明规划误差可能来自轨迹选择而非生成。

Method: 保持VLM规划器冻结，采样多条候选轨迹；利用真实未来轨迹训练轻量验证器，基于轨迹运动学、候选集一致性、似然度和自车运动特征估计候选相对质量并选择最终轨迹。

Result: 在WOD-E2E上，三个VLM规划器验证集ADE和FDE降低约15%，RFS变化较小（val-456上两个略微下降，Alpamayo-1.5官方测试集RFS从7.74升至7.81且ADE@5s降11%）；在NAVSIM上，三个规划器ADE@4s降低10-24%，PDMS变化不一致。

Conclusion: 轨迹选择可在不额外训练规划器的情况下显著提升位移精度，表明推理阶段的候选采样与验证是改善VLM驾驶规划的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly adopted for end-to-end driving planning, yet their performance is typically evaluated based on a single greedily decoded trajectory. However, greedy decoding may overlook better trajectories within the model's output distribution. Experiments on WOD-E2E and NAVSIM reveal a consistent performance gap between greedily decoded trajectories and the best sampled candidates, showing that planning errors can arise from trajectory selection, rather than trajectory generation alone. On WOD-E2E, when the ego vehicle should start moving, the model often assigns probability to different possible start times, causing greedy decoding to repeatedly favor waiting and produce a stationary plan even though moving trajectories are available. Motivated by these findings, we propose Sample-and-Verify (SaV), which keeps the VLM planner frozen and samples multiple candidate trajectories. A lightweight verifier is trained from ground-truth futures to estimate the relative quality of these candidates. It uses trajectory kinematics, candidate-set agreement and likelihood, and ego-motion features to select the final trajectory. On WOD-E2E, SaV reduces ADE and FDE by approximately 15% across three VLM planners on the validation set. Changes in RFS are smaller: two planners show slight decreases on val-456, while Alpamayo-1.5 improves from 7.74 to 7.81 RFS on the official test set as ADE@5s decreases by 11%. On NAVSIM, SaV reduces ADE@4s by 10-24% across three VLM planners, while PDMS changes are less consistent. These results show that trajectory selection can substantially improve displacement accuracy without additional planner training. The code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [80] [DFLAT: Model-based Diffusion on Differentially Flat Spaces for Long-Horizon Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33586)
*Ege Yuceel,Taekyung Kim,Keyvan Majd,Hideki Okamoto,Bardh Hoxha,Sayan Mitra,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DFLAT直接在微分平坦轨迹表示上扩散，去噪Hermite曲线参数而非时间离散控制，从而在保证动力学可行性的同时实现长时程快速规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程扩散规划需兼顾可扩展性与可行性，但现有扩散规划器往往牺牲其一：在时间离散控制上扩散导致维度高、可行性需事后修复。

Method: 提出DFLAT算法，在平坦输出空间中对紧凑的Hermite曲线参数（中间路点及各段端点切向量）进行去噪；每个去噪样本通过构造直接映射为动力学一致的轨迹。

Result: 在拖拉机-挂车规划基准上，DFLAT以更短规划时间达到或超过基线成功率，尤其在需多次前进-倒车机动的长时程问题上；在运动动力学RRT需超100秒的场景中，DFLAT可在亚秒级完成规划。

Conclusion: 基于微分平坦表示的扩散规划能同时保证可行性与可扩展性，使长时程运动动力学约束规划具备实际部署能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon diffusion-based planning for kinodynamically constrained systems requires both scalability and feasibility, yet existing diffusion planners typically sacrifice one for the other. We present DFLAT, a diffusion-based planning algorithm that operates directly on a differentially flat trajectory representation. Instead of diffusing over time-discretized controls, DFLAT denoises a compact set of Hermite curve parameters in the flat-output space, consisting of intermediate waypoints together with the endpoint tangents of each segment. Every denoised sample maps to a dynamically consistent trajectory, so feasibility holds by construction rather than being restored afterward. Experiments on challenging tractor-trailer planning benchmarks show that DFLAT matches or exceeds the success rates of existing baselines at consistently lower planning times, particularly on long-horizon problems requiring multiple forward-reverse maneuvers. In cases where kinodynamic RRT requires over 100 seconds, DFLAT plans in subsecond time, enabling practical deployment.

</details>


### [81] [DexEngine: Scaling Human Demonstrations into Physically Executable Whole-Robot Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33579)
*Wongyun Yu,Honggeun Kim,Donguk Lee,Minchan Kim,Kyoung Whan Choe,Minsu Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexEngine是一个仿真流水线，将人类手物演示数据转换为可用于训练、物理可执行的臂手轨迹，规模大且速度快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将人类手物演示重定向到机器人手，但训练全身机器人策略需要运动学可行、物理可执行且能大规模高效生成的重定向运动。

Method: DexEngine先将每段手物运动放置在手臂可达范围内，找到跟随人类手部的臂运动，再用残差强化学习在物理仿真中校正运动；从九个数据集为四种灵巧手各生成约5200段，扩充为65000条轨迹（116小时）和325000个回合（578小时）。

Result: 即使连接手臂，DexEngine比先前悬浮手方法更准确跟踪物体，且比基于RL的方法快10倍以上；用其数据训练的策略远超仅用相同片段（无物理校正或悬浮手校正后再接手臂）训练的策略；在相同微调预算下，中期训练进一步提升性能，仿真和真实机器人均有效。

Conclusion: 微调数据需要物理校正，DexEngine能高效生成大规模物理可执行的臂手轨迹并显著提升策略性能。

Abstract: A growing body of recent work addresses the retargeting of human hand-object demonstrations to robotic hands. To train whole-robot policies, however, the retargeted motions must be kinematically feasible and physically executable, and be produced efficiently at scale. We present DexEngine, a simulation pipeline that turns human manipulation data into train-ready, physically executable arm-hand trajectories. DexEngine first places each clip's hand-object motion where the arms can reach it, finds an arm motion that follows the human hands, and then corrects the motion in physics simulation with residual reinforcement learning. From nine human hand-object datasets, we generate about 5,200 clips for each of four dexterous hands, augmented into 65,000 trajectories (116 h) and 325,000 episodes (578 h) in total. Even with arms attached, DexEngine tracks objects more accurately than prior floating-hand methods and runs over 10x faster than RL-based ones. Policies trained on our data far outperform those trained on the same clips without physics correction or with the arms attached after a floating-hand correction, showing that fine-tuning data need physics correction. Mid-training on our data further improves them at equal fine-tuning budget, in simulation and on a real robot.

</details>


### [82] [Skeptic: Grounding Failure Diagnosis for Robot Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33560)
*Wenfang Sun,Jianghao Deng,Wenzhe Yin,Yingjun Du,Cees G. M. Snoek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Skeptic：一种免训练协议，用实测动作结果指导修复、用实测任务成功约束技能入库，减少虚假不可能判断并提升技能发现成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人代理从执行反馈修改程序来发现可复用技能，但当执行失败被误判为任务物理上不可能时，代理会提前终止探索、浪费修复预算；需要解决失败诊断与技能入库两个决策问题。

Method: 提出免训练协议Skeptic：基于深度图像和本体感觉构建有据事件日志，报告动作效果与物体终态，区分传感器观测与推断诊断；用实测动作结果指导修复，用实测任务成功控制技能准入，并在独立复现检查之外增加源程序的开发成功要求（验证晋升）。

Result: 在60个审计任务上，虚假不可能判断的平均任务数从每轮19.5降至8.5；在80个LIBERO-Pro任务和7个Robosuite任务上，冻结模型权重且程序修订预算相同的情况下，留出成功率分别提升14.9和11.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 将失败诊断建立在实测动作效果上、将技能准入建立在实测任务成功上，可减少虚假不可能判断并显著提升技能发现的留出成功率，且无需训练或增加修订预算。

Abstract: Robot agents discover reusable skills by revising programs from execution feedback and retaining experience for later tasks. This process can fail when an agent treats an unsuccessful program as evidence that the task is physically impossible, ending exploration with revision budget remaining. We study this failure in Qwen3.8-27B and examine two decisions that shape skill discovery: I) how execution failures are diagnosed, and II) which lessons enter the shared skill library. Failure diagnosis requires the agent to reconstruct physical action effects from multimodal traces, while independent reproduction of a proposed repair does not require adequate task performance from its source program. We introduce Skeptic, a training-free protocol that uses measured action outcomes to guide repair and measured task success to govern skill admission. A grounded event log reports action effects and object end states from depth images and proprioception, separating sensor observations from inferred diagnoses. Verified promotion adds a development-success requirement on the source program to the existing independent reproduction check. Across 60 audited tasks, the mean number of tasks with false impossibility judgments decreases from 19.5 to 8.5 per campaign. On 80 LIBERO-Pro tasks and seven Robosuite tasks, Skeptic improves held-out success by 14.9 and 11.7 percentage points, with frozen model weights and the same program-revision budget.

</details>


### [83] [Model Predictive Control of a Multirotor UAV with a Cable-Suspended Payload: A Comparison of Shifted and Transformer-Based Warm Starts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33514)
*Athanasios Zeris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究将Transformer预测的暖启动与常规移位初始化在缆绳悬挂负载多旋翼无人机的模型预测控制中进行对比，发现Transformer初始化在独立问题上缩短计算时间，但在闭环顺序仿真中移位初始化计算时间最低，且三者跟踪性能相近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPC求解计算量大，暖启动可加速优化；需验证基于学习的暖启动是否优于复用前序解的常规移位初始化。

Method: 采用弹性缆绳和双摆负载的耦合动力学模型，MPC包含移动阻塞控制输入、有限差分灵敏度、约束二次子问题及输入可行性投影；用专家SCP解训练Transformer预测初始控制序列，再经同一SCP优化器精化；在相同设置下比较零初始化、移位初始化和Transformer初始化。

Result: 独立求解MPC问题时，Transformer初始化比零初始化减少计算时间；在顺序闭环仿真中，移位前一优化控制序列计算时间最低，三种方法跟踪行为相似；优化时间超过控制采样周期，未实现实时。

Conclusion: 应将领学暖启动与复用前序MPC解的常规初始化方法进行对比；当前学习暖启动在闭环顺序场景下未优于移位初始化，且计算耗时超出采样周期，实时性未验证。

Abstract: This study investigates model predictive control (MPC) of a multirotor drone (unmanned aerial vehicle, UAV) carrying a cable-suspended payload, comparing conventional shifted initialization with Transformer-based warm starting of a sequential convex programming (SCP) optimizer. The controller uses a coupled mechanical model with an elastic cable and a double-pendulum representation of the suspended load. Its formulation includes move-blocked control inputs, finite-difference sensitivities, constrained quadratic subproblems, and input-feasibility projection. A Transformer trained on expert SCP solutions predicts an initial control sequence, which is subsequently refined by the same optimizer used for the conventional methods. Numerical experiments compare zero, shifted, and Transformer initialization under identical controller settings. For independently solved MPC problems, Transformer initialization reduces computation time relative to zero initialization. In sequential closed-loop simulations, however, shifting the preceding optimized control sequence provides the lowest computation time, while all three methods produce similar tracking behavior. These findings emphasize the importance of comparing learned warm starts with conventional initialization methods that reuse previous MPC solutions. The measured optimization times exceed the control sampling period; consequently, real-time implementation is not demonstrated. Keywords: Model predictive control; sequential convex programming; Transformer; warm start; cable-suspended payload; multirotor drone.

</details>


### [84] [OnCue: Generating Safety-Critical Driving Scenarios under Occlusion with Temporal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33439)
*Kailang Ma,Li Song,Weining Ren,Zirui Li,Xianjie Dai,Jingguang Li,Zuyi Guo,Benwang Chen,Heye Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OnCue框架在真实驾驶日志中生成具有指定暴露时间与参考碰撞时间的安全关键遮挡场景，用于评估和改进自动驾驶规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遮挡危险中可见性时序决定可用避让动作，现有场景生成器控制危险运动但未联合指定暴露与参考碰撞时间。

Method: 利用场景几何提供隐藏起点和定时路点生成行人、骑行者、车辆轨迹；离线响应oracle将遭遇标为Reactive或Anticipatory并存储安全机动作为训练示范。

Result: 在Waymo数据集上90.1-96.6%有效轨迹满足两个请求时间（±0.2s）；共享车辆请求中92.8%匹配反应窗口（对比基线至多42.1%）；骑行者场景窗口延长使PDM-Closed碰撞率从59.1%降至5.5%，LatentDriver呈相反趋势；示范使LatentDriver目标碰撞降低54.8个百分点但无目标进度减少12.7m。

Conclusion: 时序控制对遮挡下评估和改进规划器具有重要价值。

Abstract: Occluded hazards challenge autonomous driving because visibility timing determines available avoidance actions. Existing scenario generators control hazard motion without jointly specifying exposure and reference-contact times. We propose OnCue, a framework that generates safety-critical encounters in real driving logs with these temporal conditions. Scene geometry provides hidden starts and timed waypoints for pedestrian, cyclist, and vehicle trajectory generation. An offline response oracle labels encounters as Reactive (avoidable after exposure) or Anticipatory (avoidable only with earlier visibility) and stores safe maneuvers as training demonstrations. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, 90.1-96.6% of valid trajectories meet both requested times within +/-0.2 s. OnCue matches the requested reaction window between them in 92.8% of shared vehicle requests versus at most 42.1% for adapted adversarial generators. Across matched cyclist encounters, longer windows lower PDM-Closed collision from 59.1% to 5.5%, while the learned planner LatentDriver shows the opposite trend. OnCue demonstrations reduce LatentDriver target collision by 54.8 points on held-out Reactive tests, but reduce overall no-target progress by 12.7 m. These results highlight the value of temporal control for evaluating and improving planners under occlusion.

</details>


### [85] [An Adversarial Multi-Agent Pursuit Game for Locomotor Agility Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33407)
*Nathan A.C. Hogg,Matt Hyatt,Neil Klingensmith,George K. Thiruvathukal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MJTag任务套件，利用稀疏反馈的竞争性自博弈训练角色条件化的低层运动控制器，在多人追逐-逃避游戏中涌现出闪避、冲刺和擒抱等策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有敏捷人形运动控制器依赖密集奖励塑形或参考跟踪奖励，一旦行为最大化奖励便停止学习，且参考跟踪将运动生成与控制分离，难以适应快速变化的运动需求。

Method: 在物理模拟器中训练神经网络进行多人追逐-逃避游戏，采用角色条件化策略作为低层运动控制器并可选平面速度指令，使用联盟式自博弈与稀疏反馈进行训练。

Result: MJTag的联盟式自博弈与稀疏反馈使运动控制器习得新策略，包括闪避、冲刺和擒抱。

Conclusion: 稀疏反馈的竞争性自博弈可同时驱动运动生成与控制策略的涌现，为敏捷人形运动控制提供了一种不依赖密集奖励塑形或参考跟踪的新途径。

Abstract: Agile humanoid locomotion requires controllers that can adapt to rapidly changing motion demands while remaining stable and robust. Recent locomotion controllers are typically designed with either a dense reward that is carefully shaped to encourage steps and penalize falling, or by using a reference tracking reward which teaches the controller to follow predetermined motions. Once a behavior that maximizes the reward is discovered, the controller ceases to learn. While generally stable, reference tracking methods focus on motor control separately from motion generation. Self-play has been proven as an effective technique for superhuman performance on several multiagent games like Go, Starcraft, hide-and-seek, and capture-the-flag. We train neural networks to play the game of multiagent tag in a physics simulator to study if new motion generation and control strategies can emerge through sparse feedback and competitive self-play. We present MJTag, a task suite for role-conditioned locomotion during competitive pursuit-evasion. Our model is a role-conditioned policy for a low-level locomotion controller and optional planar velocity commands. MJTag's league-based play with sparse feedback enables a locomotion controller to adopt new strategies, including dodging, sprinting, and tackling.

</details>


### [86] [Best-of-$N$ Test-Time Steering Can Lead to Reward Hacking in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33398)
*Vanshaj Khattar,Jing Liu,Toshiaki Koike-Akino,Ming Jin,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 增大最优N采样中的候选数量并不总能提升VLA模型性能，验证器不准确时会导致奖励黑客。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在环境变化时适应能力不足，测试时引导方法虽能提升适应性，但最优N选择中验证器误差的影响尚未被充分研究。

Method: 在60个LIBERO-Safety任务级案例上用π_{0.5}策略各重复10次，向MuJoCo轨迹验证器引入受控误差，分析N增大时候选奖励与准确验证器奖励的偏离。

Result: 随着N增大，所选候选奖励偏离准确验证器奖励，出现奖励黑客；验证器校准和基于softmax的候选选择在某些设置下可缓解该问题。

Conclusion: 验证器不准确时，最优N测试时引导会因预测误差被利用而失效，需通过校准和选择策略缓解奖励黑客。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown promise for general-purpose robotic manipulation, but adapting to changing environmental conditions still remains challenging. Recent test-time steering methods have shown promise in enabling adaptive VLA policies by utilizing additional computation during deployment. In this paper, we consider best-of-$N$ test-time steering for VLA models, where multiple action candidates are sampled from a fixed policy and a verifier is used to select the best one during deployment. We show that increasing $N$ does not always improve performance when the verifier is inaccurate, and can favor candidates whose scores exploit prediction errors, a form of reward hacking. We study this effect on 60 LIBERO-Safety task-level cases using a $\pi_{0.5}$ policy, with ten repetitions per case. We introduce controlled errors in a MuJoCo trajectory verifier and show that as $N$ increases, selected candidate rewards diverge from the accurate verifier rewards, leading to reward hacking. We also show that verifier calibration and softmax-based candidate selection can mitigate reward hacking in some settings.

</details>


### [87] [RoboARC: Physical Puzzles for Robotic Manipulation under Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33390)
*Qineng Wang,Jianwen Lyu,Pingyue Zhang,Reuben Tan,Xiaoyu Huang,Tonghe Zhang,Kangrui Wang,Yelong Shen,Jianfeng Gao,Guanya Shi,Ruohan Zhang,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboARC是一个包含90个空间约束操作任务的基准，评估编码智能体与VLA策略在特权与标准两种设置下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多困难操作任务本质上是空间约束问题，依赖场景精确几何，异地演示和技能难以迁移，因此需要研究机器人能否自主推理出正确运动。

Method: 构建RoboARC基准：6类约束、刚性与软材料、3种机器人、MuJoCo仿真，共90个冻结场景任务，每个任务有物理评分器和可逐位复现的参考解；分特权设置（完整仿真状态与重置）和标准设置（真实传感器输入、单次尝试无重置）；评估7个编码智能体和4个VLA策略，均未在这些任务上训练。

Result: 最强智能体GPT-6 Astra在Codex中特权设置解决86%、标准设置解决43%；4个VLA策略零样本下18个单臂谜题全部失败；在15个特权成功但标准失败的任务上，物体位姿提升4点、零件几何提升13点，二者结合提升36点（24%到60%）；编码智能体失败运行中仅7%通过任务一半以上，标准设置53%的失败卡在单一步骤。

Conclusion: 前沿编码智能体在已知零件位置和形状时已能推理出许多任务的正確运动，但仅凭自身传感器单次尝试时最强者也解决不到一半任务。

Abstract: Many hard manipulation tasks come down to a spatial constraint that only a few motions satisfy, such as passing a part through a gap barely wider than itself or separating two interlocked parts. These motions depend on the exact geometry of each scene, so demonstrations and skills recorded elsewhere rarely transfer. We therefore ask: when manipulation hinges on spatial constraints, can robots reason out the right motions? We introduce RoboARC, a benchmark of 90 tasks in six constraint types over rigid and soft materials, on three robots in MuJoCo. Every task is a frozen scene with a physics-based grader and a verified reference solution that replays bit-identically. Each task comes in a Privileged regime, with the full simulator state and resets, and a Standard regime with real-sensor inputs (camera images and proprioception) and a single attempt without resets. We evaluate seven coding agents and four VLA policies, none trained on these tasks. The strongest agent, GPT-6 Astra in Codex, solves 86% of the tasks in the Privileged regime and 43% in the Standard regime, and the four VLA policies, run zero-shot, solve none of their 18 one-arm puzzles. On 15 puzzles that GPT-6 Astra solved in the Privileged regime and failed in the Standard regime, object poses add 4 points and part geometry 13, but both together add 36, from 24% to 60%. Only 7% of the coding agents' failed runs pass more than half of a task, and 53% of the failed runs in the Standard regime are stuck on a single step. Frontier coding agents already reason out the right motions for many of these tasks once they know where the parts are and what shape they have. From their own sensors, in a single attempt, the strongest solves fewer than half of the tasks. The website can be found at this https URL

</details>


### [88] [FOCI: Trajectory Optimization on Gaussian Splats for Gaussian Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33380)
*Mario Gomez Andreu,Carlos Martinez Cuenca,Maximum Wilder-Smith,Victor Klemm,Vaishakh Patil,Marco Hutter,Jesus Tordesillas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FOCI算法，基于3D高斯泼溅表示机器人与环境，利用高斯重叠积分构建可解释碰撞模型，直接在Gaussians上优化轨迹，实现毫秒级无碰撞轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法用保守包围盒表示机器人，低估环境可通行性，难以穿越狭窄空间；3DGS重建速度快且含空间信息，但缺少直接在其上规划轨迹的方法。

Method: 定义高斯间重叠积分作为碰撞度量，将机器人和环境均表示为高斯泼溅，支持固定/移动底座机械臂、腿足机器人、无人机等多种形态及静态/动态环境，并实现朝向感知规划。

Result: 在合成与真实高斯场景中测试，含数十万高斯的环境下可在数秒内为腿足、空中和机械臂机器人计算无碰撞轨迹，且能通过极紧窄空间。

Conclusion: FOCI以高斯重叠积分实现可解释、朝向感知的碰撞建模与轨迹优化，计算高效、适用形态广，优于保守包围盒方法。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has recently gained popularity as a faster alternative to Neural Radiance Fields (NeRFs) in 3D reconstruction and view synthesis methods. Leveraging the spatial information encoded in 3DGS, this work proposes FOCI (Field Overlap Collision Integral), an algorithm that is able to optimize trajectories directly on the Gaussians themselves. FOCI leverages a novel and interpretable collision formulation for 3DGS using the notion of the overlap integral between Gaussians. The formulation presented applies to different robot morphologies (e.g., fixed and mobile base manipulators, legged robots, UAVs) and handles both static and dynamic Gaussian-Splat environments. Contrary to other approaches, which represent the robot with conservative bounding boxes that underestimate the traversability of the environment, we propose to represent the environment and the robot as Gaussian Splats. This not only has desirable computational properties, but also allows for orientation-aware planning, allowing the robot to pass through very tight and narrow spaces. We extensively test our algorithm in both synthetic and real Gaussian Splats, showcasing that collision-free trajectories for legged, aerial and manipulator robots can be computed in seconds, even with hundreds of thousands of Gaussians making up the environment. The code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [89] [Why Does Sensor Depth Fail Learned Manipulation Policies? Finding the Failure Mode with ACT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33352)
*Inayat Rasool,Chulwoo Pack,Shabbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在仿真中分别评估深度噪声与缺失孔洞对RGB-D模仿策略的影响，发现深度覆盖度比测量精度更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-D模仿策略中深度相机的噪声与缺失值（孔洞）的相对影响尚不明确，且部分研究因噪声回避深度相机，需厘清两者作用。

Method: 用真实腕部立体相机标定传感器模型，在仿真中对三种视觉编码器的RGB-D模仿策略分别评估噪声和孔洞的影响，并考察有效性掩码与部署时孔洞的作用。

Result: 完整深度提升成功率约10个百分点，噪声达相机20倍时增益仍大部分保留；孔洞覆盖约1/6腕部图像即消除深度可检测增益；将少量像素标为无效会降低深度依赖，填补孔洞并标记有效可恢复大部分增益；仅在部署时出现孔洞会使策略性能下降，有时低于仅RGB。

Conclusion: 面向学习策略的深度相机应以深度覆盖度而非测量精度为主要评判标准。

Abstract: Learned manipulation policies, from task-specific imitation policies to vision-language-action (VLA) models, rely primarily on RGB images. While some studies report gains from sensor depth, others avoid depth cameras because of measurement noise. Depth cameras introduce both noise and missing depth values (holes), whose relative effects remain unclear. In this study, we calibrate a sensor model using a real wrist-mounted stereo camera and separately evaluate noise and holes on RGB-D imitation policies with three visual encoders in simulation. We found that complete depth improves success by approximately ten percentage points, and most of this gain persists even at twenty times the camera's noise level. In contrast, holes covering about one-sixth of the wrist image eliminate the detectable benefit of depth. The validity mask strongly influences depth reliance: marking a small fraction of pixels as invalid reduces reliance, while filling holes and marking them valid restores most of the benefit. Policies trained on clean depth also lose performance when holes appear only at deployment, sometimes falling below RGB-only performance. These findings suggest that depth cameras for learned policies should therefore be judged by depth coverage more than measurement precision.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [A Failure-Driven Data Flywheel for Post-Training Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34698)
*Yuhao Luo,Zihao Sheng,Zilin Huang,Zhengyang Wan,Boyue Wang,Ruolin Yang,Shuai Wang,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出失败驱动的数据飞轮，通过场景插入生成目标场景、评估失败率形成失败图并动态分配数据配额，实现VLA模型后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA模型难以满足新平台全部行为要求，且缺乏大规模部署数据来修复失败。

Method: 将任务相关实体插入重建的真实场景以构造缺失的目标场景；逐项评估行为要求，用失败率构建跨任务条件与难度等级的失败图，每轮重评估并给仍失败条件分配更大数据生成配额。

Result: 遮挡行人场景下五轮后训练使AutoVLA关键阶段无碰撞率从0.261升至0.579、ReCogDrive从0.390升至0.765，配对控制场景不必要制动率不超过0.013；实车无真实训练下ReCogDrive安全通过4/5对比基座1/5；停车让行场景停在线前率AutoVLA从0.100升至0.897、ReCogDrive从0.000升至0.667，顺序训练后为0.599和0.358，行人无碰撞率最多下降0.032。

Conclusion: 失败驱动数据飞轮可在有限数据下有效后训练VLA模型，细粒度评估揭示仅看无碰撞率会掩盖的行为模式。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models rarely satisfy all behavioral requirements of a new platform, and teams without a large deployed fleet lack the data to address the resulting failures. We propose a failure-driven data flywheel for post-training such models. It constructs target scenarios absent from the available data by inserting task-relevant entities into reconstructed real-world scenes, and evaluates the model against each behavioral requirement of the target task. Failure rates form a failure map across task conditions and difficulty levels, which is re-evaluated after each round and assigns larger data generation quotas to conditions where the model still fails. We instantiate the framework in autonomous driving for occluded-pedestrian emergence and yielding at stop-sign-controlled intersections, and evaluate it on two VLA models and one non-VLA planner in simulation and on a real vehicle. For occluded-pedestrian emergence, five rounds of post-training improve the critical-phase collision-free rate on the sealed test set from 0.261 to 0.579 for AutoVLA and from 0.390 to 0.765 for ReCogDrive, while the unnecessary braking rate in paired control scenarios never exceeds 0.013. On a full-scale vehicle without real-world training, post-trained ReCogDrive achieves 4 of 5 safe passages, versus 1 of 5 for the base model. Fine-grained evaluation reveals a pattern obscured by the collision-free rate alone: both VLA models learn anticipatory deceleration before reaching the occluding vehicle, while braking after pedestrian emergence remains limited. For stop-sign yielding, the stop-before-line rate rises from 0.100 to 0.897 for AutoVLA and from 0.000 to 0.667 for ReCogDrive. With sequential training from the pedestrian-capability models, it reaches 0.599 and 0.358, respectively, while the pedestrian collision-free rate decreases by at most 0.032.

</details>


### [91] [Navigent: A Geometry-Grounded Agentic Framework for Vision Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34249)
*Xinyu Zhou,Weiren Cai,Yitong Chen,Shenghai Yuan,Zihang Mu,Boyu Ma,Jiahua Dong,Yanshuo Lu,Jiaqi Bai,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Navigent是一个基于冻结多模态大模型和几何执行评论器(GEC)的视觉语言导航框架，通过动作-运动一致性和运动-地标一致性检查提升长程导航可靠性，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE上取得84.0%/86.0%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于通用多模态大模型的VLN方法虽灵活，但在长程执行中难以跟踪导航进度、检测指令与实际运动偏差、判断是否越过地标或入口，导致可靠性不足。

Method: 提出Navigent智能体框架，包含冻结MLLM和几何执行评论器(GEC)：GEC进行动作-运动一致性检查（比较测量位移与指令运动）和运动-地标一致性检查（在世界坐标中跟踪视觉锚点和入口候选）；检测到偏差时中断动作序列并触发重新观察、局部调整或基于节点的恢复；利用历史视觉记忆支持长路径进度跟踪；通过离线自改进过程分析成功/失败轨迹来优化导航提示和GEC逻辑，同时保持模型权重固定。

Result: 在R2R-CE验证未见集（1839回合）和RxR-CE验证未见集（3669回合）上，成功率分别为84.0%和86.0%，SPL分别为72.9%和69.1%。

Conclusion: 几何执行验证能有效提升智能体导航的可靠性，验证了所提框架在长程VLN任务中的价值。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) with general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offers a flexible alternative to task-specific navigation policies, while reliable long-horizon execution remains challenging. Agents must track navigation progress, detect discrepancies between commanded and actual motion, and recognize when movement has passed relevant landmarks or entrances. We present Navigent, an agentic VLN framework built around a frozen MLLM and a Geometry-Grounded Execution Critic (GEC). GEC performs two complementary checks: action-to-motion consistency, which evaluates measured displacement against commanded movement, and motion-to-landmark consistency, which tracks agent-selected visual anchors and entrance candidates in world coordinates. Detected discrepancies interrupt the remaining action sequence and trigger reobservation, local adjustment, or node-based recovery. Historical visual memory supports progress tracking across extended routes. An offline self-improvement procedure further analyzes successful and failed training trajectories to refine navigation prompts and GEC logic while keeping model weights fixed. On the R2R-CE validation-unseen split (1,839 episodes) and RxR-CE validation-unseen split(3,669 episodes), Navigent achieves 84.0%/86.0% SR and 72.9%/69.1% SPL, respectively, demonstrating the value of geometry-grounded execution verification for reliable agentic navigation. Project Page: this https URL Code: this https URL

</details>


### [92] [Ensuring Deployment-Time Safety of Neural Network Controlled Systems via Localized Certificate Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33813)
*Xiaoyang Lv,Peixin Wang,Jianhao Bai,Bai Xue,Min Zhang,Luke Ong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自适应分段屏障证书，将部署时适应转化为局部证书修复优化问题，无需全局重训练即可快速恢复神经网络控制系统的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的神经网络控制系统遇到新障碍或约束变化时，原有屏障证书失效，重新训练成本高且常失败，需要高效的部署时局部更新方法。

Method: 将部署时适应建模为局部证书修复问题，利用已学习屏障证书的结构进行快速优化；设计分阶段修复框架，从局部修复逐步过渡到策略-证书联合更新，必要时进行局部重训练。

Result: 在4个基准、5个新障碍场景中全部成功，平均数十秒完成；从头重训练多数失败；重训练成功实例上平均快17.3倍，策略性能仅下降16.2%。

Conclusion: 自适应分段屏障证书能在部署时高效恢复形式化安全保证，显著优于从头重训练，且性能退化可控。

Abstract: Barrier certificates provide formal safety guarantees for neural network controlled systems by ensuring that system trajectories avoid unsafe regions over time. However, after deployment, previously unseen obstacles or constraint changes may introduce new unsafe regions, invalidating the original certificate and potentially leading to unsafe behavior. We address this setting with adaptive piecewise barrier certificates, which preserve the original certificate and policy in unchanged regions while introducing localized updates around newly emerged unsafe regions. The central question is how to perform such updates efficiently during system execution without retraining from scratch. Our key idea is to cast deployment-time adaptation as a localized certificate repair problem and reduce it to an optimization task that leverages the learned structure of previous barrier certificates, enabling fast updates without global retraining. We further design a staged repair framework that progressively transitions from localized repair to joint policy-certificate updates and, when necessary, localized retraining. Empirical results on four benchmarks with five new obstacles show that our method succeeds in all cases and completes within tens of seconds on average, whereas retraining from scratch fails in most cases. On instances where retraining succeeds, our method is 17.3$\times$ faster on average. It incurs only 16.2% performance degradation relative to the original policy.

</details>


### [93] [WhatIf: Counterfactual World Models over Action-Grounded Causal Graphs for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33745)
*Xu Yang,Kailai Sun,Qianchuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出反事实世界模型WhatIf，通过共享潜在物理与转移噪声耦合候选未来，结合稀疏对象因子因果图与往返损失实现无标签反事实推演，并据此构建反事实优势规划CAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事实转移只能确定动作的边际未来与平均效应，无法给出个体效应或切换动作造成伤害的概率，因此需要在同一物理情境下比较不同动作的耦合结果。

Method: WhatIf将候选未来通过共享潜在物理与从事实历史推断的转移噪声耦合；每个候选动作诱导稀疏对象因子因果图描述其激活的物理通路，在共享情境下展开并锚定观测结果；采用类型条件共享函数支持可变对象数，用往返损失在无标签下实现动作不变的反事实归因。在此基础上提出CAP，按共享情境下相对现状的优势排序计划并约束伤害概率；配对优势将估计方差降低1-ρ并抵消共享模型误差；每次执行的转移提供后见优势以更新物理后验、下一计划与摊销策略，无需学习critic；并给出观测转移识别干预动力学的条件。

Result: 替换个体效应为条件均值会产生等于条件方差的均方误差；配对优势将估计方差降低1-ρ并抵消共享模型误差；成对模拟器干预评估了反事实准确性、图恢复、组合泛化与决策质量。

Conclusion: 通过耦合候选未来与共享物理的反事实世界模型，可在无标签条件下进行个体效应比较与安全规划，配对优势降低方差并抵消共享误差，后见优势支持在线更新且无需critic。

Abstract: World models predict what happens under an action, but choosing between actions requires comparing their outcomes in the same physical situation. Factual transitions alone determine action-wise marginal futures and average effects, but not individual effects or the probability that switching actions causes harm. We introduce WhatIf, a counterfactual world model for manipulation that couples candidate futures through shared latent physics and transition noise inferred from factual history. Each candidate action induces a sparse object-factor causal graph describing the physical pathways it activates. Rolling out these graphs under the shared situation anchors predictions to observed outcomes and confines action contrasts to the objects each action reaches. Shared type-conditioned functions support varying numbers of objects, while a round-trip loss enforces action-invariant abduction without counterfactual labels. We show that replacing an individual effect with its conditional mean incurs mean-squared error equal to its conditional variance, motivating comparisons between coupled futures. Building on these comparisons, Counterfactual Advantage Planning (CAP) ranks plans by their advantage over an incumbent within shared situations and constrains the probability of harm. Paired advantages reduce estimator variance by a factor of $1-\rho$, where $\rho$ is the correlation between branch outcomes, and cancel model errors shared by both branches. Each executed transition provides hindsight advantages to update the physics posterior, the next plan, and an amortized policy without a learned critic. We further establish when observational transitions identify interventional dynamics. Paired simulator interventions evaluate counterfactual accuracy, graph recovery, compositional generalization, and decision quality.

</details>


### [94] [Constraint-Aware Test-Time Scoring for LLM and VLM Candidate Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33161)
*Rangarajan Ramesh,Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应评分框架，将少数硬性检查与多项软性检查融合，用于从LLM/VLM的多个候选答案中选出最佳答案，并在四个任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有候选答案选择方法（模型自身置信度、学习判官、领域检查）各有缺陷：流畅但不安全、数学合理但物理不可行、判官高分但违反任务要求。

Method: 框架由三部分组成：生成候选的起草模型、为每个任务选择并加权检查项的配置模型、对无法由工具计算的性质进行评分的评分模型；硬性检查必须全部通过，软性检查分数融合为单一分数。

Result: 在CWEval、LIBERO（含安全变体）、LiveCodeBench、HotpotQA上实验；消融显示最大增益来自硬性检查与候选比较的结合，检索增强问答的联合正确性与安全性提升至+0.21，代码生成安全性提升至+0.28，增益随首个样本质量越差而越大；软性检查的逐任务配置层相比简单替代方案无显著增益。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了统一评分框架，并通过消融表明增益来源是有依据的硬性检查而非软性因子配置。

Abstract: When an LLM or VLM produces several candidate answers, picking the best one usually relies on the model's own confidence, a learned judge model, or a domain check. Each has a known weakness: an answer can be fluent but insecure, mathematically plausible but physically infeasible, or rated highly by a judge while quietly breaking a task requirement. We propose a scoring framework that combines a small set of hard checks every answer must pass with a wider set of softer checks whose scores are blended into one number per candidate. This adaptive scoring framework consists of three components: a drafting model that produces the candidates, a configuring model that selects and weights the checks for each task, and scoring models that rate properties no grounded tool can compute. We apply our proposed method to four tasks: writing code that is both correct and secure (CWEval), generating robot manipulation plans (LIBERO with added safety variants), passing competitive-programming hidden tests (LiveCodeBench), and answering questions from retrieved documents (HotpotQA). An ablation study isolates the contribution of each component and finds that the largest gains come from combining hard checks with candidate comparison: on tasks where the first sampled answer is often broken or unsafe, this pairing lifts joint correctness-and-safety by up to +0.21 (retrieval-augmented QA) and code-generation safety by up to +0.28 (CWEval), with the size of the gain tracking how weak the first sample is, falling to near zero or slightly negative where it is already strong. The per-task layer that selects and weights the softer checks adds no measurable value over simpler alternatives. This builds a unified scoring framework, together with an ablation that identifies the grounded hard checks, not the soft-factor configuration, as the source of the gain.

</details>


### [95] [Keep or Roll Back? Out-of-Loop Judges for Self-Improving VLA and End-to-End Embodied Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33794)
*Zilin Huang,Zhengyang Wan,Kangan Qian,Zihao Sheng,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SealedLoop评估框架，用循环外的密封评审器验证具身策略自改进的真实增益，区分评审误差与迁移差距，并在仿真和实车实验中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身策略自改进依赖循环内评审器，其证据与策略不独立，可能因评审误差或真实增益无法迁移而产生虚假提升，需要独立于改进系统的评判证据。

Method: 引入密封评审器：一个改进者不可见、不可优化的循环外评估者；仅当密封评审器上的序贯检验确认增益时才保留新策略，并限定保留更差策略的概率；在LIBERO操作、NAVSIM驾驶和真实车辆上运行同一循环，分离评审误差与迁移差距两种虚假增益来源。

Result: 操作任务中成功分类器在一代内将196个失败中的65个误判为成功；驾驶任务中在循环自身帧上增益的策略在未见城市失效，即使循环内评审完美也会保留8/15；密封检验下驾驶循环最终策略优于自身评审器（PDMS 0.674对0.649，起点0.638），对已知密封增益的12对中接受11个真实增益、59个损失中零接受；真实车辆盲测6对中强化版本均比起点版本更深入弯道。

Conclusion: 循环内证据无论多准确都不足以判断策略是否改进；SealedLoop为具身策略可信自改进提供了以改进系统无法影响的证据评判进展的基础。

Abstract: Self-improvement is now a practical recipe for embodied policies: a policy collects experience, is retrained, and is kept when a judge rates it higher. However, the judge is usually trained or chosen inside the same loop, so its evidence is not independent of the policy. We introduce SealedLoop, an evaluation framework that adds a sealed judge: an evaluator outside the loop that the improver cannot see or optimize against. A new policy is kept only when a sequential test on the sealed judge confirms the gain, with a bounded probability of keeping a worse one. Running the same loop under each judge, we separate two sources of a false gain, which we call judge error (the loop's judge is wrong on its own data) and transfer gap (a real gain there does not carry over to unseen conditions). We evaluate on LIBERO manipulation, NAVSIM driving, and a full-scale real vehicle. In manipulation, a success classifier accepted 65 of 196 failures in one generation. In driving, policies that gain on the loop's own frames lose in an unseen city, so even an exact in-loop judge would keep 8/15 of them. When the sealed test decides, a driving loop built to improve ends with a better policy than under its own critic (0.674 against 0.649 PDMS, from 0.638), and on driving pairs with known sealed gain the test accepted 11 of 12 real gains and none of 59 losses. In a real-world field experiment on a published improvement step, the reinforced version drove further into a curve than the version it started from in all 6 blind pairs, though neither completed it. These results show that evidence from inside the loop, however accurate, is not enough to decide whether a policy has improved. SealedLoop offers a foundation for trustworthy self-improvement of embodied policies, in which progress is judged by evidence the improving system cannot influence.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Not all world models can doubt: how training objective and architecture shape epistemic uncertainty in JEPA and RSSM world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34830)
*Simon Baur,Wojciech Samek,Jackie Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验比较了JEPA类世界模型LeWM与RSSM类模型DreamerV3在认知不确定性（EU）上的差异，发现RSSM通过循环更新平均化和重建目标像素加权两种机制衰减状态扰动，而JEPA无此机制，导致下游不确定性任务存在差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型的决策可靠性取决于其预测能力，而预测只在模型经验范围内可靠；认知不确定性（EU）标记经验边界，但训练目标和架构如何影响世界模型的EU尚不清楚。

Method: 在十个模拟视觉控制环境中，对两种范式代表模型（LeWM，联合嵌入预测架构JEPA；DreamerV3，循环状态空间模型RSSM）进行受控状态扰动实验，追踪扰动经过各阶段直至最终EU估计（事后EU：末层Laplace与集成），比较两种架构的EU表现。

Result: RSSM通过两种机制衰减状态扰动：循环更新对上下文平均化，使单帧变化以部分幅度到达状态且仅在变化持续时恢复；重建目标按像素覆盖加权实体，导致小物体可能因训练目标而丢失。JEPA无这两种机制，扰动贯穿所有阶段传递至不确定性。该差异导致五项不确定性相关下游任务（如陌生状态检测、不确定性惩罚规划）出现差距：RSSM不确定性遗漏了其后验已记录的偏差，而JEPA不确定性报告了模型所见。

Conclusion: 训练目标和架构显著塑造世界模型的认知不确定性；JEPA的不确定性更忠实反映模型感知，RSSM则因循环平均与重建加权而系统性低估偏差。

Abstract: World models predict how an environment responds to actions, so that candidate actions can be evaluated before execution. Executed actions, or decisions, taken in this manner are only as reliable as the model's predictive capabilities, and predictions in turn are only reliable within the model's experience. Epistemic uncertainty (EU) is the signal that marks where that experience ends. In this work we offer an extensive empirical study on how training objectives and architectures shape epistemic uncertainty of world models. More precisely, we present a controlled comparison of post-hoc EU (last-layer Laplace and ensembles) between two dominant models of different paradigms: 1) LeWorldModel (LeWM): A joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA), which predicts the next embedding without a decoder, and 2) DreamerV3: A recurrent state-space model (RSSM), which reconstructs the observation from a recurrent latent state. In ten simulated visual-control environments, we follow a controlled state perturbation of the environment state through every stage of the models up to the final EU estimate. We find that the RSSM attenuates the state perturbation through two mechanisms before it reaches an estimator on the predictor feature. Its recurrent update averages over context, so a change in a single frame reaches the state at a fraction of its size and recovers only as the change persists. Its reconstruction objective weights an entity by the pixels it covers, so a small object can be lost due to the training objective. The JEPA has neither mechanism and passes the change through all stages to the uncertainty. We further show that this translates to gaps in five uncertainty related downstream tasks, from unfamiliar-state detection to uncertainty-penalised planning: the RSSM uncertainty misses deviations that its own posterior registers, whereas the JEPA uncertainty reports what its model sees.

</details>


### [97] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Multi-UAV Data Harvesting Under Variable Team Sizes and Agent Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34818)
*Thiago de Souza Lamenza,Bruno José Olivieri de Souza,Markus Endler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: F-MADDPG是一种灵活的MADDPG变体，支持可变数量的无线传感器和UAV进行数据采集，并能应对任务中智能体的永久性故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线传感器在通信基础设施受限时难以卸载数据，UAV可移动采集数据，但为不同团队和传感器规模分别训练策略成本高，且固定规模策略对智能体故障脆弱。

Method: 提出F-MADDPG，扩展MADDPG以支持不同数量的传感器和UAV以及智能体永久性灾难故障；与使用MLP的MADDPG基线对比，并评估基于团队规模采样和智能体终止暴露的训练策略。

Result: F-MADDPG在评估的种群规模上均可学习，平均完成任务快于基线；逆基数团队规模采样优于均匀采样；在永久性智能体损失后仍有效；训练中暴露于故障未带来一致收益。

Conclusion: F-MADDPG为可变规模团队和传感器种群的数据采集提供了灵活有效的去中心化协调策略，并具备对智能体永久故障的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Collecting data from wireless sensors can be challenging when limited communication infrastructure prevents them from offloading their measurements. In such settings, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) can exploit their mobility to harvest data locally, while coordinated UAV teams can provide efficient and redundant coverage of remote areas. Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables such teams to learn decentralized coordination policies. However, training separate policies for different team and sensor populations is costly, and fixed-population policies may be vulnerable to agent failures. This work proposes F-MADDPG, a flexible variant of Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (MADDPG) that supports data harvesting with varying numbers of wireless sensors and UAVs. The framework also accommodates unexpected catastrophic failures that permanently remove agents during a mission. We compare F-MADDPG with a MADDPG baseline that uses multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) and evaluate training strategies based on team-size sampling and exposure to agent terminations. The results show that F-MADDPG learns across the evaluated population sizes, completes missions faster than the baseline on average, benefits from inverse-cardinality team-size sampling compared with uniform sampling, remains effective after permanent agent losses, and gains no consistent benefit from exposure to failures during training.

</details>


### [98] [Split-RL: Local Conflict Resolution in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34799)
*Benjamin Fuhrer,Chen Tessler,Gal Dalal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Split-RL是一种基于梯度提升树（GBT）的强化学习算法，利用引导标签在局部隔离空间和时间上相互冲突的反馈信号，避免信号干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经网络因参数共享而无法在结构上隔离不同来源的矛盾反馈，而标准树拟合过程会在叶子节点前聚合冲突梯度，导致目标特定信息丢失。

Method: Split-RL将引导标签（来自启发式、传感器事件或规则系统）整合到树构建过程中，将更新路由到不相交的状态空间区域，从而在保持学习策略的同时融入规则结构；并从理论上分析了早期梯度聚合的信息损失及Split-RL评分隔离冲突更新的条件。

Result: 在具有空间、稀有事件和时间冲突的约束RL与离线RL（混合数据集）任务中，Split-RL持续优于现有方法：在满足回合成本预算的同时保持有竞争力的奖励；离线设置下能局部化模仿与策略改进之间的权衡，在不同数据集规模下达到接近专家的性能。

Conclusion: Split-RL通过GBT的归纳偏置有效隔离局部冲突反馈，为约束RL和离线RL提供了一种优于现有方法的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) policies are often trained by mixing contradictory feedback that is localized in space and time. For example, a robot may prioritize speed in open areas versus precision in tight gaps. We introduce Split-RL, a Gradient Boosting Trees (GBT) algorithm that uses guidance labels to locally isolate these conflicting signals. Standard neural networks (NNs) lack the structural mechanism to do this, as their parameters share feedback from all sources. While GBT provides the inductive bias to explicitly partition the state-space per objective, standard tree-fitting procedures aggregate contradictory gradient signals before they reach the leaves. Split-RL leverages this bias to integrate guidance labels into tree construction, routing these updates into disjoint state-space regions and preventing signal interference. Such labels can arise from heuristics, sensor-derived events, or existing rule-based systems, allowing Split-RL to incorporate rule-derived structure while retaining a learned policy. We theoretically show how early gradient aggregation loses objective-specific information and characterize when the Split-RL score isolates conflicting updates. We focus on constrained RL and offline RL with mixed datasets, each balancing two objectives that conflict locally. Across domains with spatial, rare-event, and temporal conflicts, Split-RL consistently outperforms existing methods by meeting the episodic cost budget while maintaining competitive rewards. In offline settings, Split-RL localizes the tradeoff between imitation and policy improvement, outperforming other methods and achieving near-expert performance across varying dataset sizes.

</details>


### [99] [Distractor-Robust Reinforcement Learning via Variational Bisimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34641)
*Benjamin Freed,Vivaan Singh,Dean Zadok,Roberto Calandra,Jeff Schneider,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VIBES用对抗性目标替代像素重建，使潜在状态满足奖励与动力学充分性，从而在干扰环境下学习任务相关表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重建的目标会让模型将表示容量浪费在与任务无关的干扰因素上，降低数据效率和泛化能力。

Method: 提出VIBES目标，用对抗项替代像素重建，强制潜在状态足以预测奖励和下一潜在状态；理论证明该目标衡量编码器距离奖励与动力学充分性的程度，且全局最优诱导历史双模拟关系；结合固定容量潜在瓶颈过滤无关变化，作为Dreamer模型学习组件的即插即用替代。

Result: 在Distracting Control Suite上达到最先进性能，在Realistic ManiSkill的视觉复杂操作任务中有效，并适用于100连杆游泳机器人等高维向量状态任务；UMAP嵌入和最近邻探测表明表示对任务相关结构敏感而对干扰因素不变。

Conclusion: VIBES无需图像特定增强即可学习任务相关且抗干扰的潜在表示，适用于视觉和向量状态任务，验证了双模拟充分性目标的有效性。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) promises data efficiency and generalization, but typical reconstruction-based objectives encourage models to waste representational capacity on task-irrelevant distractors. We introduce VIBES (Variational Inference for Bisimulation-based Encoded States), a new objective that replaces pixel reconstruction with an adversarial term, which enforces that latent states suffice to predict both rewards and the next latent state. We show that this objective measures how far the encoder is from reward and dynamics sufficiency, and that whenever exact sufficiency is attainable, its global optima induce bisimulation relations on histories. Combined with a fixed-capacity latent bottleneck, this encourages latent states to retain task-relevant information while discarding irrelevant variation. Our method serves as a drop-in replacement for Dreamer's model-learning component, achieves state-of-the-art performance on the Distracting Control Suite, and extends to visually complex manipulation in Realistic ManiSkill. Unlike prior approaches, it does not rely on image-specific augmentations and applies equally well to high-dimensional vector-state tasks, demonstrated on a 100-link swimmer robot. Finally, latent-space analyses (UMAP embeddings and nearest-neighbor probes) confirm that the learned representations are sensitive to task-relevant structure while invariant to distractors.

</details>


### [100] [Inverse Reinforcement Learning with State-Dependent Foresight for Human Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34351)
*Jinil Kim,Jinwoo Jeong,Hyeongjun Yun,Min-hwan Oh,Woo-Young Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SC-AIRL通过状态条件路由器和按深度共享奖励的策略，将有限且状态依赖的前瞻性纳入逆强化学习，提升了对行人过街和公路超车任务的模仿精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准逆强化学习假设无限时域规划，而人类前瞻性有限且随状态变化，导致对自然行为轨迹的建模不准确。

Method: 提出SC-AIRL，按规划深度设置共享奖励但显式求和范围不同的策略，并用状态条件路由器根据当前状态混合这些策略。

Result: 在行人过街和公路超车任务中，SC-AIRL将top-1模仿准确率分别从0.549提升至0.647、从0.629提升至0.718，并更贴近参与者的实际行为；推断的规划深度与自报焦虑和运动冲动性相关。

Conclusion: SC-AIRL比标准AIRL更准确地复现人类行为，同时提供可解释的、模型导出的状态依赖前瞻性度量。

Abstract: Computational modeling of behavior represents individuals with a model fitted to their observed behavior and characterizes them through its parameters. Naturalistic tasks incorporate the complexity of real environments and yield high-dimensional behavioral trajectories. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) infers the latent reward function underlying these trajectories. Standard IRL, however, assumes effectively infinite-horizon planning, whereas human foresight is both bounded and state-dependent. Here we introduce SC-AIRL (State-Conditional Adversarial IRL), which builds these two cognitive properties into the planner. Per-depth policies share one reward but differ in how far ahead they sum it explicitly. A state-conditional router mixes them according to the current state. Across held-out episodes in pedestrian crossing and highway overtaking tasks, SC-AIRL improves imitation over Standard AIRL, raising top-1 accuracy from 0.549 to 0.647 and from 0.629 to 0.718, respectively, while more closely reproducing participant behavior in rollouts. Inferred planning depth correlates with self-reported anxiety and motor impulsivity. SC-AIRL thus reproduces human behavior more closely than Standard AIRL while providing an interpretable, model-derived measure of state-dependent foresight.

</details>


### [101] [SOC-Net: Second-Order-Cone-Constrained Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34136)
*Seungseok Oh,Youngjae Min,Navid Azizan,Namhoon Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOC-Net 是一种通过构造保证输入相关二阶锥约束满足的神经网络架构，可将主干输出投影到 Lorentz 锥乘积的可达切片上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多应用中神经网络需满足物理定律、安全要求等先验知识，这些约束常可表示为二阶锥形式，但现有方法依赖锥求解器、迭代方案或每个输入的可行内点。

Method: SOC-Net 将主干输出的仿射提升投影到固定 Lorentz 锥乘积的可达切片，再将修正映射回输出空间；对于可分离约束，投影分解为每个锥的投影，每个投影有闭式解或化为单个标量方程，并支持带显式雅可比的标准反向传播端到端训练。

Result: 在带摩擦约束的接触力分配和带输入鲁棒 CBF 的 3-D 避障实验中，SOC-Net 在每个评估输入上均满足约束，评估延迟低于基于求解器和迭代的基线。

Conclusion: SOC-Net 能在构造上保证输入相关二阶锥约束的满足，保持主干网络表达能力，并支持高效端到端训练。

Abstract: In many applications, neural networks must learn input-output maps that respect prior knowledge such as physical laws, safety requirements, and operating limits. These requirements constrain which outputs are admissible for a given input and often admit second-order cone (SOC) representations, including norm bounds and robust linear inequalities. Examples in control include friction cones, thrust bounds, and input-robust control barrier function (CBF) conditions. However, existing approaches to enforcing general input-dependent SOC constraints require a conic solver, an iterative scheme whose feasibility depends on convergence, or an interior feasible point for each input. We propose SOC-Net, a neural network architecture that enforces input-dependent SOC constraints by construction for every input, even outside the training distribution. SOC-Net projects an affine lifting of the backbone output onto the reachable slice of a fixed product of Lorentz cones and maps the correction back to the output space. For separable constraints, this projection splits into one projection per cone, and each is available in closed form or reduces to a single scalar equation, regardless of the cone dimension. We prove that SOC-Net retains the expressive power of the backbone network under regularity conditions and accommodates end-to-end training by standard backpropagation with an explicit Jacobian. Experiments on contact force allocation with friction constraints and 3-D obstacle avoidance with an input-robust CBF demonstrate that SOC-Net satisfies the constraints at every evaluated input, with lower evaluation latency than the tested solver-based and iterative baselines.

</details>


### [102] [Unstable Action Boundaries in Lethal Drone Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34121)
*Jacques Dumora,André-Louis Rochet,Simon Roburin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估无人机LLM代理在目标描述变化下调用致命工具的行为边界稳定性，发现无需越狱即可对普通描述发动攻击，且保护性描述下的保守性可被语义替换轻易破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型LLM代理的安全风险超出纯文本拒绝行为，尤其在军事场景下自主武器可能将武力决策委托给软件代理，需考察动作边界是否稳定。

Method: 在真实无人机影像上构建模拟感知-动作流水线，测试主流LLM能否定位目标并调用模拟致命工具，并改变目标描述符以检验行为边界。

Result: 发现两类失败模式：部分代理对“people”等普通描述直接调用致命工具；初始在保护性描述下表现保守的代理，经简单语义目标替换后变得不安全。

Conclusion: 工具型LLM代理的安全对齐在动作层面脆弱，目标描述的语义变化可绕过表面拒绝行为，需针对工具调用建立更稳健的安全边界。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents raise safety risks that are not captured by text-only refusal behavior, especially when tools can trigger high-impact actions. This concern is salient in military settings, where autonomous weapons raise the possibility of delegating force decisions to software agents. In this work, we study whether a drone agent's action boundary is stable under changes to the target descriptor. We evaluate recent and popular LLMs in a simulated perception-action pipeline over real-world drone imagery, where models can localize requested targets and invoke a simulated lethal tool. We find two concerning failure modes. First, some LLM agents invoke the lethal tool for ordinary generic descriptors such as \emph{people}, requiring no jailbreak at all. Second, an agent that initially appears conservative under protected descriptors can become unsafe under a simple semantic target shift.

</details>


### [103] [CondUQ: Calibrated Conditional Uncertainty Quantification Using Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33939)
*Shizhuang Wang,Tianhao Wang,Xingqun Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: condUQ通过将可观测因素映射到潜在质量指数(QI)，对误差模型进行条件化，实现准确可靠的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯混合模型和校准分位数无法捕捉误差随输入质量和操作条件等因素的变化，单一高斯模型描述不准确。

Method: 构建在现有系统之上（可黑盒），首先端到端训练QI预测器，将可观测因素映射到潜在质量指数，使同一QI下的误差服从近似高斯条件分布，无需质量标签；然后对每个QI拟合高斯误差模型并在大型留出数据集上校准。

Result: 在GPS多径误差、Transformer视觉相对位姿回归的误差、世界模型预测误差三个示例中，condUQ相比单一全局模型显著降低建模误差分布与经验误差分布之间的差异，且学习到的QI揭示因素对误差特性的影响与物理原理一致。

Conclusion: condUQ能够基于可观测因素实现准确、可靠的条件化不确定性量化，适用于黑盒系统且无需修改原系统。

Abstract: Many applications, especially reliability- and safety-critical ones, require accurate uncertainty quantification for the outputs of model- or learning-based measurement, estimation, and prediction systems. The errors of such systems often vary with factors such as input quality and operating conditions, and thus cannot be accurately described by a single Gaussian model. Although Gaussian mixture models and calibrated quantiles can approximate such error distributions, their unconditional forms do not capture how the error behavior depends on these factors. Hence, we propose condUQ, an approach that conditions error models on observable factors for enabling accurate and reliable uncertainty quantification. condUQ is built on top of an existing system without modifying it and applies even when the system is a black box. First, condUQ maps the observable factors to a latent quality index (QI) such that errors sharing the same QI follow a common, approximately Gaussian conditional distribution. The QI predictor is trained end-to-end with a distribution-matching objective, without requiring quality labels. Second, for each QI, a Gaussian error model is then fitted and calibrated on a large held-out dataset. condUQ is demonstrated on three examples: (a) GPS multipath errors conditioned on satellite elevation and signal strength, (b) relative attitude errors of a transformer-based visual relative pose regression model conditioned on its internal features of the image pairs, and (c) prediction errors of a world model conditioned on the input observations and actions. In all three examples, condUQ substantially reduces the discrepancy between the modeled and empirical error distributions compared with a single global model. Moreover, the learned QIs reveal how the underlying factors affect the error characteristics in a manner consistent with physical principles.

</details>


### [104] [Normalizing Flows are Capable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33872)
*Zijia Li,Yong-Hyun Park,Shusheng Yang,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出归一化流世界模型NFWM，用可逆观测编码器与动作条件概率预测器，通过最大化未来观测的精确条件似然联合训练表征与动力学，并支持潜在空间规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让智能体预测动作后果并利用想象未来做决策，同时需要一个既能精确建模观测分布、又能在潜空间高效规划的世界模型。

Method: NFWM将可逆观测编码器与动作条件概率预测器结合，使潜状态预测分布对应未来观测分布，从而以精确条件似然联合训练；智能体在潜空间规划并解码采样轨迹；目标条件控制中引入辅助逆动力学目标来塑造用于比较预测状态与目标的坐标。

Result: Teleport中采样能捕捉同一动作的两种可能结果；Flappy Bird中仅用似然训练的世界模型支持基于奖励的规划，在十局评测中均达到100根管道；LIBERO上NFWM比LeWM和SMWM更常选出成功动作序列；Atari Boxing中在想象轨迹上训练可提升行为克隆初始化的策略。

Conclusion: 结果表明归一化流可作为有能力的世界模型，兼顾精确似然建模、潜空间规划与目标条件控制。

Abstract: A world model lets an agent anticipate the consequences of its actions and use imagined futures to make decisions. We introduce Normalizing-Flow World Model (NFWM), which pairs an invertible observation encoder with an action-conditioned probabilistic predictor. Because the encoder is invertible, the predicted distribution over latent states defines a distribution over future observations. Maximizing the exact conditional likelihood of those observations therefore trains the representation and dynamics jointly. An agent can plan directly in the learned latent space and decode sampled trajectories to inspect possible futures. On Teleport, these samples capture the two possible outcomes of the same action. In Flappy Bird, a world model trained by likelihood alone supports reward-based planning that reaches 100 pipes in all ten evaluated games. For goal-conditioned control, an auxiliary inverse-dynamics objective shapes the coordinates used to compare predicted states with a goal. On LIBERO, NFWM uses this distance to select successful action sequences more often than LeWM and SMWM. In Atari Boxing, training on imagined trajectories improves a policy beyond its behavior-cloned initialization. Together, these results establish normalizing flows as capable world models.

</details>


### [105] [Mitigating Overgeneralization in RND via Spectral Target Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33850)
*Minseok Jeong,Yechan Lee,Hyewon Choi,Jeongyong Yang,SooJean Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从核理论视角分析RND的过泛化问题，将其归因于谱欠激励，并提出基于谱目标设计的RFF目标来改善新颖性信号的几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RND作为可扩展的新颖性信号存在过泛化缺陷：预测器会在数据流形之外外推随机目标，导致对陌生输入的 novelty 分数坍缩，需要理论解释与改进方案。

Method: 在GP目标模型与NTK机制预测器下，证明RND新颖性分数是交叉核残差方差，由目标协方差核与预测器NTK共同决定；据此将过泛化识别为谱欠激励，并提出谱目标设计原则，具体用带宽可控的Random Fourier Feature目标替代隐式随机网络目标，将残差信号重新分配到预测器衰减较小的模态。

Result: 在离线D4RL、在线Atari和Fetch操作基准上实验，显示新颖性判别能力和下游RL性能均有提升。

Conclusion: RND的新颖性几何可由目标核与预测器NTK联合塑造，谱目标设计能有效缓解过泛化并提升RL表现。

Abstract: Random Network Distillation (RND) is a scalable novelty signal for RL based on a fixed random target network and a trained predictor. However, it can overgeneralize: the predictor extrapolates the random target off the data manifold, causing novelty scores to collapse on unfamiliar inputs. This paper analyzes this failure through a kernel-theoretic lens. Under a GP target model and an NTK-regime predictor, we show that the RND novelty score is a cross-kernel residual variance: it is governed jointly by the target covariance kernel and the predictor NTK, rather than by either one alone. This identifies overgeneralization as spectral under-excitation. Motivated by this view, we formulate spectral target design as a principle for shaping RND novelty geometry. As a concrete instance, we replace the implicit random-network target with a bandwidth-controlled Random Fourier Feature (RFF) target, redistributing residual signal toward modes less attenuated by the predictor. Experiments on offline D4RL, online Atari, and Fetch manipulation benchmarks show improved novelty discrimination and downstream RL performance. The code for the RL implementation is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [106] [CARE-WM: Same-Horizon Self-Conditioning with Constraint-Adaptive Regularization for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34054)
*Yuchen Lin,Kai Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE-WM是一种无需额外监督、独立成本模型或架构改动的即插即用训练方法，通过让预测器在自身预测上训练并自适应校准潜在正则化强度，同时提升多步预测精度和潜在距离与任务成本的几何对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于潜在世界模型的规划需要准确的多步预测，以及能反映到目标物理距离的潜在距离；但模型训练用真实观测、规划时却依赖自身想象，且潜在距离更近不必然对应更好的任务结果。现有方法要么引入辅助训练目标（额外训练开销），要么增加任务成本读出（潜在几何仍与任务成本错位）。

Method: 提出CARE-WM：在预测器自身产生的预测上训练预测器（自预测训练），并自适应调整潜在正则化的强度；不增加额外监督、不引入单独成本模型、不改变LeWM架构和欧氏成本规划器，仅有一个超参数配置。

Result: 在单一超参数配置下，CARE-WM在所有评估的连续控制任务上均比LeWM提升平均规划成功率，并在多数任务上达到或超过LeWM家族其他方法的最佳结果；进一步分析显示开环潜在预测误差更低，潜在成本排序与物理任务成本更一致。

Conclusion: CARE-WM以即插即用方式同时满足准确多步预测和任务对齐的潜在几何两项要求，无需额外监督或架构改动，在多个连续控制任务上稳定优于LeWM及相关方法。

Abstract: Planning with latent world models requires both accurate multi-step predictions and latent distances that reflect physical proximity to the goal. However, models are typically trained on ground-truth observations but must rely on their own imaginations during planning, while closer latent distances do not necessarily correspond to better task outcomes. Existing methods resort to auxiliary training objectives or additional task-cost readouts, which respectively incur extra training overhead or leave the latent geometry itself misaligned with task cost. We introduce CARE-WM, a drop-in training method that meets both requirements without additional supervision, a separate cost model, or any change to LeWM's architecture and Euclidean-cost planner. CARE-WM trains the predictor on its own predictions and adaptively calibrates the strength of latent regularization. With a single hyperparameter configuration, CARE-WM improves mean planning success over LeWM across all evaluated continuous-control tasks and matches or exceeds the best results of other LeWM-family methods on most tasks. Further analyses show lower open-loop latent prediction error and closer agreement between latent-cost rankings and physical task costs.

</details>


### [107] [Fast Muscle-Driven Whole-Body Motion Tracking with a Differentiable World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33460)
*Kaibo He,Maciej Bakowicz,Shihe Zhou,Yusen Feng,Yanan Sui,Dhanush Radhakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SuperMimic 通过在世界模型 rollout 上反向传播训练全身肌肉骨骼运动跟踪策略，作者针对肌肉激活动力学改造训练配方，使 700 肌肉模型训练提速 37 倍、354 肌肉模型提速 34 倍，并在测试时用世界模型精修动作进一步降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身肌肉骨骼模型运动跟踪需协调数百块肌肉并处理接触，现有控制器训练需数小时到数天 GPU 时间，每次调参代价高昂，因此需要大幅加速训练。

Method: 将 SuperMimic 扩展到 MS-Human-700（700 肌肉）和 MyoFullBody（354 肌肉），在世界模型中嵌入 MuJoCo 激活动力学；训练配方沿用 Unitree G1 的加速技巧，替换其中三个在肌肉上失效的设置，并新增四项改动：去除肌肉几何的观测、数据采集与更新并发、保持激励在 [0,1] 的 Beta 探索噪声、50 Hz 控制；另在测试时通过世界模型精修策略动作。

Result: 单张 RTX 5090 上，MS-Human-700 用 6.3 分钟达到 SuperMimic 发布超参 3.9 小时的留出回报（快 37 倍），20 分钟跟踪误差低于其 10 小时结果；MyoFullBody 无需重调即快 34 倍。消融显示更高策略学习率使半数运行停滞，裁剪高斯噪声引入持续性共收缩而 Beta 噪声可避免；测试时精修使位置误差再降 26%，无留出片段失败，且 5.1 分钟即达相同回报。

Conclusion: 针对肌肉激活动力学的训练配方能数量级加速全身肌肉骨骼运动跟踪策略训练，Beta 探索噪声与测试时世界模型精修是其中关键且可迁移的改进。

Abstract: Tracking motion capture with a whole-body musculoskeletal model means coordinating hundreds of muscles through contact, and current controllers take hours to days of GPU time to train, so every tuning decision is expensive. SuperMimic trains a tracking policy by backpropagating through the rollouts of a learned world model. We extend it to MS-Human-700 (700 muscles) and MyoFullBody (354 muscles), with MuJoCo's activation dynamics inside the world model. To make its training fast, our recipe takes the speedups developed for the Unitree G1, replaces three of their settings that fail on muscles, and adds four changes of our own: observations without muscle geometry, data collection concurrent with the updates, Beta exploration noise that keeps every excitation in $[0,1]$, and 50 Hz control. On one RTX 5090 it trains MS-Human-700 in 6.3 minutes to the held-out return that SuperMimic's published hyperparameters reach in 3.9 hours, 37$\times$ sooner, and in 20 minutes to lower tracking errors than theirs after 10 hours. It trains MyoFullBody 34$\times$ sooner without retuning. Ablations show what is specific to muscles: a higher policy learning rate stalls half of the runs, and clipped Gaussian noise adds a tonic co-contraction that Beta noise avoids. Finally, refining the policy's actions through the world model at test time lowers the position error by a further 26% with no held-out clip failing, and reaches the same return after 5.1 instead of 6.3 minutes of training. Code, the training recipe and videos are on the project page this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [BOP-Refer: Benchmark for Monocular 3D and 2D Object Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34862)
*Tomas Hodan,Vineet Bhat,Martin Sundermeyer,Jonathan Tremblay,Yash Patel,Médéric Fourmy,Stephen Tyree,Junwen Huang,Varun Burde,Martin Cífka,Eric Brachmann,Vincent Lepetit,Stan Birchfield,Jiri Matas,Carsten Rother*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个基于BOP数据集的单目2D/3D目标定位基准BOP-Refer，评测17个前沿VLM发现2D定位较好但3D深度估计误差大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在单目2D和3D目标定位上的能力缺乏统一、系统的评测基准，尤其是针对日常与工业物体、且具备准确3D真值的场景。

Method: 从9个BOP数据集中选取图像，配对由VLM生成并经人工校验的指代表达，并利用物体3D模型和6DoF位姿标注推导出准确的2D与3D包围框真值；对17个主流专有与开源VLM（Gemini、GPT、Claude、Qwen、Gemma）进行系统评测。

Result: 最佳模型Gemini 4 Carbon在2D上达到72.6% AP_IoU2D，但在3D上仅12.4% AP_IoU3D，尽管3D的IoU阈值更宽松；预测的3D框在图像中通常以目标为中心，但深度误差常超过物体自身尺寸。

Conclusion: 度量尺度估计（依赖可靠的物体尺寸先验）是目前3D定位的主要误差来源，当前VLM在单目3D目标定位上仍存在显著瓶颈。

Abstract: We introduce BOP-Refer, the first benchmark for monocular 3D and 2D grounding of everyday and industrial objects sourced from 9 diverse BOP datasets. We pair images from these datasets with VLM-generated and human-verified referring expressions, and derive accurate ground-truth 3D and 2D bounding boxes of the referred objects from their 3D models and 6DoF pose annotations. Our systematic evaluation of 17 frontier VLMs across the major proprietary and open-weight families (Gemini, GPT, Claude, Qwen, Gemma) shows that the best model, Gemini 4 Carbon, reaches 72.6% AP_IoU2D in 2D but only 12.4% AP_IoU3D in 3D, despite the more lenient IoU thresholds in 3D. The predicted 3D boxes are usually centered on the target objects in the image but often off in depth by more than the object's size, suggesting that metric scale estimation, which needs reliable object size priors, is currently by far the main source of error.

</details>


### [109] [What a Drop Reveals: Sim-to-Real Material Estimation for Deformable Objects from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34894)
*Ziyue Peng,Yanxi Hu,Junyoung Kim,Andrew Chen,Jin Yang,Chandrajit L. Bajaj,Etienne Vouga,William T. Freeman,Qixing Huang,Brandon Y. Feng*

Main category: cs.CV
