TL;DR: 从单段真实跌落视频中估计可变形物体的密度归一化刚度和屈服应力，提出PhysReader预测器，发现2D光流轨迹比3D轨迹在真实域迁移更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实视频无材料标签，现有预测器只能在合成数据上验证，但合成验证的几何输入排序在真实域失效，需研究何种感知测量能在两域一致地反映材料属性。

Method: 训练仅基于仿真的前馈预测器；对比3D变形轨迹与短2D光流轨迹；PhysReader读取视频和2D轨迹，在仿真中学习向3D的提升以匹配可见精确3D变形的教师模型；构建PhysReader-Real数据集（16个日常物体各跌落5次，参考材料通过联合拟合并对照拉伸测试校验）。

Result: 3D轨迹在仿真中最准但在真实跌落中最差，且更好深度无法修复；2D光流轨迹在仿真中无提升却迁移最佳。PhysReader在PhysReader-Real上比所有其他输入和基线更接近参考材料；仅每场景优化器略优，但耗时49分钟/视频而非12秒。

Conclusion: 材料属性应从两域感知一致测量的信息中读取，而非仿真中最具信息量的信息；PhysReader实现了快速且迁移性好的真实视频材料估计。

Abstract: How an object deforms when it hits the ground reveals what it is made of. From a single real video of a drop, we estimate a deformable object's density-normalized stiffness $E/\rho$, and its yield stress $\sigma_Y/\rho$ when the impact causes plastic deformation, with a feed-forward predictor trained only in simulation. Real videos carry no material labels, so such predictors are developed and validated on synthetic data alone. We show that synthetic validation ranks their geometric inputs in the wrong order. Material acts through 3D deformation, and tracklets lifted to 3D with estimated depth are indeed the most accurate input in simulation. However, on real drops they are the least accurate, and better depth does not repair them: the failure persists with simulator depth in training, LiDAR depth at test time, and exact depth on rendered replays of the real drops. Short 2D optical-flow tracklets do the opposite: they improve nothing in simulation, yet they transfer best. Material is therefore better read from what perception measures consistently in both domains than from what is most informative in simulation. Our predictor, PhysReader, reads the video and 2D tracklets and learns the lift to 3D in simulation by matching a teacher that sees exact 3D deformation. To measure transfer, we introduce PhysReader-Real: 16 everyday objects dropped five times each, with reference materials fit jointly to each object's drops and checked against tensile tests. On PhysReader-Real, PhysReader is closer to the reference materials than every other input and baseline. The one exception is a per-scene optimizer started from PhysReader's prediction, which ends slightly closer but takes 49 minutes per video instead of 12 seconds.

</details>


### [110] [WorldInteract: Controllable Streaming Video Generation for Multi-Material Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34730)
*Ahmed Hatem,Caoliwen Wang,Robert Hoang,Siyuan Chen,Spencer Szabados,Zike Wu,Xiyang Tan,Shenyifan Lu,Rafael Bischof,Bernd Bickel,Renjie Liao,Peter Yichen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldInteract是一个流式视频生成框架，将跨材质交互分解为材质相关行为生成与接触响应计算，通过共享速度增量接口复用无交互视频中学到的材质行为，并用物理控制器预测接触与速度增量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型需要捕捉不同材质的运动与交互，但现有可控视频生成对材质相关动态探索不足，难以在多样材质场景中实现可靠控制。

Method: 将跨材质交互分解为材质相关行为生成和接触响应计算；外部推力和接触冲量通过共享速度增量接口表达；物理控制器根据估计质量和恢复系数预测接触并计算速度增量，因果生成器利用学到的材质行为执行增量；在3万段覆盖22类材质的无交互仿真视频上训练生成器，并引入Material-Aware RL，用可验证物理奖励优化材质行为与控制执行。

Result: 在Physics-IQ上显著优于现有最先进方法，并在真实图像上实现更可靠的推力控制。

Conclusion: 分离材质行为学习与接触响应计算，可在无需覆盖多样材质配对和接触配置的监督接触视频情况下实现跨材质交互，并提升可控视频生成的物理可靠性。

Abstract: Video world models aim to create interactive physical worlds that capture how diverse materials move and interact. Yet material-dependent dynamics remain underexplored in controllable video generation, limiting reliable control in scenes with different materials. We present WorldInteract, a streaming video generation framework that decomposes cross-material interaction into material-dependent behavior generation and contact response computation. External pushes and contact impulses are expressed through a shared velocity-increment interface, allowing material behaviors learned from interaction-free videos to be reused in interacting scenes. A physics controller predicts contacts and computes velocity increments from estimated masses and restitution coefficients, while a causal generator executes these increments using learned material behaviors. This separation enables cross-material interaction without supervised contact videos spanning diverse material pairings and contact configurations. We train the generator on 30k simulated interaction-free videos spanning 22 material classes and introduce Material-Aware RL, using verifiable physical rewards to refine material behavior and control execution. WorldInteract substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods on Physics-IQ and delivers more reliable push control on real-world images.

</details>


### [111] [ShowNow: Fine-Grained Speculative Streaming for Responsive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34693)
*Yiren Song,Guian Fang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShowNow 提出细粒度推测流式框架，利用播放期计算预先生成动作条件未来帧，将输入到显示中位延迟从 620ms 降至 208ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型需持续生成视频并及时响应用户动作，但仅靠实时吞吐无法解决输入延迟：粗粒度生成块延迟动作更新，输入后生成又把工作加入响应路径。

Method: 采用单潜变量承诺实现高频决策，可配置准备覆盖1至8个动作；构建匹配多未来数据集训练同一状态下的替代延续，通过共享历史树执行与融合承诺提升准备效率；在单张 H100 上部署5B模型。

Result: 480p 生成达16fps并准备8个未来；ViZDoom 中中位输入到显示延迟由620ms降至208ms；匹配策略实验将重启空 tick 从1.56%降至0.09%（94%），并提升对动作变化的准时响应；同时给出视觉保真度与长时一致性的权衡。

Conclusion: 细粒度承诺与提前准备具有互补作用，ShowNow 将播放期计算转化为响应式视觉交互。

Abstract: Interactive world models must generate video continuously while responding promptly to user actions. Real-time throughput alone leaves input latency unresolved: coarse generation blocks delay action updates, and generation after input adds work to the response path. We introduce ShowNow, a fine-grained speculative streaming framework that uses computation available during playback to prepare action-conditioned futures before input arrives. Single-latent commitment enables frequent decisions, while configurable preparation covers one to eight actions. We construct a matched multi-future dataset to train alternative continuations from a common state; shared-history tree execution and fused commitment make their preparation efficient. On a single H100, our 5B model sustains 480p generation at 16 fps with eight prepared futures. In ViZDoom, the complete ShowNow deployment reduces median input-to-display latency from 620 to 208 ms relative to the native-block SolarWM-FT deployment. Matched-policy experiments reduce restart empty ticks from 1.56% to 0.09% (94%) and improve on-time responses to action changes. These results establish complementary roles for fine-grained commitment and advance preparation, with measured trade-offs in visual fidelity and long-horizon consistency. ShowNow turns playback-time computation into responsive visual interaction. Project: this https URL .

</details>


### [112] [Commit2Drive: Perception-Grounded Language Decisions as Commitments for Trajectory Planning in Vision-Language-Action Driving Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34614)
*Zikai Zhang,Hubert P. Shum,Toby Breckon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Commit2Drive框架，将语言决策作为轨迹规划的一致性约束，通过机动库和自动数据管线训练，在Bench2Drive上达到DS 78.50、SR 55.91%，显著降低语言-动作不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA自动驾驶模型中语言决策与轨迹相互独立或仅用于候选轨迹选择，标注未与轨迹验证，导致闭环驾驶中二者常不一致。

Method: 将决策定义为带连续参数的机动类别，由确定性规则从任意轨迹读取并基于自感知事实；用Maneuver Bank映射为具运动学含义的代码；通过一致性损失训练规划器执行场景中所有可采纳决策；自动数据管线从专家未来运动标注决策、用反事实执行联合训练、并由规则函数与VLM生成审核精炼语言标注。

Result: 使用0.5B语言模型，在单张消费级显卡上在线闭环推理；Bench2Drive上DS 78.50、SR 55.91%，与最先进方法具竞争力，并大幅降低语言-动作不一致。

Conclusion: Commit2Drive通过将语言决策作为轨迹规划的一致性约束，实现了轻量、可扩展至规则决策且无需重训的VLA自动驾驶，有效提升了语言与动作的一致性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models introduce language reasoning into end-to-end autonomous driving. They output a language decision together with a trajectory, which makes the planner more interpretable. However, in existing methods the language decision is either an independent output or an index that selects among similar candidate trajectories, and its annotations are not verified against the trajectory. As a result, the language decision and the trajectory are often inconsistent in closed-loop driving. To address this, we propose Commit2Drive, a framework that imposes the language decision as a consistency constraint on trajectory planning. The decision is a commitment, a maneuver class with a continuous parameter that one deterministic rule reads from any trajectory, and it is grounded in facts reported by the perception of the model itself. A Maneuver Bank maps the commitment to a code with a kinematic meaning, and consistency losses train the planner to execute every admissible decision of a scene, so that the trajectory follows the decision. An automatic data pipeline supplies the supervision. Decisions are labeled from the future motion of the expert, counterfactual executions train the decision and the trajectory together, and rule-based functions with a Vision-Language Model (VLM) generate, review and refine the language annotations. The code is a lookup that involves no hidden state of the language model, so Commit2Drive is extensible to rule-based decisions, which the same planner executes without retraining. Commit2Drive uses a 0.5B language model and runs closed-loop inference online on a single consumer graphics card. On Bench2Drive, Commit2Drive reaches a Driving Score (DS) of 78.50 and a Success Rate (SR) of 55.91% with a lightweight model, which is competitive with the state of the art, and greatly reduces the language-action inconsistency. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [113] [CADENCE: Sensor-GroundedWorld-Consistent Gaussian SLAM for Collaborative Mobile Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34549)
*Reshad Ul Karim,Abrar Samin,Md Rashad Al Hasan Rony*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CADENCE协作高斯SLAM系统，分离跨设备匹配与地图更新决策，在八栋房屋和iPhone扫描上验证了鲁棒性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级手机协作重建可加速室内地图构建，但异构设备导致退化观测和不可靠跨设备关联，单个差贡献者或错误匹配会污染共享高斯地图。

Method: CADENCE将跨设备匹配发现与是否及如何更新地图的决策分离：用传感器锚定的区域记忆汇总已提交观测，基于外观、相对位姿、重力、气压高度和观测质量对候选区域排序；经确定性几何验证和可选物理检查接受匹配；接受后，基于不同线索的置信度分别控制其对位姿约束、几何、外观和记忆的贡献；本地跟踪与建图、验证、网络隔离。

Result: 在未参与记忆训练的八栋Matterport3D房屋上，区域记忆AUROC为0.95-0.97，优于外观检索，误链接率低于0.5%；软置信度加权在八种退化下将平均PSNR损失从2.66 dB降至1.05 dB；在十二个留出iPhone扫描上平均绝对轨迹误差为6.98 cm，精化地图平均渲染质量优于基线。

Conclusion: CADENCE通过解耦匹配与更新决策及多线索置信度加权，有效提升异构设备协作高斯SLAM的鲁棒性、定位精度与建图质量。

Abstract: Collaborative reconstruction with consumer phones promises faster and more complete indoor maps. However, heterogeneous devices introduce degraded observations and unreliable cross-device associations, so a single poor contributor or incorrect match can corrupt the shared Gaussian map. To address this, we propose \textbf{CADENCE}, a collaborative Gaussian simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) system that separates finding cross-device matches from deciding whether and how peer observations update the map. To find matches, a sensor-grounded regional memory summarizes committed observations and ranks candidate peer regions using appearance, relative pose, gravity, barometric altitude, and observation quality. Deterministic geometric verification and optional physical checks then decide which of the proposed matches are accepted. Once a match is accepted, separate cue-based confidences control how its observations contribute to pose constraints, geometry, appearance, and memory. As a result, useful depth can still contribute even when color is unreliable. Throughout this process, local tracking remains isolated from mapping, verification, and networking. We evaluate CADENCE on eight Matterport3D houses that were not used to train the memory. On these houses, regional memory achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of $0.95$ to $0.97$ and thus outperforms appearance retrieval, while verification keeps false-link rates below $0.5\,\%$. Furthermore, soft confidence weighting reduces the mean receiver peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) loss from $2.66$ to $1.05$\,dB across eight degradations while retaining useful peer depth. Finally, using inertial and platform motion, CADENCE tracks all twelve held-out iPhone scans with a mean absolute trajectory error of $6.98$\,cm, and its refined maps achieve better average rendering quality than the baselines.

</details>


### [114] [Most of the Way There: Turning a Strong Video Prior into an Action-Conditioned World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34435)
*Zanyi Wang,Xia Chen,Liu Liu,Xianglong Guo,Dengyang Jiang,Haodong Li,Tingyang Xiao,Wenhao Hu,Zhizhong Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Dianjing在冻结的33B视频基础模型上仅训练0.34%参数，通过统一动作条件化与高噪声层集中训练，以约65小时机器人视频实现动作条件世界建模，并在WorldOracle五条评测赛道中四条取得最佳成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频生成器擅长按文本和图像提示生成视觉上合理的未来，但其对语义合理性的侧重无法保证忠实反映特定动作的物理后果；动作条件世界模型需模拟世界对机器人动作的响应以支持可扩展的策略评估与改进，而核心挑战在于预测特定物理动作的后果，而非仅生成看似合理的未来。作者据此追问：在视频先验日益强大的背景下，弥合这一差距究竟需要多少适配。

Method: 提出Dianjing最小化配方：保留并释放强视频先验能力，采用统一物理动作条件化、将训练集中在决定运动与布局（即动作后果）的高噪声水平、以及原生历史条件化；基于33B视频基础模型MiniMax H3，冻结预训练骨干，仅训练0.34%参数，使用约65小时、两种本体的机器人视频，无需额外机器人专用预训练或相机标定；并通过受控消融比较训练在噪声水平上的分配与动作进入网络的方式。

Result: 受控消融显示，训练在噪声水平上的分配位置比动作如何进入网络更重要；在WorldOracle（以配对真实执行评估预测）上，Dianjing在五条评测赛道中的四条取得最佳分数，在AgiBot-Long上排名第二。

Conclusion: 强视频先验已把世界建模推进了大部分路程，轻量级物理动作接地即可释放其用于真实世界预测的潜力。

Abstract: Modern video generators excel at producing visually plausible futures guided by text and image prompts. Yet their emphasis on semantic plausibility does not guarantee fidelity to the physical consequences of specific actions. Action-conditioned world models (ACWMs), in contrast, aim to simulate how the world responds to robot actions, enabling scalable policy evaluation and improvement without costly real-world rollouts. The challenge is not merely to generate a plausible future, but to predict what follows from a specific physical action. As video priors grow stronger, we ask how little adaptation is truly needed to bridge this gap. We introduce Dianjing, a minimal recipe that preserves and unlocks the capabilities of strong video priors through unified physical action conditioning, training concentrated at the high noise levels where motion and layout, and thus the consequences of actions, are decided, and native history conditioning. Built upon MiniMax H3, a 33B video foundation model, Dianjing freezes the pretrained backbone and trains only 0.34% of its parameters using approximately 65 hours of robot video across two embodiments, without additional robot-specific pretraining or camera calibration. Controlled ablations show that where training is allocated across noise levels matters more than how actions enter the network. On WorldOracle, which evaluates predictions against paired real-world executions, Dianjing achieves the best scores on four of the five evaluation tracks and ranks second on AgiBot-Long. Our findings suggest that strong video priors already bring us most of the way to world modeling; lightweight physical action grounding can unlock their potential for real-world prediction.

</details>


### [115] [MoCA4D: Monocular 3D Bounding Box Lifting and Tracking with Camera and Object Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34370)
*Changwoo Jeon,Jongyoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoCA4D框架通过分离相机运动与物体刚体运动，并利用星优化从单目视频中提升并跟踪3D边界框，在多个数据集上显著降低中心运动误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目3D边界框跟踪需同时处理物体运动与相机视角变化；逐帧框提升加时间滤波使运动分量隐式，静态场景融合假设物体静止，均不理想。

Method: 提出MoCA4D，包含观测分支估计逐帧几何、动态分支预测跨物体共享的相机变换及每物体独立刚体变换；用星优化结合观测框与历史框经变换传播来精化当前深度和朝向，精化框再指导后续传播。

Result: 在Argoverse 2和ADT上，星优化相比同观测的卡尔曼滤波将中心运动误差分别降低38.1%和38.7%；在MOVi-F八次转换运动窗口上，动态分支取得最低角点和旋转误差及最高IoU；真实数据仅相机控制实验表明预测物体运动可改善动态物体轨迹定位。

Conclusion: 显式分离相机与物体运动并结合星优化能有效提升单目3D框提升与跟踪精度，验证了运动分解与历史传播精化策略的有效性。

Abstract: Monocular 3D bounding box tracking must account for both object motion and changes in camera viewpoint. Framewise box lifting with temporal filtering leaves these motion components implicit, while static-scene fusion assumes stationary objects. We introduce MoCA4D, a framework for 3D box lifting and tracking from monocular video, associated 2D box prompts, and camera intrinsics. An observation branch estimates per-frame geometry, while a dynamic branch predicts a camera transformation shared across objects and a separate rigid transformation for each object. We propose Star Optimization to refine current depth and orientation using the observation and historical boxes propagated through these transformations. The refined box then guides subsequent propagation. Across all evaluated objects in Argoverse 2 and ADT, Star Optimization reduces center motion error by 38.1% and 38.7%, respectively, relative to Kalman filtering of the same observations. On MOVi-F, the dynamic branch achieves the lowest corner and rotation errors and the highest IoU among the evaluated methods over eight-transition motion windows. Real-data camera-only controls further link predicted object motion to improved localization on dynamic-object tracks.

</details>


### [116] [One Patch, Many Policies: Object-Associated Physical Patch Attacks on Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34360)
*Yiqian Liu,Yuqi Lin,Hui Lu,Jiaming He,Qi Zhu,Deyi Ji,Bing Li,Kaizheng Wang,Zhiyuan Zha,Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OAPA，一种通用可迁移的物理补丁攻击框架，通过贴片位置选择干扰哪个任务物体的操作，无需重新优化补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA和WAM模型易受对抗性视觉干扰，需研究通用且可迁移的物理补丁攻击。

Method: OAPA结合物体关联放置、可微分物理合成（考虑多相机视角下的透视与遮挡）以及最终动作专家表示散度（用相对RMS或余弦距离引导补丁优化）。

Result: 在任务套件和策略上表现出强攻击迁移性，并能通过贴片放置选择性干扰攻击者选定任务物体的操作。

Conclusion: OAPA实现了通用、可迁移且可选择性定向的物理补丁攻击，暴露了VLA/WAM模型的对抗脆弱性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and World Action Models (WAMs) are vulnerable to adversarial visual interference, motivating the study of universal and transferable physical patch attacks. We propose Object-Associated Patch Attack (OAPA), a universal and transferable physical patch framework that enables an attacker to select which task object's manipulation to disrupt through patch placement, without re-optimizing the patch. Our framework combines object-associated placement, differentiable physical compositing, and final action-expert representation divergence. Physical compositing accounts for perspective and occlusion across camera views, while representation divergence guides patch optimization using either relative RMS or cosine distance. Experimental results demonstrate strong attack transferability across task suites and policies, together with the ability to selectively disrupt manipulation of an attacker-chosen task object through patch placement.

</details>


### [117] [AIRIS: Benchmarking Social Embodied Intelligence in Egocentric Streaming Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34347)
*Yuchen Zhou,Hongsheng Wu,Jiayu Tang,Yunxuan Mao,Hanjing Ye,Shenghao Zhang,Jing Tang,Chun-Kai Fan,Yufei Huang,Yonggen Ling,He Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出因果流式基准AIRIS，将社交具身智能建模为时间锚定的注意-意图-反应-交互（A-I-R-I）链，并评估45个模型，发现其能力碎片化且时间校准差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准孤立评估社交能力或使用完整视频，无法揭示在线社交决策的失败点；需要因果流式基准来诊断智能体何时证据充分、是否干预及如何合规响应。

Method: 构建AIRIS基准：基于357个自我中心视频片段、11个上下文类别和36个能力维度，由专家策划8000个问题；每个查询仅提供当时可用的观测前缀，采用决策感知指标奖励校准等待和完整反应、惩罚过早承诺；评估45个专有与开源通用、具身、流式视频及全模态模型。

Result: 模型能力碎片化且时间校准差：通用视觉语言模型擅长被动线索 grounding 和意图识别，具身专家在孤立反应子决策上领先；但所有模型常证据不足即猜测意图，且无法在同一片段内联合对齐干预时机、动作域和具体执行。

Conclusion: AIRIS为弥合被动社交感知与可靠具身交互之间的差距提供了诊断基础。

Abstract: Embodied agents operating around people must not only recognize social cues, but also decide when the available evidence is sufficient, whether to intervene, and how to respond without violating social norms. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate these abilities in isolation or with access to completed videos, obscuring where an online social decision fails. We introduce AIRIS, a causal streaming benchmark that formulates social embodied intelligence as a time-anchored chain of Attention, Intention, Reaction, and Interaction (A-I-R-I). AIRIS contains 8,000 expert-curated questions grounded in 357 egocentric clips across 11 context categories and 36 capability dimensions. At each query, models receive only the observation prefix available at that time; decision-aware metrics reward calibrated waiting and complete reactions while penalizing premature commitment. Evaluating 45 proprietary and open-source general-purpose, embodied, streaming-video, and omni-modal models reveals fragmented and temporally miscalibrated competence. General-purpose VLMs excel at passive cue grounding and intent recognition, whereas embodied specialists lead on isolated reaction sub-decisions; yet across all families, models frequently guess intents before sufficient evidence emerges and fail to jointly align intervention timing, action domain, and concrete execution within the same episode. AIRIS thus provides a diagnostic foundation for bridging the gap between passive social perception and reliable embodied interaction.

</details>


### [118] [EgoMyo: Physical Hand State Estimation from Egocentric Video and Wrist Electromyography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34224)
*Guanglong Sun,Kanglei Zhou,Ziteng Wang,Nana Wang,Hengkai Tan,Xiaolong Liu,Hang Su,Yi Zhong,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出从第一视角视频与手腕表面肌电（sEMG）联合估计手部物理状态（配置、接触、力），构建了EgoMyo基准并提出MyoBridge方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭外观难以区分接触状态与作用力，而物理手交互依赖配置、接触与力三要素，需融合视觉几何与神经肌肉证据。

Method: 构建EgoMyo基准（575万帧、50名被试、34种接触配置，同步3D手姿与裸手压力标注的接触及法向力）；提出MyoBridge，以手部区域为视觉-sEMG融合单元，各区域自门控sEMG份额，推理时仅在预测接触顶点分配区域力幅值。

Result: 受控分析表明视觉解析空间结构、sEMG更擅长估计力大小，sEMG增益集中于视觉最不可靠区域，接触定位限制力精度；在留出试验上，MyoBridge较最强基线位姿误差降低39.8%、力误差降低18.5%，并在未见会话与受试者上取得最低位姿误差。

Conclusion: 视觉与sEMG互补融合可有效估计物理手状态，以区域为单元的门控融合与接触约束力分配显著提升位姿与力估计精度。

Abstract: Physical hand interaction depends on configuration, contact, and force, yet nearly identical appearances can conceal different contact states and exerted forces. We therefore formulate physical hand state estimation from egocentric video and wrist surface electromyography (sEMG), combining visual geometry with neuromuscular evidence to jointly recover these coupled components. To support this task, we introduce EgoMyo, a benchmark spanning 5.75M video frames, 50 participants, 34 contact configurations, and repeated donnings, synchronizing these inputs with 3D hand pose and with contact and normal force registered onto the hand surface from bare-hand pressure. Controlled analysis reveals that vision resolves spatial structure while sEMG better estimates force magnitude, that sEMG's gains concentrate in regions where vision is least reliable, and that contact localization limits force accuracy. Guided by these findings, we propose MyoBridge, which makes the hand region the unit of vision-sEMG fusion, lets each region gate its own share of sEMG, and distributes regional force magnitudes only over predicted contact vertices at inference. On held-out trials, MyoBridge reduces pose and force error by 39.8% and 18.5% over the strongest baseline and achieves the lowest pose error on unseen sessions and subjects.

</details>


### [119] [Variation-aware Temporal Correspondence for Self-Supervised Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34220)
*Yang Liu,Qianqian Xu,Peisong Wen,Siran Dai,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VT-CoRe框架，通过夹层采样、块注册模块和自蒸馏架构解决视频掩码建模中的时序对应与语义一致性/时序变化联合建模问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频中目标位置、外观和运动状态持续变化，MVM需在恢复掩码内容时同时保持语义一致并捕捉时序变化，核心挑战是建立可靠帧间时序对应和设计兼顾语义一致与时序变化的表示学习目标。

Method: VT-CoRe引入夹层采样策略构建双侧时序参考，并用块注册模块(PRM)建立可靠的块级对应；在注册表示上通过自蒸馏架构施加隐空间语义对齐，并提出时序变化建模以约束表示随时间的轨迹。

Result: 在16个基准的8个下游任务上验证了有效性，覆盖像素级、帧级、视频级和视觉机器人学习场景；表示分析表明VT-CoRe学到了更结构化的隐空间。

Conclusion: VT-CoRe能有效学习变化感知的时序对应视频表示，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Self-supervised video representation learning has achieved remarkable progress by leveraging large-scale unlabeled video data. Recent methods commonly extend Masked Image Modeling (MIM) to Masked Video Modeling (MVM), where video representations are learned by capturing semantic information from temporal contexts during masked content recovery. Unlike static images, videos exhibit inherent temporal variation, as visual objects continuously change their positions, appearances, and motion states across frames. Therefore, MVM needs to preserve semantic consistency while capturing temporal changes of corresponding regions, which raises two central challenges: 1) how to establish reliable temporal correspondence across frames, and 2) how to formulate an appropriate representation learning objective for modeling both semantic consistency and temporal variation. To address these challenges, we propose Variation-aware Temporal Correspondence for video Representation learning (VT-CoRe) framework. For challenge 1), VT-CoRe introduces a sandwich sampling strategy to construct two-sided temporal references and a Patch Registration Module (PRM) to establish reliable patch-level correspondence. For challenge 2), VT-CoRe imposes latent-space semantic alignment on registered representations through a self-distillation architecture and further proposes temporal variation modeling to constrain representation trajectories over time. Experiments on 8 downstream tasks across 16 benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and applicability of VT-CoRe across pixel-level, frame-level, video-level, and vision-based robotic learning scenarios. Complementary representation analysis shows that VT-CoRe learns a more structured latent space for variation-aware temporal correspondence learning.

</details>


### [120] [Loop4D: Embodied World Modeling with 4D VAEs and Looped Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34199)
*Gen Li,Fan Luo,Mengjun Lin,Shu Han,Bohan Li,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Loop4D框架通过运动保持的4D表示和循环生成精炼，实现机器人操作结果的RGB与几何联合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作结果预测需同时建模视觉外观与演化3D几何，但视频压缩会模糊精细机器人运动，且联合预测RGB与几何对生成能力要求高。

Method: 提出Loop4D：4D VAE将RGB和点图序列编码为紧凑时空潜变量并保留运动细节与几何结构；RGB VAE结合几何引导监督与预测重建减少压缩运动伪影；几何VAE用轻量适配器适配预训练RGB表示并保留先验；双流循环扩散Transformer保持外观与几何独立流，共享Transformer权重与跨模态连接，通过跨循环复用块增加有效深度而不复制主干参数。

Result: 实验展示高质量4D生成，并在模拟机器人操作任务中验证Loop4D有效性。

Conclusion: Loop4D以较少参数通过额外计算改进RGB-几何联合预测，适用于机器人操作结果预测。

Abstract: Predicting the outcomes of robotic manipulation requires modeling both visual appearance and evolving 3D geometry. This poses two challenges: video compression can obscure fine-grained robot motion, while jointly predicting RGB and geometry demands sufficient generative capacity. We introduce Loop4D, a framework that addresses these challenges through motion-preserving 4D representations and recurrent generative refinement. Our 4D VAE encodes RGB and pointmap sequences into compact spatiotemporal latents while preserving motion details and geometric structure. Our RGB VAE combines geometry-guided supervision with predictive reconstruction to reduce compression-induced motion artifacts. Our geometry VAE uses lightweight adapters to adapt pretrained RGB representations while preserving their priors. Building on these representations, our Dual-Stream Looped Diffusion Transformer maintains separate appearance and geometry streams with shared Transformer weights and cross-modal connections. By reusing Transformer blocks across loops, we increase effective depth without replicating backbone parameters, enabling models with fewer parameters to improve joint RGB-geometry prediction through additional computation. Experiments demonstrate high-quality 4D generation and validate Loop4D in simulated robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [121] [An Image Detectability Field as a Viewpoint Utility for 2D-3D Localization in Sparse Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34139)
*Han Sun,Jun Li,Christy Jie Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究图像可检测性场作为视角效用函数在2D-3D定位中的价值，在合成物体和真实室内场景中验证其能提升稀疏地图下的定位成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手持或机器人搭载的相机在拍摄前可选择视角，作者探究仅利用地图几何信息时，图像可检测性场能否作为有用的视角规划效用函数。

Method: 利用地图局部几何训练可检测性场，预测2D检测器在3D点投影处触发的概率；直接使用该场（不微调）进行视角选择，配合固定SuperPoint和EPnP-RANSAC定位器，在ShapeNet合成物体和SceneFun3D真实室内场景中与计数、Harris-3D秩等基线对比。

Result: 在ShapeNet稀疏点地图上，对可见地标求和该场比计数高2.0个百分点定位成功率，比Harris-3D秩求和高2.2；在SceneFun3D测试房间，学习视角评分器LWL低于计数，但将该场加入学习式逐地标规划器后比无场版本高1.8个百分点，比Harris-3D和曲率替代分别高1.0和0.8个百分点，三个房间聚类区间均高于零。

Conclusion: 图像可检测性场在稀疏地图中有助于视角选择并提升定位成功率，在稠密地图中与Harris-3D表现相当。

Abstract: In 2D-3D localization, a camera estimates its pose by matching an image to a 3D map. A camera held in a hand or mounted on a robot can often choose its viewpoint before it takes the image. In this work, we ask whether an image detectability field is a useful viewpoint utility when the planner reads only the geometry of the map. The field can be queried at any 3D point near a surface. From the local geometry around the point, it predicts how likely a 2D image detector is to fire at the projection of the point. We use the trained field as it is, without fine-tuning it for view selection. A fixed SuperPoint and EPnP-RANSAC localizer then estimates the pose from the chosen view. We answer in two settings, each with a test registered before the runs. On synthetic ShapeNet objects with sparse point maps, summing the field over the visible landmarks beats counting them by +2.0 points of localization success rate (object-clustered 95% interval [+0.7, +3.2]). It also beats summing Harris-3D ranks by +2.2 [+0.8, +3.5]. On real indoor scenes from SceneFun3D, our adaptation of the learned view scorer LWL falls below counting on the test rooms. We therefore add the field to a learned per-landmark planner. On the test rooms with sparse maps whose landmarks the field also selects, the planner with the field beats the same planner without it by +1.8 points. It beats the planner with Harris-3D or curvature in its place by +1.0 and +0.8 points. All three room-clustered intervals lie above zero. In both settings, the field helps in sparse maps and is on par with Harris-3D in dense maps.

</details>


### [122] [When to Respond, Where to Ground: Streaming 3D Affordance Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34067)
*Wenting Luan,Weilong Yan,Siqi Lu,Ming Li,Beibei Lin,Yongbin Zheng,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamAff通过模型自身视觉证据决定响应时机和功能部件定位，实现流式3D可供性 grounding，在15秒流上达到30.14% StreamIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D可供性 grounding 方法假设完整场景观测，而流式视觉语言模型缺乏细粒度3D功能定位，因此需在连续观测中联合决定何时响应与何处定位。

Method: 提出StreamAff，基于视觉patch支持度与等待选项比较决定响应时机，最高分patch选择 grounding 帧；采用条件回看策略和缩放精炼 grounding 在选定帧3D点上分割部件；并构建StreamFun3D基准与StreamIoU指标。

Result: StreamAff在15秒流上达到30.14% StreamIoU，较最强基线相对提升40.6%。

Conclusion: 利用模型自身视觉证据可有效联合解决流式3D可供性 grounding 的响应时机与功能定位问题，显著优于离线方法适配和外部决策基线。

Abstract: Given a continuous RGB-D stream and a language request, streaming 3D affordance grounding requires an agent to decide when to respond and where to locate the requested functional part using only observations available so far. Existing 3D affordance grounding methods typically assume complete scene observations, while streaming vision-language models lack fine-grained 3D functional grounding. Our goal is to jointly determine response timing and functional-part localization during continuous scene observation. We propose StreamAff, an evidence-guided response strategy that makes both decisions from the grounding model's own visual evidence. The model scores the visual patches of the frames it reads: their combined support, weighed against a wait option, decides when to respond, and the highest-scoring patch selects the frame to ground in. A conditional look-back policy lets the model answer from an earlier observation at the moment of the request or wait for new evidence, and zoom-and-refine grounding segments the part named in the answer on the 3D points of the selected frame. We further introduce StreamFun3D, a benchmark derived from real-world 3D scenes that evaluates response timing and grounding across four evidence settings: Current, History, Future, and Unobserved, together with StreamIoU, a metric that credits a mask only when the response is timely and grounded in a valid source frame. We adapt offline 3D affordance grounding methods and build external-decider baselines that pair vision-language models with our mask decoder. StreamAff achieves 30.14% StreamIoU on 15-second streams, a 40.6% relative improvement over the strongest of these baselines.

</details>


### [123] [ContextHands: Completing Hand Motion with Learned Context and Joint-Specific Body Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34027)
*Taewoo Kim,Seunghun Koh,Hyosup Shin,Sungho Jo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ContextHands从身体序列和文本描述中生成手部细节动作，在OakInk2上取得更低FHD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部细节决定生成人体动作的真实感与表现力，但仅凭身体动作和语言推断手部关节运动需要将动作语境与手部关节运动相连接。

Method: 从文本描述和身体动作中学习概率手部特征，用辅助手部预测将特征映射到关节运动；多时间尺度身体编码器保留关节与身体部位特征，供每个手部关节查询关节特定信息；扩散生成器将其与学习到的手部上下文结合，编码器与生成器联合训练。

Result: 在OakInk2数据集上，ContextHands生成的手部动作更准确，Fréchet Hand Distance低于现有手部动作生成方法及适配该任务的通用人体动作生成方法；在抓取和工具使用的定性示例中更接近参考动作的手部姿态。

Conclusion: 联合学习上下文特征与手部生成可有效提升手部动作细节的真实性，代码与预训练模型将公开。

Abstract: Detailed hand motion contributes to the realism and expressiveness of generated human motion. Completing these details from body motion and language requires connecting the context of an action to the articulation of the hands. We introduce ContextHands, which completes hand motion from an observed body sequence and text descriptions assigned to hands and time intervals. Our approach learns probabilistic hand features from the descriptions and body motion, using auxiliary hand prediction to connect these features to hand articulation. A body encoder preserves joint and body-part features across multiple temporal scales, allowing each hand joint to query these features for joint-specific body information. The diffusion generator combines this joint-specific body information with the learned hand context. Both encoders and the generator are trained jointly, so the final generation objective also shapes the context features. Experiments on the OakInk2 dataset show that ContextHands generates more accurate hand motion and achieves lower Fréchet Hand Distance than existing hand-motion and general human-motion generation methods adapted to the task. In qualitative examples of grasping and tool use, ContextHands more closely reproduces the detailed hand poses in the reference motion. We will release our code and pretrained models to support further research.

</details>


### [124] [Embodied-VGGT: Preserving Reliable Local Geometry for Long-Sequence Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33964)
*Yiheng Han,Ziang Chen,Zhuyong Fu,Yiqing Yin,Nan Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Embodied-VGGT利用具身采集中的平台-环境几何与重复观测线索，通过记忆增强接地块、超点对齐和跨环优化来校正长序列重建中的漂移，同时保持局部几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列重建需要纠正累积漂移，同时保持局部一致的几何结构；机器人和自动驾驶中的具身采集提供了可重复利用的平台-环境几何和重复观测线索。

Method: 提出Embodied-VGGT：MAGC基于Scal3R的全局上下文记忆，利用预测地面几何得到的相对尺度参考来正则化测试时记忆更新；SA在地面与非地面证据间平衡以估计块间平移；CL利用交错窗口的几何与运动一致性选择连贯块组，并对每组施加共享Sim(3)校正以保持内部相对变换。

Result: 在五个公开驾驶基准上取得一致且显著的提升；KITTI Odometry上平均绝对轨迹误差7.30 m、Chamfer距离2.67 m，相比最优基线分别降低46.8%和23.3%。

Conclusion: 具身采集线索可有效用于长序列重建中的漂移校正与局部几何保持，Embodied-VGGT在多个驾驶基准上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Long sequence reconstruction must correct accumulated drift while preserving locally consistent geometry. In robotics and autonomous driving, embodied acquisition provides recurring platform- environment geometry and repeated observations of scene structure and platform motion. We present Embodied-VGGT, which exploits these cues to establish reliable local relationships and selectively preserve them during global refinement. Memory-Augmented Grounded Chunks (MAGC) builds on Scal3R's global context memory and uses relative scale references derived from predicted ground geometry to regularize test-time memory updates, encouraging cross-chunk consistency while limiting changes to reliable local geometry. Superpoint Alignment (SA) and Cross-Loop Optimization (CL) jointly address the robust establishment and preservation of geometric relationships. SA balances ground and non-ground evidence across spatial regions to estimate inter-chunk translation. CL uses geometric and motion consistency across interleaved windows to select coherent chunk groups, each receiving a shared Sim(3) correction that preserves its internal relative transformations during global optimization. Experiments on five public driving benchmarks demonstrate consistent and substantial improvements in long sequence reconstruction. On KITTI Odometry, Embodied-VGGT achieves a mean absolute trajectory error of 7.30 m and a Chamfer distance of 2.67 m, reducing these errors by 46.8% and 23.3% compared with the state-of-the-art baseline under same evaluation protocol. To facilitate reproducibility, we released our code: this https URL .

</details>


### [125] [Towards 360$^\circ$ Embodied UAV Tracking Without Turning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33934)
*Bingyu Li,Wu Du,Zhiyuan Zhao,Junyu Gao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出全景感知的无人机目标跟踪新范式与基准360UAV-ETrack及VLA框架360UAV-TVLA，在无需调整航向的情况下显著提升跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统具身无人机跟踪将目标可观测性与航向控制耦合，目标易离开前视相机视场，因此探索利用全向感知在无需主动航向调整下跟随指定目标。

Method: 构建含2,652条序列、超173万同步观测、8类跟踪任务和150个留出闭环场景的仿真基准360UAV-ETrack；提出全景视觉-语言-动作框架360UAV-TVLA，集成持续视觉指代(PVR)、压缩时序记忆(CTM)和几何对齐轨迹监督(GTS)，用于实例识别、运动推理与三维控制。

Result: 在两种VLA骨干网络上实验均较标准微调有一致提升，π_0的归一化平均跟踪帧数从22.56%增至35.28%，π_{0.5}从25.68%增至33.64%；基准还揭示离线模仿精度与闭环跟踪可靠性之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 全景感知无航向调整的跟踪范式可行且有效，所提基准与框架为后续研究提供了具有挑战性的测试平台。

Abstract: Conventional embodied UAV tracking couples target observability with heading control because targets may leave the field of view of forward-facing cameras. We investigate a complementary paradigm that uses full-spherical perception to follow designated targets without active heading adjustment. To support this setting, we introduce 360UAV-ETrack, a simulation benchmark comprising 2,652 sequences, over 1.73 million synchronized observations, eight tracking tasks, and 150 held-out closed-loop scenarios. We propose 360UAV-TVLA, a panoramic vision--language--action (VLA) framework integrating persistent visual referring (PVR), compressed temporal memory (CTM), and geometry-aligned trajectory supervision (GTS) for instance identification, motion reasoning, and three-dimensional control. Experiments across two VLA backbones show consistent gains over standard fine-tuning, with normalized average tracking frames increasing from 22.56\% to 35.28\% for $\pi_0$ and from 25.68\% to 33.64\% for $\pi_{0.5}$. Our benchmark further reveals a substantial gap between offline imitation accuracy and closed-loop tracking reliability, establishing a challenging testbed for future research this https URL

</details>


### [126] [Event-WAM: Learning to Act with Event-Derived Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33917)
*Jongseong Bae,Hyunwoo Kim,Yeongin Lee,Eubin So,Ha Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Event-WAM将事件相机衍生的视觉变化作为RGB之外的辅助未来监督信号，联合RGB-事件潜在流匹配，无需事件观测或显式未来生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型主要依赖RGB预测作为未来视觉建模目标，探索RGB之外的替代或补充目标，以引入变化导向的监督。

Method: 从RGB演示构建时间结构化事件表示，用冻结的预训练RGB视频VAE编码（不训练事件专用VAE），联合RGB与事件潜在流匹配进行训练；推理时不需要事件观测或显式未来生成。

Result: 仿真基准上标准条件下性能相当，扰动下鲁棒性提升依基准而异（如LIBERO-Plus有提升）；真实世界在标准和未见扰动条件下操作成功率提升；分析显示策略改进并不总伴随更高的未来预测保真度。

Conclusion: 变化导向的监督对动作学习具有潜力，表明未来视觉建模的监督目标可超越RGB预测本身。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) leverage future visual modeling for robotic action learning, with recent studies exploring alternative or complementary objectives beyond conventional RGB prediction. We introduce Event-WAM, which incorporates event-derived visual changes as auxiliary future supervision alongside RGB. Event-WAM constructs temporally structured event representations from RGB demonstrations and encodes them using a frozen pretrained RGB video VAE, without training an event-specific VAE. Joint RGB--Event latent flow matching provides appearance- and change-oriented supervision without requiring event observations or explicit future generation at inference. Across simulated benchmarks, Event-WAM maintains comparable performance under standard conditions, while robustness gains under perturbations are benchmark-dependent, including improvements on LIBERO-Plus. Real-world evaluations further demonstrate improved manipulation success under both standard and unseen perturbation conditions. Further analyses show that policy improvements are not consistently accompanied by higher future prediction fidelity, suggesting the potential of change-oriented supervision for action learning.

</details>


### [127] [Off the Driven Path: Transferable Road Understanding in Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33878)
*Lena Wild,Christian Helten,Katie Z Luo,Jakob Thumm,Rafael Valencia,Patric Jensfelt,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Road-KSU框架，将HD地图与标注转为道路中心场景图，用语言问答与轨迹指标交叉评估端到端驾驶策略的道路知识，发现驾驶分数提升多源于记忆道路几何，地理多样性才是可迁移道路知识的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶策略推理时 increasingly 不依赖显式HD地图，但其内化了何种道路知识尚不明确，需通过模型输出探测。

Method: 构建Road-KSU，自动将HD地图和actor标注转换为道路中心场景图，派生道路聚焦的问答与推理目标及基于地图的轨迹合规指标；结合地理不相交划分，形成输出模态（语言vs动作）×地点熟悉度（见过vs未见过）的2×2评估；在NAVSIM上分析13个规划器，并重训练与数据缩放实验。

Result: NAVSIM测试样本95%距训练样本10m内，13个已发表规划器在转弯处分数随经过该地点的训练行驶数陡增，指向记忆的道路几何；地理不相交重训练显示动作空间在不熟悉转弯处退化最明显，失败以几何合规为主；语言准确率在直线与弯曲道路VQA上均下降，而动态场景内容稳定；数据缩放呈双重分离：同一地点更多行驶提升未见过道路驾驶但道路问答不变，固定预算分散到更多地点的道路问答提升而驾驶性能变化很小。

Conclusion: 驾驶分数提升不必意味着对未见过道路的更广道路理解，地理多样性是可迁移道路知识的关键。

Abstract: End-to-end driving policies increasingly operate without explicit HD maps at inference, raising the question of what road knowledge they actually internalize. To probe this knowledge through model outputs, we introduce Road-KSU, a framework that automatically converts HD maps and actor annotations into a road-centric scene graph. From this, we derive road-focused question answering and reasoning targets, complemented by map-based trajectory compliance metrics. Pairing Road-KSU with geographically disjoint splits yields a $2 \times 2$ evaluation crossing output modality (language vs. action) with place familiarity (seen vs. unseen roads). On NAVSIM, 95% of test samples lie within 10m of a training sample, and scores of 13 published planners on turns rise steeply with the number of training drives through the test location, pointing to memorized road geometry. Retraining on geographically disjoint splits and probing with Road-KSU confirm that action-space performance degrades most on unfamiliar turns, with failures dominated by geometric compliance. Language accuracy, however, drops on road-centric VQA on straight and curved roads alike, and remains stable on dynamic scene content. Scaling the training data further reveals a double dissociation: more drives through the same places improve driving on unseen roads but leave road question answering unchanged, whereas spreading a fixed data budget over more places increases road question answering with little change in driving performance. These findings show that improved driving scores need not imply broader road understanding on unseen roads, and identify geographic diversity as key to transferable road knowledge.

</details>


### [128] [FlipVLA: Backdoor Attacks on Vision-Language-Action(VLA) Models Only Through Bit Flips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33810)
*Zexi Zhao,Zizhen Liu,Jing Justin Ye,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FlipVLA，通过两阶段选择器-放大器方法仅翻转少量固定参数位即可在VLA模型中植入对象触发后门，无需重训练，攻击成功率达90.22%–100%，干净场景任务成功率最多下降1.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型从视觉与语言指令生成真实环境中的机器人动作，恶意模型篡改会威胁物理安全，作者研究能否通过少量固定参数位翻转（而非重训练）植入对象触发后门，难点在于持久权重修改需破坏触发时行为同时保持非触发输入性能。

Method: FlipVLA采用选择器-放大器两阶段方法，利用FC-GELU-FC结构中已有视觉特征：选择器在选定通道构造触发相关响应并用GELU衰减背景活动；放大器修改相连下游权重将该响应转为动作相关扰动；通过通道筛选与离散位搜索在有限修改预算下构建两部分。

Result: 在OpenVLA-OFT和GR00T N1.7上跨四个LIBERO套件实验，攻击成功率达90.22%至100%，干净场景下任务成功率最多下降1.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 对象触发后门可通过少量固定参数位翻转植入VLA模型，无需重训练，且能在高攻击成功率下基本保持干净场景性能，表明VLA模型存在实际物理安全风险。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models generate robot actions from visual observations and language instructions in real-world environments, making malicious model tampering a potential threat to physical safety. We study whether object-triggered backdoors can be implanted through a small, fixed set of parameter bit flips without retraining. The central challenge is to make persistent weight modifications disrupt trigger-present behavior while preserving performance on non-trigger inputs. We propose FlipVLA, a two-stage selector-amplifier method that exploits existing visual features within an FC-GELU-FC structure. The selector constructs a trigger-dependent response in a selected channel, using GELU to attenuate background activity. The amplifier modifies a connected downstream weight to convert this response into an action-relevant perturbation. Channel screening and discrete bit search construct both components under a limited modification budget. Experiments on OpenVLA-OFT and GR00T N1.7 across four LIBERO suites achieve attack success rates of 90.22% to 100%, with task success decreasing by at most 1.5 percentage points on clean scenes.

</details>


### [129] [ScanAR: Parallel Row-Scan Decoding for Efficient Image Generation in Unified Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33761)
*Jingen Qu,Xi Chen,Yingbo Tang,Xinghang Li,Tao Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScanAR通过仅重定义视觉token的前驱关系，将统一多模态自回归模型图像生成的解码深度从HW降至H+W，在不改变主干、词表或输出头且不增加参数的情况下实现高质量加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态自回归模型以光栅顺序逐token解码H×W视觉网格，需HW步串行，主导推理时间；现有视觉AR加速方法或改动分词器/预测头/模型接口，或保持模型但难以高并行且不掉质量，无法迁移到统一AR模型。

Method: 提出行扫描并行解码ScanAR，仅重定义视觉token的前驱：首行视觉token照常从左到右生成，后续每行在单个并行步中生成，每个token由上一行垂直对齐的token预测；非视觉token保持标准自回归规则。该方法不改变主干、词表、输出头，不增加参数，支持从头训练和轻量后训练。

Result: 在Emu3.5-Image-34B上，后训练达到GenEval 82.36，为同数据训练的后训练加速方法中最佳，相同推理设置下生成512×512图像比光栅解码快13.8倍；在ImageNet上，从头训练的ScanAR在各模型尺寸下吞吐最高，FID与光栅LlamaGen相当；在34B Xiaomi-Robotics-U0模型上保持具身场景生成、图文交错生成和视频生成的总体质量。

Conclusion: ScanAR以最小改动实现统一多模态AR模型视觉生成的高并行加速，在质量与效率上均优于现有方法，并具备良好的通用性。

Abstract: Unified multimodal autoregressive (AR) models represent text and images in one token sequence and generate all modalities with a shared next-token prediction head. Their image generation, however, decodes an $H\times W$ visual grid token by token in raster order, which takes $HW$ sequential steps and dominates inference time. Existing acceleration methods for visual AR generation either change the tokenizer, the prediction head, or the model interface, or keep the model but cannot reach high parallelism without losing quality, so they do not carry over to unified AR models. We propose ScanAR, a row-scan parallel decoding method that only redefines the predecessors of visual tokens. The first visual row is generated from left to right as usual, and each later row is generated in a single parallel step, with every token predicted from the vertically aligned token of the previous row. Nonvisual tokens keep the standard autoregressive rule. ScanAR reduces the visual decoding depth from $HW$ to $H+W$ without changing the backbone, vocabulary, or output head and without adding parameters, and it supports both training from scratch and lightweight post-training. On Emu3.5-Image-34B, ScanAR post-training reaches a GenEval score of 82.36, the best among post-training acceleration methods trained on the same data, and generates $512\times512$ images $13.8\times$ faster than raster decoding under the same inference setting. On ImageNet, ScanAR trained from scratch has the highest throughput at every model size with FID comparable to raster LlamaGen. On the 34B Xiaomi-Robotics-U0 model, ScanAR preserves the overall quality of embodied scene generation, interleaved image-text generation, and video generation.

</details>


### [130] [GeoTex: Geometry-as-Texture Encoding and History-Grounded 4D Occupancy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33693)
*Shijun Guo,Yaming Yang,Haoran Xu,Wei Zhao,Ziyu Guan,Cai Xu,Peixi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoTex是一种4D占用预测世界模型，通过几何即纹理编码与历史锚定预测来提升自动驾驶场景演化预测的精度并降低参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义占用预测方法借用图像编码框架（高度通道化或平面分解）压缩潜表示，未在压缩前联合建模列内高度模式与跨列关系，弱化了3D几何线索；同时预测阶段对互补历史利用不足，难以保留可靠观测证据。

Method: 提出GeoTex，包含表示与预测两部分：表示端将列内高度模式与跨列关系联合编码为紧凑连续潜表示，并通过内容相关的高度展开与几何交互恢复3D结构；预测端用固定跨观测关系记忆保留初始证据，并以目标参照残差更新融入近期历史，预测训练后采用仅解码器适配改进语义解码。

Result: 相比GenieDrive，平均预测mIoU和IoU分别提升9.79%和11.91%，参数量从3.47M降至2.33M，且预测时域越长增益越明显。

Conclusion: 联合建模列内与跨列几何线索并结合历史锚定预测，可在更小模型下显著提升长时域4D占用预测性能。

Abstract: 4D occupancy forecasting models 3D scene evolution to support future prediction and scenario generation in autonomous-driving world models. Semantic occupancy abstracts image color and texture into discrete labels, leaving 3D voxel arrangements as the primary cues for distinguishing local shapes within each class. Existing methods borrow image-encoding frameworks to obtain compact latent representations through height channelization or plane factorization, but do not sufficiently model within-column height patterns and cross-column relations jointly before compression, weakening these geometric cues. Forecasting also underutilizes complementary history and struggles to retain reliable observation evidence. We therefore propose GeoTex, an occupancy world model combining geometry-as-texture encoding with history-grounded forecasting. GeoTex Representation jointly encodes within-column height patterns and cross-column relations into compact continuous latent representations and restores 3D structure through content-dependent height unfolding and geometric interaction. GeoTex Prediction retains initial evidence throughout rollout in fixed cross-observation relational memory and incorporates complementary recent history through target-referenced residual updates. After prediction training, decoder-only adaptation improves semantic decoding of predicted states. GeoTex improves average forecasting mIoU and IoU by 9.79% and 11.91% relative to GenieDrive while reducing parameters from 3.47M to 2.33M, with gains increasing at longer horizons. Code is included in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [131] [WorldUAV: Scaling UAV Data Synthesis via World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33682)
*Xuewen Liu,Mengjuan Chen,Jing Zhang,Mengzhe Shen,Zekun Xu,Zibo Chen,Zuoxiu Zhang,Yue Dong,Zhikai Li,Qingyi Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WorldUAV数据集，利用世界模型将无人机融入真实视频，结合多模态大模型智能体实现全自动数据生成，显著提升无人机检测性能并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机检测与跟踪受限于高质量训练数据稀缺：游戏引擎合成数据存在合成到真实的巨大差距，而人工采集真实数据成本高、规模受限。

Method: 构建由世界模型驱动的高效框架：将无人机直接集成到现成真实视频中，保留真实背景、光照与场景动态；利用世界模型的时空先验生成运动合理、姿态变化且跨帧一致的无人机视频；开发由多模态大语言模型编排的智能体流水线，自动完成源获取、轨迹规划、合成调用与视频验证。

Result: WorldUAV包含6,878段无人机视频和205,778张无人机图像；实验表明其显著提升无人机检测器性能，且每张高质量无人机图像仅需15秒和0.0005美元API成本。

Conclusion: 该工作同时提供了高质量数据集和可扩展的数据生成范式，有效缩小合成与真实数据差距，降低数据集构建成本，推动无人机检测与跟踪研究。

Abstract: UAV detection and tracking remain constrained by the scarcity of high-quality training data. Existing game-engine-based synthetic UAV data suffer from a substantial synthetic-to-real gap, while the high cost of manually collected real-world UAV data limits dataset scale. Therefore, we introduce WorldUAV, a high-quality UAV dataset constructed through an efficient framework powered by world models. Specifically, WorldUAV directly integrates UAVs into readily available real-world videos, naturally preserving realistic backgrounds, illumination, and scene dynamics. Leveraging the spatiotemporal priors of world models, it generates UAV videos with plausible motion, pose variations, and cross-frame consistency, effectively narrowing the gap between synthetic and real data. We further develop an agentic pipeline orchestrated by a multimodal large language model that acquires sources, plans UAV trajectories, invokes synthesis, and verifies generated videos, enabling fully automated dataset construction. WorldUAV comprises 6,878 UAV videos and 205,778 UAV images. Experiments show that WorldUAV significantly improves the performance of UAV detectors. Moreover, it substantially reduces dataset construction costs, requiring only 15 seconds and USD 0.0005 API cost per high-quality UAV image. Our work offers both a high-quality dataset and a scalable data generation paradigm for UAV detection and tracking.

</details>


### [132] [ArtLang-4D: Articulation-Aware Dynamic Language Fields from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33511)
*Sylvia Yuan,Dan Wang,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Xinrui Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtLang-4D从单目RGB-D视频重建铰接物体，并构建以关节状态归一化时间t为索引的动态语言场，实现基于部件和状态的语言查询与命令执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D语言场每点仅一个特征，无法区分描述物体状态的语言；4D语言场以视频时间为条件，缺乏运动学意义且用户无法设定。

Method: 使用规范三角场，三角形跟随从视频中无初始化发现的类型化螺旋关节；语言场由带t标签的描述各部件比较状态短语的标题库监督，在白化开放词汇文本空间中训练；自由查询通过联合最大化部件和状态相关性返回部件及状态，命令则沿恢复关节移动该部件。

Result: 在Articulate-100的100个物体上，ArtLang-4D恢复的关节和部件几何比重建与铰接基线更准确，状态检索比状态无关语言场更可靠，语言命令执行的运动误差更低。

Conclusion: 以关节状态时间索引的动态语言场能有效关联语言描述与物体运动状态，提升铰接物体重建、状态检索和语言命令执行的性能。

Abstract: Language that describes an articulated object is often about its state: the drawer pulled out the furthest, the door opened halfway, push the drawer all the way in. Existing 3D language fields attach one feature to each point and cannot tell these descriptions apart, and 4D language fields condition on video time, which has no kinematic meaning and cannot be set by a user. We present ArtLang-4D, which reconstructs an articulated object from a single monocular RGB-D video and equips it with a dynamic language field indexed by articulation time t, the normalized state of its joints, instead of video time. The geometry is a canonical triangle field whose triangles follow typed screw joints, discovered from the video without any joint initialization. The language field is supervised by a t-tagged caption bank that describes each part with comparative state phrases in a whitened open-vocabulary text space. A free-form query is answered by maximizing part relevance jointly over parts and states, which returns both the part and the articulation state it describes; a command is then executed by moving only that part along its recovered joint. On the 100 objects of Articulate-100, ArtLang-4D recovers more accurate joints and part geometry than reconstruction and articulation baselines, retrieves the queried state far more reliably than a state-agnostic language field, and executes language commands with lower motion error.

</details>


### [133] [ArtKit: Motion-Driven Re-Segmentation and Link Re-Generation for Articulated Objects from RGB-D Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33403)
*Sylvia Yuan,Dan Wang,Vivian Yu,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Xinrui Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtKit从单视角RGB-D视频生成完整铰接模型（含部件、运动学树和类型化关节），通过生成隐藏几何并与视频观测验证，在轴和部件运动误差上显著优于最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-D相机从单一视角拍摄运动物体时只能看到一侧及其部件运动，无法获得完整的铰接结构（完整部件、运动学树和类型化关节），因此需要从视频中生成并验证被遮挡的几何与关节。

Method: 基于首帧用生成式部件先验提出完整且部件分解的几何；将点轨迹分组为刚性轨迹以分裂/合并生成部件为连杆；拟合旋转和移动关节，并用可见性感知能量确保铰接模型解释视频中的深度；通过规则在同步组拟合与逐帧链拟合间选择；最后重新生成每个连杆并保留与观测更一致的结果。

Result: 在Articulate-100和HArt（60个PartNet-Mobility物体和8个机器人手/夹爪的RGB-D视频基准）上，ArtKit将物体上的轴和部件运动误差比最强基线降低一半以上，并降低手部轴误差；还能在真实录像和生成视频上运行。

Conclusion: ArtKit通过生成隐藏几何并与视频观测验证，有效从单视角RGB-D视频重建完整铰接模型，适用于多样铰接物体，并在误差上大幅优于基线。

Abstract: An RGB-D camera that films an object being moved, often from one viewpoint, sees one side of it and how its parts move. We present ArtKit, which takes such a video as input to create an articulated model with complete parts, a kinematic tree, and typed joints. Our central idea is generating the hidden geometry and verifying it against what the camera sees. A generative part prior proposes complete, part-decomposed geometry based on the first frame. Point tracks, grouped into rigid trajectories, split and merge the generated parts into links. We then fit revolute and prismatic joints, with a visibility-aware energy ensuring that the articulated model explains the depth observed across the video. A rule checks against the video to choose between synchronous group fits and per-frame chain fits, so that one pipeline serves a diverse range of articulated objects, such as household items and robotic hands. Finally, we regenerate each link of the chosen fit as one part and keep it wherever it agrees better with the observations. We evaluate on Articulate-100 and on HArt, a new benchmark of 60 PartNet-Mobility objects and 8 robotic hands and grippers rendered as RGB-D videos with ground-truth parts and joints. ArtKit reduces the axis and part motion errors of the strongest baseline by more than half on the objects, as well as the axis error on the hands. ArtKit also runs on real recordings and generated videos.

</details>


### [134] [ArtTet: Watertight Part Meshes of Articulated Objects from Rigid-Body Rules and Partitioned Marching Tetrahedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33382)
*Sylvia Yuan,Dan Wang,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Xinrui Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtTet从两个关节状态的多视角观测重建铰接物体，输出每个刚性部件的水密网格及运动关节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于网格泼溅的方法将物体视为单一场景，提取全局表面，无法分离不同部件的三角形，导致网格分层、自相交和孔洞。

Method: 采用分治策略：先通过动态与几何约束的关节及归属推断将物体划分为刚性部件，用多体配准恢复部件及其螺旋关节，并用刚体规则校正关节类型和部件标签；再将空间划分为部件单元（铰接四面体划分），在每个单元上于共享四面体网格使用可微符号距离场，分区移动四面体保证场互斥，从而提取闭合且不重叠的部件网格，并联合优化两状态图像。

Result: 在Articulate-100上，在不使用部件掩码的方法中，ArtTet在轴方向、部件运动和部件几何精度上最优，部件网格几乎无开放边界边；并展示了SplArt数据集真实采集的重建结果。

Conclusion: ArtTet通过分治式部件划分与互斥符号距离场，实现了无部件标注下高质量、水密且不重叠的铰接物体部件重建。

Abstract: We present ArtTet, a method that reconstructs an articulated object from multi-view observations of two articulation states and outputs one watertight mesh per rigid part together with the joints that move them. Mesh splatting gives explicit triangles, but it treats the object as one scene. It extracts a single global surface and cannot keep the triangles of different parts apart; the mesh it exports is layered, self-intersecting, and full of holes. ArtTet follows a divide-and-conquer strategy instead. We first divide the object into rigid parts with dynamic- and geometric-constrained articulation and ownership inference. A multibody registration of the two states recovers the parts and their screw joints. Rigid-body rules on attachment, contact, and swept motion then correct joint types and part labels without any part annotation. We then divide space into part cells with an articulated tetrahedral partition, and conquer each cell with its own differentiable signed distance field on a shared tetrahedral grid. In these partitioned marching tetrahedra the fields are mutually exclusive by construction, so the extracted part meshes are closed and never overlap. All part meshes are optimized jointly from the images of both states through the recovered joints. On Articulate-100, ArtTet gives the most accurate axis directions, part motions, and part geometry over all objects among methods that use no part masks, and its part meshes have virtually no open boundary edges. We also show reconstructions of real captures from the SplArt dataset.

</details>


### [135] [CriticalScenes: Scalable Photorealistic Critical-Scenario Generation for Closed-Loop Driving Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33255)
*Alexandros Theocharous,Holger Caesar,Ziqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CriticalScenes框架将普通驾驶场景重建为逼真的安全关键交互，为端到端规划器提供避撞监督，并在闭环基准中降低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记录驾驶数据中安全关键交互稀少，端到端规划器训练时缺乏规避行为监督，可能导致不安全行为和碰撞。

Method: 提出CriticalScenes，基于重建将普通记录场景转化为具有碰撞规避监督的逼真安全关键交互；在53个源场景中尝试1060次交互，并测试两种结构不同的端到端规划器。

Result: 979次交互生成可行威胁轨迹（92.4%），插入的威胁主体在5-35米非遮挡观测中可检测率超过95%；SparseDrive在独立逼真闭环基准上碰撞率相对降低46.5%，UniAD闭环提升有限但开环增益显著。

Conclusion: 生成的安全关键监督可提升关键场景规划，但闭环迁移因规划器架构而异；开环轨迹预测改进不一定转化为等效安全增益，凸显闭环评估的重要性。

Abstract: Safety-critical interactions are rare in recorded driving data, leaving end-to-end driving planners with limited exposure to evasive behaviour during training. This lack of critical supervision can lead to unsafe behaviour and, in rare instances, collisions. To address this gap, we introduce CriticalScenes, a reconstruction-based framework that transforms ordinary recorded driving scenes into photorealistic safety-critical interactions with corresponding collision-avoidance supervision. Across 53 reconstructed source scenes, 979 of 1,060 attempted interactions yield feasible threat trajectories (92.4\%), while the inserted threat actors remain detectable in more than 95\% of non-occluded observations within 5--35\,m. Experiments with two structurally different end-to-end planners show that the generated supervision improves critical-scenario planning, while closed-loop transfer varies across planner architectures. SparseDrive achieves a 46.5\% relative reduction in collision rate on an independent photorealistic closed-loop benchmark, whereas UniAD shows only a modest closed-loop improvement despite substantial open-loop gains. The results further highlight the importance of closed-loop evaluation, as improvements in open-loop trajectory prediction do not necessarily translate into equivalent safety gains.

</details>


### [136] [HOG-WAM: Improving Visual Robustness through Future Gradient Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34811)
*Boxuan Dong,Yupeng Qian,Ying Wu,Heshuai Li,Junhao Tan,Zhenxiong Tan,Weijia Wu,Weihao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HOG-WAM通过将未来HOG特征作为辅助监督，提升世界动作模型在视觉损坏下的策略鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型联合预测观测与动作，但视觉损坏会干扰其策略；需要一种能增强视觉表示鲁棒性的辅助监督。

Method: 引入未来方向梯度直方图（HOG）作为来自演示图像的结构监督；训练时用辅助HOG扩散Transformer通过非对称注意力读取RGB特征，以塑造控制所用的视觉表示，推理时移除该模块，保留RGB到动作架构。

Result: 在RoboTwin-Plus四种视觉损坏下成功率均最高，混合噪声从78.86%升至82.86%；LIBERO Plus整体从74.49%升至75.26%，传感器噪声从74.14%升至78.89%；四个Franka任务干净观测平均从40%升至59%，噪声下从17%升至24%；消融表明RGB观测加辅助HOG预测优于用HOG替代RGB。

Conclusion: 未来梯度预测可作为视觉鲁棒控制的互补监督，物理任务上的绝对增益大于仿真环境。

Abstract: World-action models jointly predict future observations and actions, yet visual corruption can disrupt the resulting policies. We introduce HOG-WAM, which adds future Histograms of Oriented Gradients (HOG) as structural supervision derived directly from demonstration images. An auxiliary HOG diffusion transformer (DiT) module reads RGB features through asymmetric attention, allowing its objective to shape the visual representation used for control. The module is used only during training and removed at inference, preserving FastWAM's RGB-to-action architecture. We evaluate four controlled observation corruptions through a visual-corruption extension of RoboTwin, termed RoboTwin-Plus; HOG-WAM achieves the highest success among the compared policies under all four. Mixed-noise success increases from 78.86% to 82.86% relative to FastWAM, with comparable performance on clean scenes. On LIBERO Plus, overall success increases from 74.49% to 75.26%, with the largest gain under sensor noise, from 74.14% to 78.89%. Across four Franka tasks, average success increases from 40% to 59% under clean observations and from 17% to 24% under noisy observations. A representation ablation favors combining RGB observations with auxiliary HOG prediction over replacing RGB with HOG. These findings support future-gradient prediction as complementary supervision for visually robust control, with larger absolute gains on physical tasks than in simulation. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [137] [Was the Model Given the Right Action? Auditing and Repairing Action Interfaces in Navigation World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34739)
*Ajmain Istiak Apon,Md Mehedi Hasan,Sejuti Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 世界模型评估中动作输入编码不匹配会夸大预测误差；修正后NWM视觉预测误差降低42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型评估默认动作准备方式与训练一致，但该假设未经检验，不匹配会使模型显得比实际更不准确。

Method: 审计Navigation World Models训练与部署代码中的三处交互差异；在不重训情况下修正输入；在RECON数据集上以DreamSim度量误差；并故意向两个替代模型引入编码错误，及提出ActRosetta工具检测与修复动作编码。

Result: 修正输入后NWM平均视觉预测误差降低42%，并改变检查点排名、消除或削弱两个替代模型的测量优势；仅修正归一化偏移反而增加误差；两个替代模型在引入编码错误后均变得更不准确。

Conclusion: 报告任何动作条件世界模型的rollout误差前，必须确认评估时动作编码与训练完全一致；交互性不匹配需一起检查，ActRosetta可辅助诊断但覆盖有限且控制改进未证实。

Abstract: World models are evaluated by predicting future observations from actions. These evaluations assume that actions are prepared the same way as during training, but an unchecked mismatch can make a model appear less accurate than it is. We audit this assumption in Navigation World Models (NWM) and find three interacting differences between its training and rollout code. Correcting the inputs, without retraining, reduces mean visual prediction error by 42%, measured with DreamSim on the RECON navigation dataset. The correction also changes the ranking of NWM checkpoints in an exploratory analysis and removes or reduces two alternative models' measured advantages. Correcting only the normalization offset instead increases error, showing why interacting mismatches must be checked together. To test whether sensitivity to input preparation extends beyond NWM, we deliberately introduce encoding errors into two alternative models; both become less accurate. We introduce ActRosetta, a tool that checks whether action encoding matches training, identifies supported causes of disagreement, and repairs inputs when sufficient information is available. Repair helps little where predictions barely respond to actions, the tool's diagnosis has limited coverage, and improved control remains unproven. For any action-conditioned world model, the lesson is the same: before reporting rollout error, verify that evaluation encodes actions exactly as training did.

</details>


### [138] [Motion Beyond Pixels: Learning Future Visual Representations for Object-Centric Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34644)
*Chengzhi Zhao,Juelin Li,Zhennan Wang,Sheng Wang,Zhuoman Liu,Yuhao Lu,Jiongxu Chen,Haitang Feng,Yanjie Li,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionRep 是一个面向具身世界模型的视觉表示世界模型，联合生成未来的 DINO 表示与 3D 物体流，将语义和几何信息与以物体为中心的动力学耦合，在 3D 物体流精度和泛化性上优于基于像素的基线，且推理仅需三步去噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身世界模型多在为 RGB 重建优化的潜空间中预测未来状态，未显式优先考虑与运动生成相关的物体级语义和几何信息，因此需要一个兼顾语义、几何与以物体为中心动力学的未来状态表示空间。

Method: 提出 MotionRep：用 DINO-VAE 对高维 DINO 特征做时序与通道压缩并结合潜变量正则化，得到紧凑且保留语义几何信息的潜表示；以初始观测、物体查询点和语言指令为条件，用统一扩散 Transformer 通过联合注意力同时预测未来视觉表示与 3D 物体流；采用非对称的模态特定噪声调度，使视觉流在联合去噪中领先于 3D 物体流，用逐步细化的未来视觉表示引导流生成。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作基准上，MotionRep 在 3D 物体流精度和对未见场景的泛化上优于基于像素的基线，同时推理仅需三次去噪步骤。

Conclusion: 预测性视觉表示是面向以物体为中心动力学建模的有效未来状态空间。

Abstract: Many embodied world models predict future states in latent spaces optimized for RGB reconstruction, which do not explicitly prioritize object-level semantics and geometry relevant to motion generation. We present MotionRep, a visual representation world model that jointly generates future DINO representations and 3D object flow, coupling semantic and geometric information with object-centric dynamics. To make high-dimensional DINO features amenable to generative modeling, we introduce DINO-VAE, which combines temporal and channel compression with latent regularization to produce compact latents while preserving semantic and geometric information. Conditioned on an initial observation, object query points, and a language instruction, a unified diffusion transformer predicts future visual representations and 3D object flow through joint attention. MotionRep employs asymmetric modality-specific noise schedules to keep the visual stream ahead of 3D object flow during joint denoising, allowing progressively refined future visual representations to guide flow generation. Experiments on simulated and real-world manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that MotionRep outperforms pixel-based baselines in 3D object flow accuracy and generalization to unseen scenes, while requiring only three denoising steps for inference. These results support predictive visual representations as an effective future-state space for object-centric dynamics modeling.

</details>


### [139] [Who Is Ego? Scaling Cross-View Camera Wearer Identification and Segmentation beyond Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34489)
*Yifan Jin,Zheng Zhang,Tianwen Qian,Dong Wang,Bo Zhao,Yanwei Fu,Yuqian Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FindEgo-1M数据集与EgoRay系列方法，利用极点在佩戴者上的几何关系，实现无需任务训练的相机佩戴者识别与分割，性能显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称图像只显示相机所见，无法确定佩戴者身份与位置；需将任务从人类扩展到机器人、车辆、动物、无人机，并覆盖佩戴者分割、缺失预测及双向相机定位。

Method: 构建含116万对真实与仿真ego-exo图像、12种佩戴者类型、17个领域、111个测试设置的FindEgo-1M；提出EgoRay-ZS，将冻结的前馈3D重建模型VGGT-Ω转为免训练方案，按候选掩码到ego相机投影（极点）的角度偏移与距离排序；再通过姿态监督训练与无标签测试时自适应得到完整EgoRay。

Result: EgoRay-ZS在TF2023原始标签上达78.0%识别准确率（PEN为52.9%）；EgoRay在FindEgo-1M已见/未见设置下机会校正准确率达87.1/77.7，匹配查询上比GPT-6 Astra高31.6/21.3点且快18倍。

Conclusion: 利用ego相机投影的几何关系可有效识别与分割佩戴者，免训练方法已具竞争力，完整模型在跨设置下达到最新最优，代码与模型将公开。

Abstract: A first-person (ego) image reveals what a camera sees, but not who or what carries it. Given a synchronized third-person (exo) image, camera wearer identification asks who is ego: which candidate carries the ego camera? We extend this task beyond humans to robots, vehicles, animals, and drones, and broaden its spatial scope (where is ego) from selecting a provided candidate mask to wearer segmentation with absence prediction and mutual camera localization across ego-exo views. We introduce FindEgo-1M, comprising 1.16M real and simulated ego-exo pairs across 12 wearer types, 17 domains, and 111 test settings. Our approach exploits a geometric relation: the ego camera's projection into the exo image, i.e., the epipole, lies on or near its wearer. We introduce EgoRay-ZS, the first method to turn a frozen feed-forward 3D reconstruction model, VGGT-$\Omega$, into a training-free solution for wearer identification and segmentation. Its geometric readout ranks candidate masks or segmentation proposals by angular offset and distance to the ego camera, while the relative pose provides mutual camera directions. We then develop the full EgoRay model through pose-supervised training on FindEgo-1M and label-free test-time adaptation constrained by candidate masks. Without task-specific training, EgoRay-ZS achieves 78.0% identification accuracy on TF2023's original labels, versus 52.9% reported for the dataset-trained PEN. On FindEgo-1M, EgoRay achieves state-of-the-art chance-corrected accuracy of 87.1 and 77.7 on seen and unseen settings. On matched queries, EgoRay also outperforms GPT-6 Astra by 31.6 and 21.3 points on seen and unseen settings, respectively, while running $18\times$ faster. Data, code, and models will be released.

</details>


### [140] [Can Visual World Models See Force?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34422)
*Felix Tristram,Benjamin D. Killeen,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉基础模型能感知接触发生但无法表征力大小，机器人后训练也无法弥补此缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作不仅依赖可见轨迹，还依赖隐性的交互力；人类具有力的视觉先验，因此探究视觉基础模型表征是否捕获交互力。

Method: 定义力感知为三方面能力，用轻量读出层在同步视频与力测量数据上探查冻结的图像、视频、世界模型和策略模型表征。

Result: 多数模型能判断接触何时发生，但不能追踪力变化或反映力的大小；机器人后训练未能改善该能力。

Conclusion: 当前视觉基础模型在交互力表征上存在重要能力空白。

Abstract: World models and vision-language-action policy models are increasingly proposed as foundations for robot manipulation. They predict how scenes evolve and which actions to take, yet successful manipulation depends not solely on following observable trajectories but also on the hidden variable of interaction force. Neurophysiology has shown that humans possess a visual prior for this hidden variable, motivating us to ask the question: Do the representations of visual foundation models also capture the forces involved in an interaction? We define force awareness as the ability of representations to (1) indicate when contact force is applied, (2) track how it changes during an interaction, and (3) reflect how large it is. We probe frozen image, video, world- and policy model representations with a lightweight readout trained on synchronized video and force measurements from two manipulation datasets. Our experiments show that most models capture when contact occurs but not how much force is being applied. Surprisingly, robot post-training does not improve this either, highlighting an important gap in current models capabilities.

</details>


### [141] [A Simple Yet Effective Agent for Open-World Aerial Object Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34115)
*Jitai Hao,Liangxing Yang,Zhenyang Li,Fan Liu,Qiang Huang,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimpleUAV 是一个无需训练、基于冻结视觉语言模型的最小化空中目标搜索智能体，性能显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放世界空中目标搜索方法依赖持久空间地图、世界模型或任务特定策略训练，本文探究极简无训练设计能达到的性能上限。

Method: SimpleUAV 使用冻结的现成 VLM，将当前视图目标评估与基于历史条件的动作选择分离；结合局部几何将视觉选择转为有界直线运动，用深度检查终止受阻路段，并利用执行反馈指导后续决策，形成无需持久地图与任务训练的闭环搜索。

Result: 在 UAV-ON 上达到 41.61% 成功率（此前最强基线 23.80%），在 AerialDojo-200K 上达到 11.98%（基线 3.20%）；经少量基准适配后迁移至 TravelUAV 空中视觉语言导航，在 L1 辅助下三个划分的 Full SR 均提升至少 19.66%。

Conclusion: 该工作为评估额外架构复杂度何时有益于空中搜索建立了强基线，表明极简无训练设计可在该任务上取得显著性能。

Abstract: Open-world aerial object search methods often rely on persistent spatial maps, learned world models, or task-specific policy training. To examine how far a minimal, training-free design can go, we introduce SimpleUAV, an agent built around a frozen, off-the-shelf vision-language model (VLM). SimpleUAV separates current-view target assessment from history-conditioned action selection, combining fresh candidate evidence with lightweight search context. Local geometry converts visual selections into bounded straight-line movements, depth-based checks halt blocked segments, and measured execution feedback guides subsequent decisions. This closed loop sustains search without persistent spatial maps or task-specific training. SimpleUAV achieves 41.61% Success Rate (SR) on UAV-ON and 11.98% on AerialDojo-200K, compared with 23.80% and 3.20% for the strongest prior baselines, respectively. With limited benchmark-specific adaptation, the same core agent transfers to aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) on TravelUAV, improving Full SR by at least 19.66% across all three splits under L1 assistance. These results establish a strong baseline for evaluating when additional architectural complexity benefits aerial search.

</details>


### [142] [Dynamic Forcing: Learning Action-Grounded Sparse Dynamic Representations with Dense Future Guidance for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33799)
*Ziqiang Li,Shisheng Chen,Yunnan Wang,Wenyao Zhang,Zili Lin,Yuyang Zhang,Xudong Yang,Fangzhuo Jin,Chenyu Zhou,Wenjun Zeng,Xin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CompactWAM 提出稀疏动态表示学习框架，用少量动态 token 表示未来变化、以当前观测为锚点，并通过 Dynamic Forcing 训练，无需密集未来建模即可在多个机器人操作任务上保持高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型通过稠密未来预测支持机器人控制，但反复编码当前观测中已有的静态上下文，而真正与动作相关的动态变化在空间上往往稀疏，造成计算冗余。

Method: 构建稀疏-稠密联合预测框架 CompactWAM，在视频监督的共享骨干中学习稀疏表示且无需重建目标；提出 Dynamic Forcing，将直接动作监督与特权稠密未来引导结合，包括动作功能引导（匹配稀疏与稠密对动作专家的贡献）和动态感知分布匹配（对齐相对静态参考的时间关系变化）；推理时仅一次视觉前向编码当前观测与紧凑动态 token 生成动作。

Result: LIBERO 上成功率达 98.4%，LIBERO-Plus 上达 77.9%；仅用 9.2% 的稠密未来 token 预算时分别保持 98.3% 和 76.6%；RoboTwin 2.0 与真实机器人操作实验进一步验证有效性。

Conclusion: 稀疏动态表示能够在显著降低未来建模开销的同时保留动作相关信息，是实现高效世界动作模型的有效途径。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) support robot control through visual prediction, but dense future modeling repeatedly encodes static context already available in the current observation, while action-relevant changes are often spatially sparse. We study sparse dynamic representation learning, retaining the current observation as a contextual anchor and using a small set of compact dynamic tokens to represent anticipated changes. We develop CompactWAM, a sparse-dense joint predictive framework that enables sparse representation learning within a video-supervised shared backbone, without requiring a reconstruction objective for the sparse pathway. Since a limited token budget alone does not specify which information to retain, we introduce Dynamic Forcing, which pairs direct action supervision with privileged dense future guidance to promote action relevance and dynamic fidelity. While action prediction gradients directly drive compact tokens to capture control-relevant cues, dense future guidance further shapes them via action functional guidance that matches sparse and dense contributions to the action expert, and dynamics-aware distribution matching, which aligns temporal relational changes relative to a static reference. At inference, a single visual forward pass encodes current observations and compact dynamic tokens for action generation, eliminating dense future modeling. CompactWAM achieves 98.4\% success on LIBERO and 77.9\% on LIBERO-Plus, retaining respective success rates of 98.3\% and 76.6\% with only 9.2\% of the dense future token budget. Evaluations on RoboTwin~2.0 and real-world manipulation further demonstrate the effectiveness of the learned sparse predictive representations.

</details>


### [143] [DriftWAM: Faster World-Action Models via Action-Aware Video Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33797)
*Minyang Li,Siyu Xu,Yunke Wang,Ying-Cong Chen,Chang Xu,Daochang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriftWAM 提出一种少步世界-动作模型蒸馏框架，通过动作感知视频漂移与动作引导视频正则，将诱导动作信息纳入视频生成学习，在保持推理效率的同时提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型依赖迭代去噪导致推理成本高；蒸馏为少步生成器时，逐点轨迹一致性蒸馏只追求视觉保真度，未利用下游动作信息优化视频生成，且缺乏显式分布级对齐，激进步数减少下任务性能难以维持。

Method: DriftWAM 将预测视频诱导的动作引入视频学习：一是动作感知视频漂移，通过联合视觉-动作相似度核进行视频分布匹配，在诱导动作引导下驱动样本演化；二是动作引导视频正则，利用联合核识别的兼容教师-学生匹配，将动作级梯度直接反传至视频生成器。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 与 LIBERO 基准及真实机器人操作任务上，DriftWAM 在相同推理速度的少步设置下持续优于先前的一致性蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: 将诱导动作信息与分布级对齐结合到少步蒸馏中，可在不增加推理成本的前提下有效提升世界-动作模型蒸馏后的下游任务表现。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) unify future video prediction and action generation for robotic control, but their reliance on iterative denoising incurs substantial inference costs. Distilling these models into few-step generators offers a promising route to efficient deployment, yet maintaining task performance under aggressive step reduction remains challenging. Existing methods rely on point-wise trajectory consistency distillation, which pursues high visual fidelity while leaving downstream action information unexploited in optimizing video generation, and also lacks explicit distribution-level alignment. To address this, we propose DriftWAM, a few-step WAM distillation framework that incorporates the actions induced by predicted videos into video learning. Specifically, DriftWAM integrates action-aware video drifting, which performs video distribution matching through a joint visual--action similarity kernel to steer sample evolution under the guidance of these induced actions, with action-guided video regularization, which leverages the compatible teacher--student matches identified by the joint kernel to backpropagate action-level gradients directly into the video generator. Evaluations on the RoboTwin 2.0 and LIBERO benchmarks, as well as in real-world robotic manipulation tasks, show that DriftWAM consistently outperforms prior consistency distillation methods across few-step settings at the same inference speed.

</details>


### [144] [DuoWM:Integrating Dual 6-DoF Visual Flows into Action-Conditioned Drone World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33779)
*Zixuan Li,Kangyao Huang,Meng Xu,Wan Tian,Xiaoyu Tang,Jinge Sun,Yingliang Yao,He Chen,Lingzhong Meng,Yunzhi Xue,Jingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DuoWM，一种基于动作流和物体流双视觉条件的第一人称无人机视频世界模型，并结合变换感知预训练，显著提升视频质量、3D一致性和轨迹跟随性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件视频世界模型难以将相机指令与视觉动态对齐，且无法表示完整的6自由度场景变换。

Method: DuoWM采用两种互补视觉条件：动作流将未来相机位置和方向投影到目标帧；物体流前向扭曲初始观测并叠加局部6-DoF变换以约束场景运动。另引入变换感知预训练，在标准RGB视频训练前将物体外观与显式变换轴配对。

Result: 在Sekai-drone数据集上，DuoWM在视频质量、3D一致性和轨迹跟随方面均最佳。相比SOTA方法Captain Safari，真实域FVD和LPIPS分别降低17.2%和21.6%，游戏域AUC@30从0.5003提升至0.5719，AUC@15从0.3088提升至0.3747。

Conclusion: DuoWM通过双视觉条件和变换感知预训练有效对齐相机指令与视觉动态并建模完整6-DoF变换，在多项指标上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video world models generate future observations from an initial frame and a camera trajectory, but often fail to align camera commands with visual dynamics or represent full 6-DoF scene transformations. We present DuoWM, a first-person drone video world model with two complementary visual conditions. Action flow projects future camera positions and orientations into each target frame, while Object flow forward-warps the initial observation and overlays local 6-DoF transformations to ground the induced scene motion.
