We further introduce transform-aware pretraining, which pairs object appearance with explicit transformation axes before standard RGB video training. On Sekai-drone dataset, DuoWM achieves the best results across video quality, 3D consistency, and trajectory following. Compared with Captain Safari, the state-of-the-art method, our approach reduces real-domain FVD and LPIPS by 17.2\% and 21.6\%, respectively, while improving game-domain AUC@30 from 0.5003 to 0.5719 and AUC@15 from 0.3088 to 0.3747.

</details>


### [145] [Fine-Grained Ego-Vehicle Action Recognition from Driving Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33654)
*Zhipeng Bao,Wenjie Zhao,Qianwen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的细粒度自车动作识别基准EgoManeuver，整合七个公开驾驶数据集，并设计密集监督视频基线DenseEgo。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模驾驶视频缺乏明确的自车动作标签，且现有数据集在动作定义、标注粒度和监督信号上不统一，难以支撑关键驾驶操作的发现与自动驾驶模型的系统评估。

Method: 构建EgoManeuver统一基准，涵盖16种横向与6种纵向动作；采用多源标注系统，融合车辆信号、自车位姿、高清地图拓扑和文本描述等证据，统一细粒度动作分类体系并标准化时间动作边界，通过运动学一致性检查与人工复核验证标签；提出DenseEgo视频基线，结合空间感知表示与逐步密集动作监督。

Result: 基准包含86.6K个片段、190.8小时、覆盖26个国家，并提供长尾感知评估协议；DenseEgo在横向和纵向动作上分别取得82.7和83.5 mAP，较最强现有视频模型分别提升1.5和5.0个点；并展示了与语言模型智能体结合进行动作与场景检索的应用。

Conclusion: EgoManeuver为细粒度自车动作识别提供了统一、可扩展的基准，DenseEgo验证了密集监督的有效性，并为驾驶视频检索与自动驾驶模型评估奠定基础，代码、数据与模型权重将公开。

Abstract: Fine-grained ego-vehicle action recognition turns large-scale driving video into structured, searchable driving scenarios, providing a foundation for discovering critical maneuvers and systematically developing and evaluating autonomous driving models. However, large-scale driving videos often lack explicit ego-action labels, and existing datasets vary in action definitions, annotation granularity, and available supervision. We introduce EgoManeuver, a unified benchmark of 16 lateral and 6 longitudinal actions constructed from seven public driving datasets. Our multi-source labeling system leverages existing annotations and complementary evidence available in each source dataset, including vehicle signals, ego poses, HD-map topology, and captions, to assign labels under a unified fine-grained action taxonomy. We further standardize temporal action boundaries across datasets and validate the resulting labels through kinematic consistency checks and targeted human review. The resulting benchmark contains 86.6K segments spanning 190.8 h across 26 countries and provides a long-tail-aware evaluation protocol. We also introduce DenseEgo, a video-based baseline that combines spatially aware representations with dense per-step action supervision. On the EgoManeuver, DenseEgo achieves 82.7 lateral and 83.5 longitudinal mAP, outperforming the strongest existing video model by 1.5 and 5.0 points, respectively. Finally, we demonstrate one way to integrate DenseEgo with a language-model agent for retrieving driving videos that match user-specified actions and scenarios. All code, datasets, and model weights will be publicly released.

</details>


### [146] [A 3D Simulator for Passive SPAD Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33498)
*Elia Mergaert,Rong Zou,Roberto Pellerito,Simone Nascivera,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个3D仿真管线，生成带相机运动和语义标签的真实感光子探测数据，使SPAD感知模型可在合成数据上训练并零样本迁移到真实低光场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动SPAD相机适合低光感知，但真实标注数据稀缺且采集困难，因此需要可提供标注训练数据的仿真器。

Method: 构建耦合可控场景几何与运动的传感器观测模型，模拟光子散粒噪声、暗计数和空间串扰，生成光子探测及相机运动、语义标签；通过光子计数校准和三类下游任务的零样本仿真到真实迁移进行评估。

Result: 仅用合成SPAD数据训练的分类器在低于2 lux的真实低光记录上无需微调即达高准确率；纯仿真训练的相机旋转估计在0.5和5.0 lux下对真实20度和30度扫掠的零样本平均绝对旋转误差小于1度；合成稠密标签训练的语义分割模型可直接在真实低光记录上推理。

Conclusion: 该仿真器为真实SPAD感知提供了实用的标注训练数据来源，验证了合成数据零样本迁移到真实低光任务的可行性。

Abstract: Passive single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) cameras provide short-exposure observations for low-light perception, but collecting task annotations for these sensors remains difficult, and annotated datasets are scarce. We present a 3D simulation pipeline that generates realistic photon detections together with camera-motion and semantic labels, enabling perception models to be trained on synthetic SPAD observations and applied directly to real-world data. The pipeline couples controllable scene geometry and motion with a sensor-informed observation model that accounts for photon shot noise, dark counts, and spatial crosstalk. We evaluate the pipeline through calibrated photon-count comparisons and zero-shot sim-to-real transfer across three downstream perception tasks. We show that classifiers trained only on synthetic SPAD data achieve high accuracy on real low-light recordings at illumination levels less than 2 lux, without real-data fine-tuning. Similarly, we apply models trained entirely on simulated camera sweeps to real-world camera-rotation estimation and achieve mean absolute rotation errors of less than 1 degree on real 20-degree and 30-degree sweeps at 0.5 and 5.0 lux, when deployed zero-shot. We additionally train semantic segmentation models on synthetic data with automatically generated dense labels and perform direct inference on real-world low-light recordings; the results further demonstrate the usefulness of synthetic dense supervision for real low-light perception. Our simulator thus provides a practical source of annotated training data for real SPAD perception.

</details>


### [147] [SplatWAM: Splatting World Action Models for Real-Time 3D Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33467)
*Frank Tsu-Fang Lu,Karthik Sundar Senthilkumar,Christopher Thirgood,Oscar Mendez,Simon Hadfield*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatWAM 是一个直接在紧凑 3D 高斯原语上学习动作条件动力学的生成式世界模型，通过分词器编码无位姿视图、动力学模型预测未来高斯状态，实现快速且高保真的新视角渲染与策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在像素或潜空间预测未来观测，难以直接表示真实三维场景演化。

Method: 提出 SplatWAM-T 分词器将可变数量无位姿视图编码为固定预算的高斯令牌；SplatWAM-D 动力学模型用对数欧氏协方差坐标和可微渲染监督，单次前向预测未来高斯状态；并引入 SplatWAM-P 轻量流匹配策略头与动力学模型联合训练。

Result: 在 OGBench Scene 上渲染新视角比视频世界模型快 12 倍（约 80 FPS），PSNR 最高提升 2.0 dB；8 个任务套件中无模型高斯令牌策略比像素策略高 24.8 个百分点，SplatWAM-P 比 LPWM 高 12 个百分点并匹敌 HIQL；LIBERO 上约 1B 模型超越 WorldVLA 和 OpenVLA，无大规模机器人预训练时匹敌 OpenVLA-OFT。

Conclusion: 直接在显式 3D 高斯空间学习动作条件动力学可构建跨视图共享表示，显著提升渲染效率、预测质量与策略性能。

Abstract: World models typically predict future observations in pixel or latent space, limiting their ability to directly represent true 3D scene evolution. We present SplatWAM (Splatting World Action Model), a generative world model that learns action-conditioned dynamics directly over compact 3D Gaussian primitives. A tokenizer SplatWAM-T first encodes a variable number of unposed views into a fixed budget of Gaussian tokens. A dynamics model SplatWAM-D then predicts future Gaussian states in a single forward pass per action, using log-Euclidean covariance coordinates and differentiable rendering supervision to facilitate learning in an explicit 3D space. Each predicted state can be rasterized from arbitrary viewpoints, enforcing a shared 3D representation across views by construction. On OGBench Scene, SplatWAM renders novel views 12x faster than video world models (~80 FPS on an RTX 5090) with up to 2.0 dB higher PSNR. We further introduce SplatWAM-P, a lightweight flow-matching policy head jointly trained with the dynamics model. Across 8 task suites, model-free Gaussian-token policies outperform pixel-based counterparts by up to 24.8 percentage points. SplatWAM-P improves over Latent Particle World Model (LPWM) by 12 percentage points and matches HIQL without a learned value function. On LIBERO, our ~1B model outperforms WorldVLA (~7B) and the robot-pretrained OpenVLA (~7B), while matching OpenVLA-OFT when both are trained without large-scale robot pretraining. Code and checkpoints will be released.

</details>
